# Lao ki hono Malu‘i ‘o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo‘ui 2025

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 1 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 7 C T LAO KI HONO MALU'I 'O E FAKAMATALA TAAUTAHA KI HE MO'UI 2025 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 KO E LAO FEKAU'AKI MO E FAKAMATALA TAAUTAHA KI HE MO'UI MO E NGAAHI ME'A FELAVE'I MO IA KE LAVA 'O MALU'I 'A E TOTONU 'O HA TAHA KI HE 'ENE ME'A FAKAFO'ITUITUI MO TAUHI 'A E FAKAPULIPULI 'O E FAKAMATALA TAAUTAHA KI HE MO'UI PEA KUO PAU 'E PULE'I 'E HA NGAAHI LAO MO HA NGAAHI TU‘UTU‘UNI MAHINO ‘A E HU, TANAKI, NGAUE‘AKI MO E FAKAHA

‘O E FAKAMATALA TAAUTAHA

‘Oku ou loto ki ai, TUPOU VI,

‘Aho 16 ‘o Tisema 2025.

‘OKU TU’UTU’UNI ‘e he Tu’i mo e Fale Alea ‘o Tonga ‘i he Fakataha Alea ‘o e Pule’anga ‘o pehe:

## KONGA 1 - TALATEU

#### 1 Hingoa nounou

‘E ui ‘a e Lao ni ko e Lao ki hono Malu‘i ‘o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo‘ui 2025.

Kupu 2 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 8 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

#### 2 Ngaahi Taumuʻa

Ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e Lao ni ke –

(a) fokotuʻu ha ngaahi lao ki he hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki mo e fakaha ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo fekauʻaki mo e kakai ʻa ʻoku ne maluʻi ʻa e fakapulipuli ʻo e fakamatala ko ia mo e totonu ʻa ha taha ki heʻene meʻa fakafoʻituiui ʻo fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala ko ia, kae ʻi he taimi tatau ʻoku tokoniʻi ʻa e fakahoko lelei ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui;

(b) ʻoatu ki ha taha ʻa e totonu ke aʻu ki he fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo fekauʻaki pe mo ia, ʻo fakatatau ki he ngaahi fakaʻatā ʻoku fakangatangata mo fakamahino pau ʻa ia kuo fokotuʻu atu ʻi he Lao ni;

(c) ʻoatu ki ha taha ʻa e totonu ke tuʻutuʻuni hono fakatonutonu (correction) pe fakatonutonu (amendment) ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo fekauʻaki mo kinautolu pe, ʻo fakatatau ki he ngaahi fakaʻatā ʻoku fakangatangata mo fakamahino pau ʻa ia kuo fakaha ʻi he Lao ni;

(d) ʻoatuʻaefoungangauekihatangi ʻofekauʻakimoefakamatalataautaha ki he moʻui; pea

(e) ʻoatuhangaahifakaleleikihengaahimaumaulaofekauʻakimoe Lao ni. 3 ʻUhingaʻi lea

(1) ʻI he Lao ni –

“fakafofonga” ʻi heʻene felaveʻi mo ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui, ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ha taha ʻa ia, ʻi hono fakamafai ʻe he tauhi fakamatala, ʻoku ne fakahoko fatongia maʻae pe fakafofongaʻi ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ʻo fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻa e tauhi fakamatala, kae ʻikai ko e ngaahi taumuʻa pe ʻa e fakafofonga;

“malava” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki he malava fakaeʻatamai, pea ‘e ‘uhinga tatau mo ia ‘a e “ivi malava”;

“ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue” ‘oku ‘uhinga ki he ‘Ofisa Pule Ngaue ‘oku ne tokangaekina ‘a e moʻui;

“tanaki” ʻi heʻene felaveʻi mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, ʻoku ʻuhinga ki hono tanaki pe maʻu mai ʻa e fakamatala mei ha maʻuʻanga fakamatala ʻi ha founga pe, pea ʻoku ʻuhinga tatau mo ia ʻa e

“tanaki” (collection);

“fakapuliki” ʻi heʻene felaveʻi mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo ha taha, ʻoku ʻuhinga ke toʻo ha fakamatala, ʻo fakatatau mo e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ko ia ʻe ngofua ke tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, ʻa ia ʻoku ne fakahaaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia pe ʻa ia ʻoku ala fakafuofua ʻi he ngaahi makatuʻunga ʻe lava ʻo ngaueʻaki, ʻiate ia pe pe fakataha mo ha Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 3 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 9 fakamatala kehe, ke fakahaaʻi ʻa e taha, pea ʻoku ʻuhinga tatau mo ia ʻa e

“fakapuliki” (de-identification);

“fakaha” ʻi heʻene felaveʻi mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ‘a ia

‘oku tauhi pe ‘oku puleʻi ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui, tukukehe ʻi hono fakaʻuhingaʻi ʻi he Konga 6, ʻoku ʻuhinga ke tukuange ʻa e fakamatala pe fakaʻatā ke ngaueʻaki ʻe ha taha, pea ʻoku ʻuhinga tatau mo ia ʻa e “fakaha” (disclosure);

“fakamatala ʻo e sivi faʻunga tupuʻanga” ʻoku ʻuhinga –

(a) ki he fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ʻa ia kuo maʻu mei ha sivi ʻo e ngaahi kongokonga faʻunga tupuʻanga pefaʻungaʻaianaʻefakahokoʻiheʻikaiʻiaihangaahifakaʻilonga fokoutua ʻi he taha ko ia ʻo felaveʻi mo e fokoutua naʻe sivi pea ʻoku ne fakaha ʻa e faingamalie pe tuʻu lavea ngofua ki he fokoutua, mahaki, maumau pe ha ngaahi maumau, ʻo tatau ʻi he sino pe ʻatamai; mo

(b) fakamatala taautaha kehe ki he moʻui ‘a ia ‘oku tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, ka ʻoku ʻikai kau ki ai ha fakamatala kuo maʻu mei ha ngaahi fua angamaheni ʻo e sino, ngaahi sivi ʻo e sino, ko hono toʻo ʻo e hisitolia fakafamili ʻo e taha ko ia pe ha fakamatala taautaha kehe ki he moʻui ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni;

“tauhi” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ha taha ʻa ia ko ha tauhi ʻi he lao pe ko ha taha kuo fokotuʻu ʻe he Fakamaauʻanga ko ha tauhi ʻi he kupu 8 ʻo e Lao ki hono Tauhia ʻo e Fanau [Vahe 6.04];

“ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki hano siofi, sivi, vakaiʻi, tokangaekina, ngaue pe founga ngaue ʻa ia ʻoku fakahoko koeʻuhi ko ha taumuʻa ʻoku felaveʻi mo e moʻui pea ʻoku –

(a) fakahoko koeʻuhi ke ʻiloʻi, faitoʻo mo tauhi ʻa e tuʻunga fakaesino mo fakaʻatāmai ʻo ha taha;

(b) fakahoko koeʻuhi ke fakaʻehiʻehi mei he mahaki pe ha lavea pe fakaʻaiʻai ʻa e moʻui lelei;

(c) fakahoko ko e konga ia ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e fakanonga maʻae fokoutua, pea kau ki ai ʻa hono fakatahaʻi, tufaki pe fakatau atu ʻo ha faitoʻo, ko ha meʻangaue fakafaitoʻo, naunau pe ha meʻa kehe ki ha taha, pe ki hono ngaueʻaki ʻe ha taha;

“taha ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ha –

(a) taha ʻa ia ʻoku lesisita ʻi he Lao ki he Kau Toketa, Kau Ngaue ki he Nifo mo e Kau Ngaue ki he Moʻui 2021, ko e Lao ki he Kau Neesi, Kau Neesi Fakafaito‘o mo e Kau Ma‘uli 2021, pe ko e Lao ki he Huʻi Vai [Vahe 12.14], pea ʻoku ne fakahoko ʻa e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui;

(b) kau ngaue tokoni taautaha; pe Kupu 3 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 10 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(c) tahaʻokunefakahokoʻaengauetokangaekinaʻoemoʻuipeaʻoku nefakahokohangaueʻaiakuotuʻutuʻuni‘eheNgaahiTuʻutuʻuni,

‘o ‘ikai kau ki ai ha taha ‘oku faitoʻo faka-Tonga;

“tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui” fakatatau ki he kupu siʻi (4) mo e (5), ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ha taha ʻa ia ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he taha ʻo e ngaahi palakalafi ko ʻeni ʻa ia ʻoku ne tauhi pe puleʻi ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui koeʻuhi ko e, pe ‘i he fekauʻaki mo hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaahi mafai pe fatongia ʻo e taha ko ia pe ko e ngaue ʻa ia ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he palakalafi, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻo ʻikai ha toe meʻa kehe –

(a) ko ha taha ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui;

(b) ko ha taha ʻoku ne fakalele ha falemahaki;

(c) ko ha fakafofonga ki hono tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui, fale fakalelei ʻatamai pe ko ha toketa fakalelei ʻatamai fakatatau ki he Lao ki he Fakalelei ʻAtamai [Vahe 12.10];

(d) ko ha taha ʻoku ne fakalele ha fale huʻi vai fakatatau ki he Lao ki he Huʻi Vai [Vahe 12.14];

(e) ko ha taha ʻoku ne fakalele ha ngaue meʻalele tokoni ʻi ha fakatuʻutamaki;

(f) ko ha ʻofisa moʻui fakatatau ki he Lao ki he Moʻui ʻa e Kakai [Vahe 12.15], pe ko ha ʻofisa fakamafai kuo fokotuʻu ʻi he kupu 6 ʻo e Lao ki he Moʻui ‘a e Kakai [Vahe 12.15];

(g) ko e Minisita, fakataha mo e Potungaue, kapau ʻe tuʻutuʻuni ia ʻe he ngaahi tukunga;

(h) ko ha taha, pe ko ha faʻahinga ʻo ha niʻihi, kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he NgaahiTuʻutuʻuni,ʻaiaʻokunaufakatahatahamotauhihalesisita pe tauhiʻanga ʻo e –

(i) fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa tefito ke ʻanalaiso ʻa e fakamatala pe fakahoko ha fakatotolo; pe

(ii) fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku felaveʻi mo ha mahaki pe fokoutua pau pe ʻoku felaveʻi ki hono tauhi pe foaki ʻo e ngaahi konga ʻo e sino pe ngaahi meʻa ʻo e sino;

(i) ko ha pule ngaue;

(j) ko ha taha maluʻi pe fakafofonga maluʻi; pe

(k) ko ha taha kehe ʻa ia, pe ko ha faʻahinga ʻo ha niʻihi ʻa ia, kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni ko e kau tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui kapau ʻoku nau tauhi pe puleʻi ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui koeʻuhi ko e pe ʻi he fekauʻaki mo e ngaahi mafai pe fatongia fakahoko ngaue pe ko e ngaue ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni;

“fika falemahaki” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki he fika ʻa ia kuo foaki ki ha taha ʻi Tonga ʻi he Lao ki he Ngaahi Ngaue ki he Moʻui [Vahe 12.06];

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 3 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 11

“fakamatala fakahaaʻi” ʻoku ʻuhinga, ʻi heʻene fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, ko ha fakamatala ʻoku ne fakahaaʻi ha taha pe ʻa ia ʻoku ala fakafuofua ʻi he ngaahi makatuʻunga ʻe lava ʻo ngaueʻaki,ʻiate ia pe pe fakataha mo ha fakamatala kehe, ke fakahaaʻi ha taha;

“ʻikai malava” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki he ʻikai malava fakaeʻatamai, pea ʻoku ʻuhinga tatau mo ia ʻa e “ʻikai malava” (incapacity);

“taha” ʻi heʻene felaveʻi mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, ʻoku ʻuhinga ki he taha, ʻo tatau pe ʻoku moʻui pe kuo pekia, ʻo fekauʻaki mo e taha naʻe hū, tanaki, faʻu pe ngaueʻaki ki ai ʻa e fakamatala;

“pule fakamatala” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ha fakafofonga ʻa ia, ʻi heʻene fakafofongaʻi ʻa e Potungaue ʻoku ne –

(a) ngaueʻaki, tauhi pe fakaʻauha ʻa e ngaahi lekooti ʻa e Potungaue ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui; pe

(b) fakahoko ʻa e ngaahi ngaue puleʻi pe fakatekinolosia ʻo e fakamatala ki he Potungaue ʻo fekauʻaki mo e ngaahi lekooti ʻa e Potungaue ʻa ia ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui;

“ngaahi founga ngaue fakafakamatala” ʻi heʻene felaveʻi mo ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui, ʻoku ʻuhinga ki he ngaahi ngaue ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ʻi heʻene felaveʻi mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui pea kau ki ai ʻa e –

(a) taimi mo e founga ʻoku hū, tanaki, faʻu, ngaueʻaki, liliu, fakaha, tauhi mo fakaʻauha ai ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e fakamatala; mo e

(b) ngaahi maluʻi fakaengaue, fakatekinikale mo fakafisikale mo e ngaahi founga ngaue ʻoku tauhi ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻi heʻene fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala;

“Minisita” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki he Minisita Moʻui;

“Potungaue” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki he Potungaue Moʻui;

“fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki he fakamatala fakaʻilongaʻi fekauʻaki mo ha taha, ʻi he fotunga ʻo e lea pe kuo hiki, kapau ko e fakamatala ʻoku –

(a) felaveʻi mo e moʻui lelei fakaesino pe fakaʻatāmai pe moʻui lelei fakalukufua ʻa e taha ko ia;

(b) felaveʻi mo hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ki he taha ko ia, kau ki ai hono fakaʻilonga ʻo ha taha ʻoku ne fakahoko ʻa e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ki he taha ko ia;

(c) felaveʻi mo e lava ke kau ʻi he ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ʻo fekauʻaki mo e taha ko ia;

(d) felaveʻi mo hono foaki ʻe he taha ha konga ʻo e sino pe ngaahi meʻa ʻo e sino ʻo e taha ko ia ʻi he Lao ki he Fokotuʻu Foʻimata Kupu 3 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 12 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to mo e Kanomate [Vahe 12.02], pe ʻoku maʻu mei hono ʻahiʻahi pe sivi ʻo ha konga pehe ʻo e sino pe ngaahi meʻa ʻo e sino;

(e) fakahaaʻi ʻaki ʻa e taha ʻoku ne fakahoko ʻa e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ki he taha ko ia pe ko e fetongi ʻo e taha faituʻutuʻuni maʻae taha ʻi he Konga 3;

(f) felaveʻi mo e fika falemahaki ʻa e taha ko ia; pe

(g) felaveʻi mo e fakamatala kuo tanaki ʻo fekauʻaki mo hono faʻeleʻi pe pekia ʻa ha taha ʻi he Lao ki he Lesisita ʻo e Faʻele, Pekia mo e Mali [Vahe 6.01];

“fakatonutonu fakalao” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ha fakatonutonu fakalao ʻoku fakahoko,kimuʻape‘ihemalumalu‘oengaahilaoʻohafakamaauʻanga, fakatonutonuʻanga, komisoni, fakafofonga ‘o e fakamaau totonu, ko ha Poate kuo fokotuʻu ʻi he Lao ki he Kau Toketa, Kau Ngaue ki he Nifo mo e Kau Ngaue ki he Mo‘ui 2021, ko e Lao ki he Kau Neesi, Kau Neesi Fakafaitoʻo mo e Kau Maʻuli Fakapolofesinale 2021, pe ko e Lao ki he Huʻi Vai [Vahe 12.14], pe ko ha taha fakatonutonu pe tuʻu vahaʻa ʻi ha fakatonutonu fakalao;

“fakamatala ki he tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina” ʻoku ne maʻu ʻa e ʻuhinga ʻoku ha ʻi he Konga 6;

“lekooti” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala ʻi ha fotunga pe pe ʻi ha founga pea ʻoku kau ki ai ha fakamatala ʻa ia kuo tohi, faitaaʻi, hiki tatau, lekooti, liliu tisitolo ʻa e fakamatala pe tauhi ʻi ha founga;

“kainga” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ha taha ʻo ha ongo meʻa ʻe toko ua ʻa ia ʻoku na fekaingaʻaki, ʻo kau ki ai ʻa e mali pe ohi;

“fakatotolo” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki he fakatotolo hokohoko lelei kuo faʻu ke ne fakalahi mo fokotuʻu ʻa e ngaahi moʻoniʻi meʻa, tuʻutuʻuni pe ha ʻilo ʻoku lava ʻo fakalukufua, pe ko ha fakatahaʻi pe ʻo ia, pea kau ki ai ʻa e fakalakalaka ʻo e fakatotolo, ʻahiʻahi mo e sivi;

“taha fakatotolo” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ha taha ʻoku ne fakahoko ha fakatotolo;

“poate puleʻi ʻo e fakatotolo” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki he Komiti Fakafonua ki he Moʻui mo e ʻEfika ʻa e Potungaue;

“hoa mali” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ha taha ‘i ha toko ua ‘a ia ‘oku na mali ‘o fakatatau ki he Lao ki he Lesisita ‘o e Faʻele, Pekia mo e Mali;

“fetongi ʻo e taha faituʻutuʻuni” ʻi heʻene felaveʻi mo ha taha, ʻoku ʻuhinga, tukukehe ka fiemaʻu ha ʻuhinga kehe ʻi hono tuʻunga, ki ha taha ʻa ia kuo fakamafai ʻi he Lao ni ke ne fakangofua ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia ki he hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ‘o e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo e taha ko ia, pe taha kuo fokotuʻu ʻe he Fakamaauʻanga ʻi he kupu 30 ‘o e Lao ni.

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 4 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 13

(2) Ko e lave ki ha lekooti ‘oku kau ki ai ha konga ‘o ha lekooti tukukehe ka fiemaʻu ha ʻuhinga kehe ʻi hono tuʻunga.

(3) ʻOku kau ki he fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa e ngaahi fakamatala fakahaaʻi ʻa ia ʻoku ʻikai ko e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) ka ko ia ʻoku ʻi ha lekooti ʻa ia ʻoku ʻi ai ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi ko ia.

(4) Ko ha taha ʻa ia kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he palakalafi (a) ʻo e ʻuhingaʻi lea ʻo e

“tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui” ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) ʻoku ʻikai ko ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui kapau ʻoku fakangaueʻi ia pe ʻoku ne fakahoko fatongia maʻae pe fakafofongaʻi ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui.

(5) Ko ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku ne maʻu ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui koeʻuhi ko ʻene fakahoko fatongia ko e fakafofonga maʻae tauhi fakamatala ʻe taha ki he moʻui ʻoku ʻikai ko ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻo fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala ko ia.

(6) Ko ha lave ʻi he Lao ni ki ha taha ʻoku kau ki ai, kapau ʻoku ʻikai malava ʻe he taha ko ia, ʻa e fetongi ʻo e taha faituʻutuʻuni maʻae taha ko ia. 4 ʻE lau ʻoku ne fakafofongaʻi KoeʻuhikoengaahitaumuʻaʻoeLaoni,kuopauʻelauhatahaʻaiakohafakafofonga ʻo ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui, ʻi heʻene fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku ne fakafofongaʻi ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ʻi heʻene ngaueʻaki ʻa e ngaahi mafai pe fakahoko ʻa e ngaahi ngafa maʻae pe fakafofongaʻi ʻa e tauhi fakamatala, kae ʻikai ko e fakafofongaʻi pe ia.

#### 5 ‘Uhingaʻi lea, tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui

(1) Fakatatau ki he kupu siʻi (2), kuo pau ʻe lau ko ha tauhi fakamatala mavahe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku ne puleʻi ha feituʻu pau, polokalama pe ngaue ʻoku lahi ange ʻi he taha ʻa ia ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he taha ʻo e ngaahi palakalafi ‘o e ʻuhingaʻi lea “tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui” ʻi he kupu 3(1) ʻi heʻene fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku ne tauhi pe puleʻi koeʻuhi ko e pe ʻi he felaveʻi mo hono puleʻi ʻa e ngaahi feituʻu pau, polokalama pe ngaue takitaha ʻa ia ʻoku ne puleʻi.

(2) Kapau ʻe loto ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku ne puleʻi ha feituʻu pau, polokalama pe ngaue ʻoku lahi ange ʻi he taha ke ne vahevahe ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he vahaʻa ʻo e ngaahi feituʻu pau, polokalama pe ngaue ko ia ke lava ʻo ʻoatu ha fakatahatahaʻi ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui pe ngaahi ngaue ʻi he ngaahi feituʻu pau, polokalama, pe ngaue ko ia, ʻe ngofua ke kole ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he Minisita ʻi ha tohi ke ne hoko ko e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui pe ʻe taha ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e ngaahi feituʻu pau, polokalama pe ngaue ko ia.

Kupu 6 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 14 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(3) Kapau kuo maʻu ʻe he Minisita ha kole ʻi he kupu ni mei ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke fakahoko fatongia ko e tauhi fakamatala pe ʻe taha, kuo pau ke tali ʻe he Minisita ʻa e kole kapau ʻoku pehe ʻe he Minisita ʻoku totonu ia ʻo fakatatau ki he ngaahi tukunga, ʻi heʻene fakakaukauʻi ʻa e lelei fakalukufua mo e –

(a) malava ʻa e taha kole ke ʻoatu ki he kakai ʻa e aʻu feʻunga ki heʻenau fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui;

(b) ivi malava ‘o e taha kole ke faipau ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻutuʻuni ki hono tanaki, ngaueʻaki mo hono fakaha ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he Konga 4; pea

(c) kapau ʻoku ʻaonga hono vahevahe ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ke lava ʻo fakahoko lelei hano fakatahatahaʻi ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui.

(4) ‘E ngofua ki he Minisita ke ne ‘oatu ha faingamalie ki ha taha ke ne fanongoa ia kimuʻa ʻi ho no tali pe ʻikai tali ha kole ʻi he kupu siʻi (3).

(5) Ko ha tuʻutuʻuni kuo fakahoko ‘e he Minisita ‘i he kupu siʻi (4), kuo pau ke aofangatuku.

(6) Fakatatau ki he kupu siʻi (7), ʻoku ʻikai ngata ʻa e hoko ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ko e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻi heʻene fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui kae ʻoua kuo ‘ave hono tauhi pe puleʻi ʻo e fakamatala ki ha taha ʻe taha ʻa ia kuo fakamafaiʻi fakalao ke ne tauhi ʻa e ngaahi lekooti.

(7) Kapau ‘e pekia ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui, kuo pau ‘e lau ‘a e talasiti ‘o e koloa ‘a e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ko e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ‘o fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku tauhi ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui kae ‘oua kuo ‘ave hono tauhi pe puleʻi ʻo e fakamatala ki ha taha kehe ʻa ia kuo fakamafaiʻi fakalao ke ne tauhi ʻa e ngaahi lekooti. 6 ʻUhingaʻi lea, tanaki, ngaueʻaki mo e fakaha

(1) Koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa of Lao ni, ko hono ʻoatu ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he vahaʻa ʻo ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui mo ha fakafofonga ʻo e tauhi fakamatala ko ha ngaueʻaki ia ʻo e fakamatala ʻe he tauhi fakamatala, kae ʻikai ko ha fakaha ʻe he taha ʻoku ne ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala pe ko ha tanaki ʻe he taha ʻoku ʻoatu ki ai ʻa e fakamatala.

(2) Ka ʻi ai ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻo e Lao ni ʻe ngaueʻaki ki hono tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻi hono fakangofua ʻe he taha ko ia, ʻo tatau pe ko e ha ʻa e natula ʻo e fakangofua, ʻoku ʻikai ke ne uesia ʻa e tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻoku fakangofua ʻe he Lao ni pe ʻe tuʻutuʻuni ai ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke ne fakaʻuhingaʻi ʻa e fakamatala ʻo ʻikai ha fakangofua ʻa e taha.

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 6 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 15

(3) Ka fakangofua ʻe ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻo e Lao ni ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke ne fakaha ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ʻo ʻikai ha fakangofua ʻa e taha ko ia –

(a) ʻe ʻikai fiemaʻu ke fakaha ia ʻe he tauhi fakamatala kae ʻoua kuo tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he lao ke ne fakahoko ia;

(b) ʻoku ʻikai ke ne fakaʻatā ʻa e tauhi fakamatala mei ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e lao ke ne fakaha ʻa e fakamatala; pea

(c) ʻe ʻikai taʻofi ai ʻa e tauhi fakamatala mei heʻene maʻu mai ʻa e fakangofua ʻa e taha ko ia ki he fakaha.

#### 7 Ngaueʻaki ʻo e Lao

(1) Tukukehe ka tuʻutuʻuni fakamahino kehe ʻe he Lao ni pe ko hono Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, ʻe ngaueʻaki ʻa e Lao ni ki he fakamatala ki he tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina mo e –

(a) tanaki ‘o e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ‘e ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui;

(b) ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, ʻe ha –

(i) tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui, pe

(ii) taha ‘a ia ‘oku ‘ikai ko ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui pea ‘a ia

‘e fakaha ki ai ‘e he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ‘a e fakamatala; pea

(c) hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo ha fika falemahaki ʻe ha taha.

(2) ‘I he fehangahangai ‘a ha tu’utu’uni ‘o e Lao ni pe ko hono Ngaahi Tu‘utu‘uni mo ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻo ha Lao kehe pe ko hono Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, kuo pau ke malohi ʻa e Lao ni mo hono Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni tukukehe, kapau ʻe tuʻutuʻuni kehe ʻe he Lao ni, ko hono Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni pe ha Lao kehe.

(3) Koeʻuhi koe ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o e kupu ni, ‘e ‘ikai ke ‘i ai ha fehangahangai tukukehe kapau ‘oku ‘ikai lava ke faipau ki he Lao ni mo hono Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni mo ha ngaahi lao kehe pe ko hono Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(4) Ke maʻu ha fakapapau ʻoku lahi ange, ʻoku haʻi ʻe he Lao ni ʻa e Kalauni, kau ki ai ʻa e kotoa ʻo e ngaahi potungaue, kautaha mo e kau ngaue ʻo e Kalauni. 8 ʻIkai ngaueʻaki ʻo e Lao

(1) ʻOkuʻikaingaueʻakiʻaeLaonikihafakamatalataautahakihemoʻuifekauʻaki mo ha taha hili ʻa e ngaahi taʻu kimuʻa ʻe 120 mei hono faʻu ha lekooti ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e fakamatala, mo e taʻu ʻe 50 hili ʻa e pekia ʻa e taha ko ia.

(2) Kuo pau ‘e ʻikai ha meʻa ʻi he Lao ni ʻe fakaʻuhingaʻi ke kaunoa – Kupu 9 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 16 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(a) ʻi ha meʻa ʻoku fekauʻaki mo ha ‘eke totongi maluʻi pe ha ‘amanaki ‘eke totongi maluʻi;

(b) ʻi ha totonu fakalao, kau ki ai ha totonu ʻa e fakafofonga lao mo e taha

‘oku ne fakafofongaʻi;

(c) ʻi he lao ki he fakamoʻoni pe fakamatala ʻoku ʻata ʻi he lao ki ha faʻahi pe ha taha fakamoʻoni ʻi ha fakatonutonu fakalao;

(d) ʻi he mafai ʻo ha fakamaauʻanga pe ha fakatonutonuʻanga ke tuʻutuʻuniʻi ha taha fakamoʻoni ke fakamoʻoni pe tuʻutuʻuniʻi ke ne ʻoatu ha tohi ngaue;

(e) ʻi ha fakatonutonu fakalao pe ha tangi ʻi heʻene felaveʻi mo ha launga fekauʻaki mo ha taha ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ʻi he Lao ki he Kau Toketa, Kau Ngaue ki he Nifo mo e Kau Ngaue ki he Moʻui 2021, ko e Lao ki he Kau Neesi, Kau Neesi Fakafaitoʻo mo e Kau Maʻuli Fakapolofesinale 2021 pe ko e Lao ki he Huʻivai [Vahe 12.14]; pe

(f) ʻi ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻo ha Lao kehe pe ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e fakamaauʻanga, kapau ʻoku tapui ʻe he tuʻutuʻuni (provision) pe tuʻutuʻuni (order), ‘a hono fakaha ʻe ha taha ʻa e fakamatala ki he kakai pe mei hono pulusi ʻo e fakamatala.

## KONGA 2 – NGAAHI FOUNGA NGAUE KE MALUʻI ʻAKI ʻA E

FAKAMATALA TAAUTAHA KI HE MOʻUI VAHE 1 – FAKALUKUFUA

#### 9 Founga ngaue fakaefakamatala

(1) Kuo pau ke ʻi ai ha ngaahi founga ngaue fakafakamatala mo ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ngaue ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻoku ne tauhi pe puleʻi ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku faipau ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(2) Kuo pau ke faipau ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ki he ʻene ngaahi founga ngaue fakafakamatala mo e tuʻutuʻuni ngaue.

(3) Kuo pau ke faipau ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku ne ngaueʻaki ha ngaahi founga fakaʻilekitulonika ke ne tanaki, ngaueʻaki, liliu, fakaha, tauhi pe fakaʻauha ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘e ngofua ke tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(4) Kuo pau ke faipau ha taha ʻoku ne ʻoatu ʻa e ngaahi koloa pe ngaahi ngaue koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke malava ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻo ngaueʻaki ʻa e ngaahi founga fakaʻilekitulonika ke tanaki, ngaueʻaki, liliu, fakaha, tauhi Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 10 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 17 pe fakaʻauha ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘e ngofua ke tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

#### 10 Lekooti ʻatita fakaʻilekitulonika

(1) Kuo pau ko ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku ne ngaueʻaki ʻa e ngaahi founga fakaʻilekitulonika ke tanaki, ngaueʻaki, fakaha, liliu, tauhi pe fakaʻauha ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ke ne –

(a) tauhi, pe tuʻutuʻuni hono tauhi ʻo ha, lekooti ʻatita fakaʻilekitulonika ʻa ia ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi (2); pea

(b) ʻatita mo vakaiʻi ʻa e lekooti ʻatita fakaʻilekitulonika ʻi he taimi kotoa pe ʻe tuʻutuʻuni ai ʻe he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(2) ʻIhetaimikotoapeʻokuvakai,tokangaʻi,liliupengaueʻakihalekootipekonga ʻo ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku lava ʻo aʻu ki ai ʻaki ʻa e ngaahi founga fakaʻilekitulonika, kuo pau ke kau ʻi he lekooti ʻatita fakaʻilekitulonika ʻa e –

(a) faʻahinga ʻo e fakamatala naʻe vakai ki ai, tokangaʻi, liliu pe naʻe ngaue ki ai;

(b) ʻaho mo e taimi ʻa ia naʻe vakai, tokangaʻi, liliu pe ngaue ai ki he fakamatala;

(c) fakahaaʻi ʻo e kotoa ʻo e niʻihi naʻa nau vakai, tokangaʻi, liliu pe ngaue ki he fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui;

(d) fakahaaʻi ʻo e taha ʻoku felaveʻi mo ia ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui.

#### 11 Tonu ʻa e fakamatala

(1) Kuo pau ke fakahoko ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻoku ne ngaueʻaki ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ʻa e ngaahi ngaue fakapotopoto ke ne fakapapauʻi ko e fakamatala ʻoku tonu, kakato pea ko e fakamuimui taha ia ʻoku fiemaʻu koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻa ia ʻoku ngaueʻaki ki ai ʻa e fakamatala.

(2) Kuo pau ko ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku ne fakaha ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ke ne –

(a) fakahoko ʻa e ngaahi founga fakapotopoto ke fakapapauʻi ko e fakamatala ʻoku tonu, kakato pea ko e fakamuimui taha ia ʻoku fiemaʻu koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e fakaha ʻa ia ʻoku ʻilo ki ai ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ʻi he taimi ʻo e fakaha; pe

(b) fakaha mahino ki he taha ʻoku ne maʻu ʻa e fakaha ʻa e ngaahi fakangatangata,kapauʻokuʻiai,kihenatulatonu,kakatopefakamuimui taha ʻo e fakamatala.

Kupu 12 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 18 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

#### 12 Ngaahi ngaue ke fakapapauʻi ʻa e tanaki

Kuo pau ke fakahoko ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e ngaahi ngaue ʻa ia ʻoku fakapotopoto ʻo fakatatau ki he ngaahi tukunga ke fakapapauʻi ʻoku ʻikai tanaki ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo ʻikai ha fakamafai.

#### 13 Fakangatangata ki hono ngaueʻaki ʻo e fakamatala kuo fakapuliki

(1) Fakatatau ki he kupu siʻi (2) mo ha ngaahi fakaʻatā kehe ʻa ia ʻe ngofua ke tuʻutuʻuni ‘a e Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, kuo pau ʻe ʻikai ngaueʻaki ʻe ha taha pe feingakengaueʻaki hafakamatalaʻaiakuofakapulikikefakahaaʻiʻaki hataha, ʻo tatau pe ko e fakamatala ʻiate ia pe pe fakataha mo ha fakamatala kehe, tukukehe ka fakangofua ʻe he Lao ni pe ha Lao kehe ke ngaueʻaki ʻa e fakamatala ke fakahaaʻi ʻaki ʻa e taha ko ia.

(2) ʻOku ʻikai taʻofi ʻe he fakangatangata ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) ha taha ʻo kinautolu ni mei hono ngaueʻaki ʻo ha fakamatala ʻa ia kuo nau fakapuliki, ʻo tatau pe ko e fakamatala ko ia ʻiate ia pe pe fakataha mo ha fakamatala kehe, ke fakahaaʻi ʻaki ha taha –

(a) ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui;

(b) ha sino kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(c) ha taha kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni ke ne faipau pe tauhi ha lesisita ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui; pe

(d) ha taha kehe ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

#### 14 Malu

(1) Kuo pau ke fakahoko ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e ngaahi founga ʻa ia ʻoku fakapotopoto ʻo fakatatau ki he ngaahi tukunga ke fakapapauʻi ʻoku maluʻi ʻa e kotoa ʻo efakamatala taautaha ki hemoʻuiʻaia ʻokutauhimo puleʻi ʻehetauhifakamatalamei honokaihaʻasi,molepekoehū,ngaueʻakipefakaha ʻo ʻikai ha fakamafai mo fakapapauʻi ʻoku maluʻi ʻa e ngaahi lekooti ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e fakamatala mei hono hiki tatau, liliu pe fakaʻauha ʻo ʻikai ha fakamafai.

(2) Fakatatau ki he kupu siʻi (4) mo e ngaahi fakaʻatā mo e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni fakalahi, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, kapau kuo kaihaʻasi pe mole ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ʻa ia ʻoku ʻi he tauhi mo e pule ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui pe kapau ʻoku ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo ʻikai ha fakamafai, kuo pau ko e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui te ne –

(a) fakahakihetahakoiaʻihefuofuafaingamaliefakapotopotoʻofekauʻaki mo e kaihaʻa pe mole pe ko e ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo ʻikai ha fakamafai; pea

(b) fakakau ʻi he fanongonongo ʻa e fakamatala ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e totonu ʻa e taha ke fakahoko haʻane launga ki he ‘Ofisa Pule Ngaue ʻi he Konga 8.

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 15 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 19

(3) Kapau ʻoku fakakakato ʻe he ngaahi tukunga ʻo ha kaihaʻa, mole pe ko e hū, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo ʻikai ha fakamafai ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ai ʻi he kupu siʻi (2) ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, kuo pau ke fakaha ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ki he ‘Ofisa Pule Ngaue ʻo fekauʻaki mo e kaihaʻa pe mole pe ko e hū, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo ʻikai ha fakamafai.

(4) Kapau ko etauhifakamatala ki hemoʻuiko hatahafakatotoloʻaiakuonemaʻu ʻaefakamatalataautahakihemoʻuimei hatauhifakamatalaʻetahakihemoʻui ʻi he kupu 48(1), ‘e ‘ikai fiemaʻu ke fakaha ia ʻe he taha fakatotolo ki he taha ko ia, kapau kuo kaihaʻasi pe mole pe kapau kuo ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻa e fakamatala ʻo ʻikai ha fakamafai, tukukehe kapau ko e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui naʻa ne fakaha ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu 48(1) ‘o e Lao ni –

(a) kuo ne tomuʻa maʻu ʻa e fakangofua ʻa e taha ko ia ke fakahoko ʻe he taha fakatotolo ʻa e fetuʻutaki ki he taha ko ia; pea

(b) fakaha ki he taha fakatotolo kuo ʻoatu ʻe he taha ko ia ‘a e fakangofua.

VAHE 2 – NGAAHI LEKOOTI

#### 15 Tokangaʻi ʻo e ngaahi lekooti

(1) Kuo pau ke fakapapauʻi ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻoku tauhi, ʻave mo fakaʻauha ʻa e ngaahi lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku ne tauhi pe ʻoku ne puleʻi ʻi ha founga ʻoku malu pea fakatatau mo e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻi hono tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(2) Neongo ‘a e kupu siʻi (1), kuo pau ke tauhi ‘e he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui

‘oku ne tauhi pe puleʻi ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ‘a ia ko e makatuʻunga ʻo ha kole ke hū ʻi he kupu 58 ‘o e Lao ni, ʻa e fakamatala ki he loloa ‘e ala fiemaʻu ke malava ʻa e taha ko ia ʻo ngaueʻaki ha fili ʻi he Lao ni ʻa ia te ne ala fakahoko ʻo fekauʻaki mo e kole.

(3) ʻIhe kupusiʻi(1),ko efakaʻauhaʻi hafounga ʻoku malu, ʻokuʻikai kauai hono fakaʻauha ʻo e ngaahi lekooti tukukehe ka ʻoku fakaʻauha ʻa e ngaahi lekooti ʻi hafoungaʻeʻikailavahanotoefaʻuʻoengaahilekootiʻofakatataukihengaahi tukunga.

#### 16 Ngaahi feituʻu ʻoku tauhi ai ʻa e lekooti

(1) ʻE ngofua ke tauhi ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ʻi he ʻapi ʻo taha ko ia ʻi ha founga fakapotopoto ʻa ia ʻe loto ki ai ʻa e taha ko ia.

(2) ʻE ngofua ke tauhi ʻe ha taha ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ʻi ha feituʻu kehe mei he ʻapi ʻo e taha ko ia pea kehe mei ha feituʻu ʻoku puleʻi ʻe he taha ngaue – Kupu 17 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 20 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(a) ʻoku tauhi ʻa e lekooti ʻi ha founga ʻoku fakapotopoto;

(b) ʻoku loto ki ai ʻa e taha ko ia; pea

(c) ko e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻi hono tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, kuo fakakakato.

VAHE 3 – TALIUI MO E ʻATĀ KE NGAUEʻAKI

#### 17 Taha fetuʻutaki

(1) Kuo pau ke fokotuʻu ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha taha fetuʻutaki ʻa ia ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi (3).

(2) Kuo pau ke fokotuʻu ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku ʻikai ko ha taha fakaenatula ha taha fetuʻutaki ʻa ia ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi (3).

(3) Ko e taha fetuʻutaki ko ha fakafofonga ʻo e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui pea kuo fakamafai ia ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ke ne –

(a) fakafaingofua ʻa e faipau ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he Lao ni;

(b) fakapapauʻi ʻoku fakaha totonu ki he kotoa ʻo e kau fakafofonga ʻo e tauhi fakamatala ʻo fekauʻaki mo honau ngaahi ngafa ʻi he Lao ni;

(c) tali‘aengaahifakaʻekeʻekemeihekakaiʻofekauʻakimoengaahingaue fakamatala ʻa e tauhi fakamatala;

(d) tali ‘a e ngaahi kole ‘a ha taha ke aʻu ki he, pe fakatonutonu ha lekooti ‘o e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo e taha ko ia ʻa ia ʻoku ne tauhi pe ʻoku puleʻi ʻe he tauhi fakamatala; pea

(e) maʻu ʻa e ngaahi launga mei he kakai ʻo fekauʻaki mo e tukuakiʻi ‘o e tauhi fakamatala ki hono maumauʻi ‘a e Lao ni.

(4) Kuo pau ke fakahoko ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻiate ia pe ʻa ia ko ha taha fakaenatula pea ʻoku ʻikai ke ne fokotuʻu ha taha fetuʻutaki ʻi he kupu siʻi

(1) ʻa e ngaahi fatongia kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi (3).

#### 18 Fakamatala tohi ki he kakai

(1) Kuo pau ke fakaʻatā ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala tohi ki he kakai ʻa ia ʻoku –

(a) hāʻiaihafakamatalafakalukufuaʻoengaahingauefakamatalaʻaetauhi fakamatala;

(b) fakamatala ai ʻa e founga ke fetuʻutaki –

(i) ki he taha fetuʻutaki ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu 17(3), kapau ʻoku ʻi ai ha taha fetuʻutaki ʻa e tauhi fakamatala, pe Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 19 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 21

(ii) ki he tauhi fakamatala, kapau ʻoku ʻikai ke ʻi ai ha taha fetuʻutaki ʻa e tauhi fakamatala;

(c) fakamatala ʻa e founga ke hū ai ha taha pe ke kole ha fakatonutonu ʻo ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ʻa ia ʻoku tauhi pe puleʻi ʻe he tauhi fakamatala; pea

(d) fakamatala ʻa e founga ke fakahoko ai ha launga ki he tauhi fakamatala mo e ‘Ofisa Pule Ngaue ʻi he Lao ni.

(2) Kapau ‘e ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha, ʻo ʻikai ha fakangofua ʻa e taha ko ia, ‘i ha founga ‘a ia ‘oku ‘i tuʻa ʻi he fakamatala ʻo e ngaahi ngaue fakamatala ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ʻi he kupu siʻi (1)(a), kuo pau ko e tauhi fakamatala te ne –

(a) fakaha ki he taha ko ia ʻa e ngaahi ngaueʻaki mo e fakaha ʻi he fuofua faingamalie fakapotopoto, ʻi he kupu 57 ‘o e Lao ni, ʻoku ʻikai ha totonu ke hū ʻa e taha ko ia ki he lekooti ʻo e fakamatala;

(b) hiki ha fakamatala ʻo e ngaahi ngaueʻaki mo e fakaha; pea

(c) tauhi ‘a e fakamatala ko e konga ‘o e ngaahi lekooti ‘o e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekau’aki mo e taha ko ia ‘a ia ‘oku ne tauhi pe ‘oku ne puleʻi pe ʻi ha fotunga ʻa ia ʻoku fehokotaki ki he ngaahi lekooti ko ia.

(3) Kuo pau ke ʻi Tonga ni ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui kuo ʻuhinga ki ai ʻi he kupu ni mo ha taha fetuʻutaki kuo fokotuʻu ʻi he kupu 17(1).

#### 19 Ngaahi fakafofonga mo e fakamatala

(1) ʻOku tokangaekina ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku tauhi pe ʻoku puleʻi ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui pea ʻe ngofua ke ne fakangofua ʻa e fakafofonga ʻo e tauhi fakamatala ke ne tanaki, ngaueʻaki, fakaha, tauhi pe fakaʻauha ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ʻo kapau pe –

(a) kuo fakangofua pe tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ke ne hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki, fakaha, tauhi pe fakaʻauha ʻa e fakamatala, koe hā pe ʻe hoko;

(b) ko hono hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki, fakaha, tauhi pe fakaʻauha ʻo e fakamatala, koe ha pe ʻe hoko, ʻoku fiemaʻu ia ʻi hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaahi ngafa ʻo e fakafofonga pea ʻoku ʻikai maumau ai ʻa e Lao ni pe ha lao kehe; pea

(c) ʻoku fakakakato ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ko ia ʻi hono tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(2) ʻE ngofua ke fakatatau ha fakangofua kuo foaki ki ha fakafofonga ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) ki he ngaahi makatuʻunga pe fakangatangata ko ia ʻe ngofua ke hilifaki ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui.

Kupu 19 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 22 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(3) Fakatatau ki ha fakaʻatā ʻa ia ʻe ngofua ke tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, ʻe ngofua ke tanaki, ngaueʻaki, fakaha, tauhi pe fakaʻauha ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻe ha fakafofonga ʻo ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻo kapau pe –

(a) ko hono tanaki, ngaueʻaki, fakaha, tauhi pe fakaʻauha ʻo e fakamatala, koe ha pe ʻe hoko –

(i) kuo fakangofua ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻo fakatatau mo e kupu siʻi

(1),

(ii) ʻoku fiemaʻu koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa hono fakahoko hono ngaahi ngafa ko e fakafofonga ʻo e tauhi fakamatala,

(iii) ʻoku ʻikai maumau ai ʻa e Lao ni pe ha lao kehe, pea

(iv) ʻoku faipau ki ha ngaahi makatuʻunga pe fakangatangata kuo hilifaki ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻi he kupu siʻi (2); pea

(b) ʻoku fakakakato ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ko ia ʻi hono tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(4) Kuo pau ko e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui te ne –

(a) fakahoko ʻa e ngaahi ngaue ʻa ia ʻoku fakapotopoto ʻi he ngaahi tukunga ke fakapapauʻi ʻoku ʻikai tanaki, ngaueʻaki, fakaha, tauhi pe fakaʻauha ʻe ha fakafofonga ‘a e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui kae ʻoua ʻoku fakahoko ia ʻo fakatatau mo e kupu siʻi (3); pea

(b) kei tokangaekina ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia kuo tanaki, ngaueʻaki, fakaha, tauhi, pe fakaʻauha ʻe he kau fakafofonga ʻo e tauhi fakamatala, ʻo tatau ai pe pe ko e tanaki, ngaueʻaki, fakaha, tauhi pe fakaʻauha naʻe fakahoko ʻo fakatatau mo e kupu siʻi (3).

(5) Kuo pau ko e fakafofonga ʻo ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui te ne –

(a) faipau ki he ngaahi makatuʻunga pe fakangatangata kuo hilifaki ‘e he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ki he tanaki, ngaueʻaki, fakaha, tauhi pe fakaʻauha ʻe he fakafofonga ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu siʻi (2); pea

(b) fakaha ki he tauhi fakamatala ʻi he fuofua faingamalie fakapotopoto kapau kuo kaihaʻasi pe mole ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui kuo tanaki, ngaueʻaki, fakaha, tauhi pe fakaʻauha ʻe he fakafofonga ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e tauhi fakamatala pe ʻoku ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ia ʻo ʻikai ha fakamafai.

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 20 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 23

## KONGA 3 – FAKANGOFUA, MALAVA, FAKAFOFONGA

VAHE 1 – FAKANGOFUA

#### 20 Ngaahi ʻelemeniti ʻo e fakangofua

(1) Kapau ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Lao ni, pe ha Lao kehe, ʻa e fakangofua ʻa ha taha, kuo pau ke malava ʻa e taha ko ia ʻo ʻoatu ʻa e fakangofua pea ko e fakangofua ko ia kuo pau ko e fakangofua fakapatonu –

(a) ʻoku felaveʻi mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku fiemaʻu ki ai ʻa e fakangofua;

(b) kuo fakataukeiʻi;

(c) kuo fakahoko tauʻataina;

(d) ʻoku ʻikai maʻu ʻo fou ʻi ha fakamatala taʻemoʻoni pe kaka; pea

(e) ko e fakamuimui taha ia ʻi he ngaahi tukunga.

(2) Kuo fakataukeiʻi feʻunga ha fakangofua kapau ‘oku maʻu ʻe he taha ʻoku ne ʻoatu ʻa e fakangofua, ʻi he taimi hono ʻoatu ʻo e fakangofua ko ia, ʻa e fakamatala ʻe fiemaʻu ʻe ha taha fakapotopoto ʻi he ngaahi tukunga ke ne fakahoko ʻaki ha faituʻutuʻuni fekauʻaki mo e meʻa ʻa ia ʻoku felaveʻi mo e fakangofua.

(3) Kapau ʻoku felaveʻi ʻa e fakangofua mo hono tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, ko e fakamatala ʻoku lave ki ai ʻi he kupu siʻi

(2) ʻa ia ʻe fie maʻu ʻe ha taha fakapotopoto kuo pau ke kau ki ai ʻa e –

(a) taumuʻa ʻo e tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha;

(b) natula mo e ngataʻanga ʻo e fakamatala ke tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha;

(c) ki he ngataʻanga fakapotopoto ʻe lava, pe ʻe ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻa e fakamatala ko ia ʻi tuʻa ʻi Tonga;

(d) ki he ngataʻanga fakapotopoto ʻe lava, ko e ngaahi nunuʻa ʻoku lava ʻo fakafuofuaʻi fakapotopoto koeʻuhi ko hono ʻoatu pe taʻofi ʻo e fakangofua ki he tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha;

(e) ki he ngataʻanga fakapotopoto ʻe lava, ko e fakamatala fakaʻilongaʻi ʻo e taha te ne hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha pe maʻu mai ʻa e fakamatala; mo e

(f) fakangofua ʻa ia ʻe ngofua ke ʻoatu pe taʻofi ʻe he taha ko ia.

(4) Fakatatau ki he kupu siʻi (5), ʻe ngofua ke fakamahino pe ʻikai fakahangatonu ʻa e fakangofua ki he hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo fekauʻaki mo ha taha.

(5) Kuopaukefakamahinoʻaefakangofuakihonofakahaʻoefakamatalataautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha kapau – Kupu 21 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 24 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(a) ʻoku fakahoko ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakaha ki ha taha ʻa ia ʻoku ʻikai ko ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui; pe

(b) ʻoku fakahoko ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakaha ki ha tauhi fakamatala ʻe taha ki he moʻui pea ʻoku ʻikai fakahoko ʻa e fakaha koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ke fakahoko ʻa e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui pe tokoni ʻi hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui.

(6) ʻOku ʻikai ngaueʻaki ʻa e kupu siʻi (5) ki ha faʻahinga fakaha ʻa ia ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni pea ʻoku ʻikai kau ki ai ha fakamatala fekauʻaki mo e tuʻunga moʻui lelei ʻo ha taha.

(7) Kuopauʻeʻikailauʻokufakahokotauʻatainahafakangofuaʻaiakuotuʻutuʻuni ko e makatuʻunga ia ke maʻu ʻaki ha ngaue pe ha monuʻia koeʻuhi pe ko hono ʻoatu ʻo e fakangofua ko ia ko ha makatuʻunga ia ke maʻu ʻaki ʻa e ngaue pe monuʻia, ka kuo pau ko e fakamatala ʻa ia ʻoku felaveʻi mo e fakangofua ʻoku fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ia.

(8) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai tuʻutuʻuni ʻe ha taha ko ha makatuʻunga ki hono fakahoko ha ngaue pe ha monuʻia, ke fakangofua ʻe ha taha ʻa hono tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo ha fakamatala lahi ange ʻi he meʻa ʻoku fiemau fakapotopoto ke fakahoko ʻaki ʻa e ngaue pe monuʻia.

(9) Kuo pau ko ha fakangofua ʻoku felaveʻi mo hono tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo e fakamatala ʻo e sivi faʻunga tupuʻanga ko ha fakangofua ʻoku fakapatonu pe ki he fakamatala sivi faʻunga tupuʻanga pea kuo pau ke fakakehekeheʻi ia mei ha fakangofua kuo ʻoatu ki hono tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo ha faʻahinga kehe ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui.

(10) ‘E ngofua ke fakamahino ‘e ha taha ‘oku ne ‘oatu ha fakangofua ‘a e ngaahi fakangatangata mo e makatuʻunga ke ngaueʻaki ki he fakangofua ko ia kau ki ai ʻa e taimi ʻe ngata ai ‘a hono ‘aonga ‘o e fakangofua.

(11) Ko ha fakangofua ʻoku ʻoatu ʻe ha taha, ki hono tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo e fakamatala ʻa ia ko e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ko ha fakangofua ia ʻoku fakalao kapau ʻoku ne fakakakato ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻo e Lao ni ki he fakangofua.

#### 21 Fakataʻeʻaongaʻi ʻo e fakangofua

(1) ʻE ngofua ke fakataʻeʻaongaʻi ʻe ha taha ʻoku ne ʻoatu ha fakangofua ʻi he Lao ni, ʻo tatau pe ʻoku fakamahino pe ʻikai fakahangatonu ʻa e fakangofua, ʻi ha taimi pe ka kuo pau ko e fakataʻeʻaongaʻi ko ia ʻe ʻikai ʻi ai hano uesia ʻe lau fakafoki.

(2) Kapau ʻe fokotuʻu ʻe ha taha ha makatuʻunga ʻi heʻene fakangofua ke tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku fekauʻaki mo e taha ko ia, ʻe ʻikai ngaueʻaki ʻa e makatuʻunga ko ia ki he tuʻunga ʻoku pehe ke ne taʻofi pe fakangatangata ʻaki hano lekooti ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 22 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 25 ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he lao pe ʻe he ngaahi tuʻunga ngaue fakapalofesinale kuo tali pe founga ngaue ʻa e ngaueʻanga.

#### 22 Lau ʻoku fakalao

Ka ʻi ai ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui kuo ne maʻu ha fakangofua ʻi he Lao ni ʻoku ʻi ai ʻene totonu ke ne pehe kuo fakakakato ʻe he fakangofua ko ia ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻo e kupu ni pea ʻoku teʻeki ai ke fakataʻeʻaongaʻi, tukukehe kapau ʻoku ʻikai fakapotopoto ke fakakaukau pehe ai.

#### 23 Taha kuo pekia

ʻE ngofua kapau kuo pekia ha taha, ke fakangofua ʻe ha taha kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he taha ʻo e ngaahi palakalafi ko eni, ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e pekia pe ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui felaveʻi mo e pekia –

(a) ko e talasiti ʻo e koloa ʻa e pekia; pe

(b) ko e taha kuo ne maʻu ʻa e fatongia ke puleʻi ʻa e koloa ʻa e pekia, kapau ko e pekia ʻoku ʻikai ke ʻi ai haʻane talasiti ʻo e koloa.

VAHE 2 – MALAVA

#### 24 Malava

(1) ʻOku malava ha taha ʻo fekauʻaki mo hono ʻoatu ha fakangofua ʻi he Lao ni kapau ko e taha ko ia ʻoku ne –

(a) mahinoʻi ʻa e natula ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku felaveʻi mo e fakangofua; pea

(b) mahuʻingaʻia ʻi he ngaahi nunuʻa ʻoku lava ʻo vakai fakapotopoto ki ai ʻi he kahaʻu koeʻuhi ko hono ʻoatu pe taʻofi ʻo e fakangofua.

(2) ʻE malava ha taha ‘o fekauʻaki mo hono ‘oatu ‘o ha fakangofua ʻi he Lao ni ʻi heʻene felaveʻi mo ha ngaahi konga ʻe niʻihi ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, kae ʻikai malava ʻi heʻene fekauʻaki mo e ngaahi konga kehe.

(3) ʻE malava ha taha ‘o fekauʻaki mo hono ‘oatu ‘o ha fakangofua ʻi he Lao ni ‘i he taimi ʻe taha pea ʻikai malava ʻi ha taimi ʻe taha.

#### 25 Lau ʻoku malava

(1) ʻOku lau ʻoku malava ʻe ha taha ʻi heʻene fekauʻaki mo hono ʻoatu ha fakangofua ʻi he Lao ni.

Kupu 26 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 26 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(2) ʻE ngofua ke fakafalala ha taha ʻi he lau ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) tukukehe ka ʻi ai ha ngaahi ʻuhinga fakapotopoto ʻa e taha ko ia ke ne tui ʻoku ʻikai malava ʻa e taha ko ia ʻi heʻene fekauʻaki mo hono ʻoatu ha fakangofua.

#### 26 Ko hai ʻe lava ke ne ʻoatu ʻa e fakangofua pe ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala

(1) Kapau ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Lao ni ʻa e fakangofua ʻa ha taha, ʻoku lave ki ha fakangofua ʻa ha taha, pe ki hono ʻoatu ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui felaveʻi mo e taha ko ia, ʻe ngofua ke ʻoatu ʻe ha taha kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi ha taha ʻo e ngaahi palakalafi ko ʻeni ʻa e fakangofua kapau ʻoku ʻikai malava ʻa e taha ko ia pe te ne ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia –

(a) kapau ʻoku malava ʻa e taha ko ia ʻo ʻoatu ʻa e fakangofua, ko e taha ko ia pe ha taha ʻa ia kuo fakamafai ʻe he taha ko ia ʻi ha tohi ke ne fakafofongaʻi ia pe ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia;

(b) kapau ʻoku ʻikai malava ʻa e taha ko ia ʻo fekauʻaki mo hono ʻoatu ʻa e fakangofua, ko e taha ʻa ia kuo fakamafai ʻi he kupu 29 ke ne fakangofua ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia pe ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia;

(c) kapau kuo pekia ʻa e taha ko ia, ko e taha ʻa ia kuo fakamafai ʻi he kupu 23 ke ne fakangofua ʻo fetongi ʻa e taha ko ia pe ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia; pe

(d) ha taha ʻa ia kuo fakamafai pe tuʻutuʻuni ʻe ha Lao kehe ke ne fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia pe ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia.

(2) Ka ʻi ai ha taha kuo fakamafai ʻe he Lao ni ke ne fakangofua ʻo fakafofongaʻi ha taha pe ʻoatu ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia ʻoku ʻi ai foki ʻenetotonu ke ʻikai loto pe fakataʻeʻaongaʻi ʻae fakangofua

‘o fakafofongaʻi ‘a e taha ko ia pe ʻikai loto ke ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia.

(3) ʻOku ʻikai ngaueʻaki ʻa e kupu siʻi (1) ki ha fakangofua ʻoku fakahoko ʻe ha taha ʻi ha Lao kehe, ki ha hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo e fakamatala ʻa ia ko ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui.

#### 27 Taha ʻoku ʻikai malava: niʻihi ʻe ngofua ke ʻoatu ʻenau fakangofua

(1) Kapau ʻe ʻikai malava ʻe ha taha ʻo ʻoatu ha fakangofua, ʻe ngofua ki ha taha kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he taha ʻo e ngaahi palakalafi ko ʻeni, ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia, ke ne fakangofua hono tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻa e fakamatala pe ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia –

(a) ko e tauhi ʻo e taha ko ia, kapau ʻoku felaveʻi ʻa e fakangofua ki he mafai ʻo e tauhi ke ne fakahoko ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia;

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 27 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 27

(b) ko e fakafofonga ʻo e taha ko ia ʻa ia kuo fokotuʻu ʻe he Fakamaauʻanga ʻi he kupu 29 ‘o e Lao ni, kapau ʻoku maʻu ʻe he fakafofonga ʻa e mafai ke ne ʻoatu ʻa e fakangofua;

(c) ko e mali ʻo e taha ko ia;

(d) ko ha tamasiʻi pe matuʻa ʻo e taha ko ia pe ha taha kehe ʻa ia ʻoku ʻi ai ʻene totonu fakalao ke ʻoatu pe ʻikai ʻoatu ha fakangofua ʻo fetongi ʻa e matuʻa.ʻOkuʻikaikauʻihepalakalafikoʻenihamatuʻa‘okumaʻumafai fakataimi ki he taha ko ia. Kapau ʻoku totonu fakalao ha taha kehe ke ne fakangofua ʻo fetongi ʻa e matuʻa, ʻoku ʻikai fakakau ‘a e matu’a ʻi he palakalafi ko ʻeni;

(e) ko ha matuʻa ʻa e taha ko ia ʻa ia ko ʻene maʻu mafai fakataimi pe ki he taha ko ia;

(f) ko ha tokoua pe tuofefine ʻo e taha ko ia; pe

(g) ko ha kainga kehe ʻo e taha ko ia.

(2) ʻE ngofua ke fakangofua ʻeha taha kuofakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) ʻo kapau pe ko e taha ko ia –

(a) ʻoku ne malava ʻo fekau’aki mo hono ʻoatu ha fakangofua;

(b) ʻoku ʻikai toe siʻi hifo ʻi he taʻu 16 pe ko hamatuʻa ʻa etaha ʻoku felaveʻi mo ia ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui;

(c) ʻoku ʻikai tapui ʻe ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e fakamaauʻanga pe ha aleapau ke fakamavahe mei he maʻu mafai fakataimi ki he taha ʻa ia ʻoku felaveʻi mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui pe mei hono ʻoatu pe ʻikai ʻoatu ha fakangofua ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia;

(d) ʻoku faingamalie; pea

(e) ʻoku loto ke ne fakahoko ʻa e fatongia ke ne tuʻutuʻuni pe ʻe ʻoatu ha fakangofua pe ʻikai.

(3) Koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻaʻo e kupu siʻi(2)(d),ʻoku faingamalie ha taha kapau ʻoku malava, ke ne fetuʻutaki mo e taha ʻi loto ʻi ha taimi ʻoku fakapotopoto ʻo fakatatau ki he ngaahi tukunga, momaʻuhatuʻutuʻuni ke neʻoatu pe ʻikai ʻoatu ʻa e fakangofua.

(4) ʻE ngofua ke tuʻutuʻuni ʻe ha taha kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he palakalafi ʻo e kupu siʻi (1) ke ne ‘oatu pe ‘ikai ‘oatu ha fakangofua ‘o kapau pe ‘oku ‘ikai fakakakato ʻe ha taha kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi ha palakalafi ki muʻa ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻo e kupu siʻi (2).

(5) Neongo ʻa e kupu siʻi (4), ʻe ngofua ke tuʻutuʻuni ʻe ha taha kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he palakalafi ʻo e kupu siʻi (1) ʻa ia ʻoku ne ʻi ai pe kuo fetuʻutaki ki ai, ke ne ʻoatu pe ʻikai ʻoatu ʻa e fakangofua kapau ʻoku tui ʻa e taha ko ia –

(a) ʻoku ʻikai ke ʻi ai ha taha kehe ʻa ia kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi ha palakalafi kimuʻa pe ha palakalafi tatau; pe Kupu 28 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 28 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(b) neongo ʻa e ʻi ai ha taha kehe pehe, ko e taha kehe ko ia ʻoku ʻikai ko ha taha ia kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi (1)(a), (b) pe (c) pea ʻe ʻikai ke ne fakakikihiʻi ʻa e taha ko ia ʻoku ʻi ai pe kehe ange kuo fetuʻutaki ki ai ke ne fakahoko tuʻutuʻuni.

#### 28 Ngaahi meʻa ke vakaiʻi fekauʻaki mo e toe fakangofua

Ka ʻi ai ha taha ʻoku ne fakahoko ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻo fekauʻaki mo hono ʻoatu pe ʻikai ʻoatu ʻo ha fakangofua ʻi he Lao ni ʻo fakafofongaʻi pe fetongi ha taha pe ʻoatu ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui felaveʻi mo e taha ko ia ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia, kuo pau ke ne vakaiʻi –

(a) ʻa e ngaahi fakaʻamu, tuʻunga mo e tui ʻa ia –

(i) kapau naʻe malava ʻe he taha ko ia, ʻoku ʻiloʻi ʻe he tahako ia ʻoku moʻui ʻaki mo tui ki ai ʻa e taha ʻe fiemaʻu ʻe he taha ko ia ke fakaha ia ʻi he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻoku fakahoko fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku felaveʻi mo ia, pe

(ii) kapau ko e taha ko ia ʻoku ʻikai malava pe kuo pekia, ʻoku ʻilo ʻe he taha ko ia naʻe moʻui ‘aki ʻe he taha ko ia ʻi heʻene malava pe kei moʻui pea ʻoku ne tui naʻe mei fiemaʻu ʻe he taha ko ia ke fakaha ia ʻi he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni kuo fakahoko ʻo fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku felaveʻi mo ia;

(b) pe ʻoku mahulu atu ʻa e ngaahi monuʻia ʻoku ʻamanaki ʻa e taha ko ia ke maʻu mei hono tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo e fakamatala ʻi he faingamalie ʻo ha ngaahi nunuʻa kovi ʻe hoko ko ha ola ʻo e tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha;

(c) pe ʻoku lava ʻo fakakakato ʻa e taumuʻa ʻoku fekumi ki ai ʻa e tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo ʻikai fakahoko ha tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha; pea

(d) pe ʻoku fiemaʻu ʻa e tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ki hono fakakakato ʻo ha fatongia fakalao.

#### 29 Tohi kole ke toe vakaiʻi hono tuʻutuʻuni ʻo e taʻe-malava

(1) ‘E ngofua ke kole ha taha ki he Fakamaauʻanga ‘a ia ‘oku felaveʻi mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui kuo fakapapauʻi ʻoku ne taʻe-malava ke ‘oatu

‘a e fakangofua, ke toe vakaiʻi ʻa e tuʻutuʻuni.

(2) Ko e ngaahi faʻahi ki he tohi kole ko e –

(a) taha ʻoku kole ke fakahoko ʻa e toe vakaiʻi;

(b) tauhi fakamatala ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui; mo e

(c) kotoa ʻo e niʻihi kehe kuo fakamahino pau ʻe he Fakamaauʻanga.

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 30 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 29

(3) ʻE ngofua ke fakapapauʻi ʻe he Fakamaauʻanga ʻa hono tuʻutuʻuni ʻoku ʻikai malava pe ʻe ngofua ke tuʻutuʻuni ʻoku malava ʻa e taha ko ia ke ʻoatu ʻa e fakangofua.

(4) Kapau ʻoku fakapapauʻi ʻa e tuʻutuʻuni ʻoku ʻikai malava ha taha ʻo ʻoatu ha fakangofua ʻi hono fakahoko ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘aofangatuku ʻo ha tohi kole ʻi he kupu ni, kuo pau ʻe ʻikai fakahoko ʻe he taha ko ia ha tohi kole foʻou ʻi he kupu ni ʻi loto ʻi he mahina ʻe ono hili ʻa e fakahoko ha tuʻutuʻuni ‘aofangatuku ʻo e tohi kole ki muʻa, tukukehe ka fakangofua tomuʻa ia ʻe he Fakamaauʻanga.

(5) ʻE ngofua ke fakangofua ʻe he Fakamaauʻanga ke fakahoko ʻa e tohi kole foʻou kapau ʻoku ne fiemaʻu kuo ʻi ai ha liliu lahi ʻi he ngaahi makatuʻunga ʻa ia ʻoku fakamoʻoniʻi ʻaki hano toe fakakaukauʻi ʻo e tuʻunga malava ʻo e taha ko ia.

VAHE 3 – FAKAFOFONGA

#### 30 Tohi kole ki hono fokotuʻu ʻo ha fakafofonga

(1) ʻE ngofua ke kole ʻe ha taha ki he Fakamaauʻanga ʻa ia ‘oku taʻu 16 pea ʻoku ʻikai malava ʻo fekauʻaki mo hono ʻoatu ʻene fakangofua, ki hono fokotuʻu ʻo ha fakafofonga ke ne fakangofua ʻo fakafofongaʻi pe ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia.

(2) Kapau ʻoku teʻeki ai ke kole ʻa e taha ko ia ki he Fakamaauʻanga ʻi he kupu siʻi

(1), kuo pau ke fakaha ki ai ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻi he vave taha ʻe ala lava hili hono fakapapauʻi ʻoku ʻikai malava ʻa e taha ko ia ʻo fokotuʻu ha fakafofonga, ʻo fekauʻaki mo ʻene totonu ke kole ki he Fakamaauʻanga ki hono fokotuʻu ʻo ha fakafofonga.

(3) ʻOku ʻikai ngaueʻaki ʻa e kupu siʻi (1) mo e (2) kapau ko e taha ʻoku felaveʻi mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku ʻi ai hano tauhi ʻa ia ʻoku ʻi ai hono mafai ke ʻoatu pe ʻikai ʻoatu ʻa e fakangofua ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia pe ke ne ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia.

(4) Ko e ngaahi faʻahi ki he tohi kole ko e –

(a) taha ko ia;

(b) fakafofonga ʻoku fokotuʻu atu;

(c) taha kotoa pe ʻa ia kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu 27(1),(3),(4) pe (6); mo

(d) ha taha kehe kuo tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Fakamaauʻanga.

(5) ‘E ngofua ke fakamafai ‘e he Fakamaau’anga ‘i ha fokotuʻu ʻi he kupu ni, ke fakangofua ʻe he fakafofonga ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha pe ke ne ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia ki ha –

(a) tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe ha fakaha pau ʻi ha taimi pau;

Kupu 30 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 30 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(b) tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo e faʻahinga ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Fakamaauʻanga ʻi he ngaahi tukunga kuo tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Fakamaauʻanga, kapau kuo fakapapauʻi ko e taha ko ia ʻoku ʻikai ke ne malava ʻo ʻoatu ʻa e fakangofua ʻoku fiemaʻu ‘i he taimi ko ia; pe

(c) tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻi ha taimi pe, kapau kuo fakapapauʻi ko e taha ko ia ʻoku ʻikai ke ne malava ʻo ʻoatu ʻa e fakangofua ʻi he taimi ʻoku fiemaʻu ai ʻa e fakangofua.

(6) Kuo pau ke fokotuʻu ʻe he Fakamaauʻanga ha taha ko ha fakafofonga maʻae taha ko ia ‘o kapau pe ʻoku fakangofua ʻe he taha ko ia ʻa e fokotuʻu pea –

(a) ʻoku fiemalie ʻa e Fakamaauʻanga ko e taha ko ia ʻoku taʻu 16 pea ʻoku mahino ʻoku ne malava koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa faituʻutuʻuni pe ʻe ʻoatu ʻa e fakangofua pe ʻikai ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia, pe ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo felaveʻi mo e taha; pea

(b) ʻoku fokotuʻu atu ʻe he Fakamaauʻanga ko e fokotuʻu ko e lelei taha ia maʻae taha ko ia, hili ʻene vakai ki he ngaahi meʻa ʻa ia ʻoku fokotuʻu ʻi he kupu 28.

(7) ‘E ngofua ki he Fakamaauʻanga –

(a) ke ne fokotuʻu ha taha kehe ko ha fakafofonga mei he taha kuo fakahingoa ʻi he tohi kole;

(b) ke ne fakangatangata ʻa e vahaʻa taimi ʻo e fokotuʻu;

(c) ke ne hilifaki ha makatuʻunga kehe ki he fokotuʻu; pea

(d) ʻi he tohi kole ʻa ha taha, ke ne toʻo, liliu pe taʻofi fakataimi ha makatuʻunga kuo hilifaki ki he fokotuʻu pe ko ha makatuʻunga ʻoku tanaki atu ki he fokotuʻu.

(8) ‘E ngofua kefakataʻeʻaongaʻiʻe he Fakamaauʻanga ʻae fokotuʻu kuo fakahoko ʻi he kupu ni kapau –

(a) ʻoku kole ʻe he taha ko ia ʻoku ʻikai malava pe ko e fakafofonga ʻa e fakataʻeʻaongaʻi;

(b) ʻoku ʻikai toe lava ʻe he fakafofonga ʻo ʻoatu ʻa e fakangofua pe ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui;

(c) ko e fokotuʻu ʻoku ʻikai toe ʻi he lelei ʻa e taha ‘oku ‘ikai malava, hili ha vakai ki he ngaahi meʻa ʻoku ha ʻi he kupu 28; pe

(d) ko e taha ‘oku ‘ikai malava ‘oku ‘i ai hono tauhi ‘a ia ‘oku ne ma’u ‘a e mafai ke fakangofua pe ‘ikai fakangofua pe ‘oatu ‘a e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ‘o felaveʻi mo e taha ko ia.

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 31 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 31

## KONGA 4 – NGAAHI TUʻUTUʻUNI KI HONO TANAKI,

NGAUEʻAKI MO HONO FAKAHA ʻO E FAKAMATALA TAAUTAHA KI HE MOʻUI VAHE 1 – NGAAHI FAKANGATANGATA MO E TUʻUTUʻUNI FAKALUKUFUA

#### 31 Tuʻutuʻuni ʻo e fakangofua

Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai ke tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha kae ʻoua –

(a) kuo ne maʻu ʻa e fakangofua ʻa e taha ko ia ʻi he Lao ni pea ko e tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha, ko e ha pe ʻe hoko, ʻoku fiemaʻu koeʻuhi ko ha taumuʻa ʻoku fakalao ki he lelei taha ʻo e ʻilo ʻa e tauhi fakamatala; pe

(b) tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha, ko e ha pe ʻe hoko, kuo fakangofua pe tuʻutuʻuni ‘i he Lao ni.

#### 32 Fakamatala kehe

(1) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai ke tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui kapau ʻe fakakakato ʻe ha fakamatala kehe ʻa e taumuʻa ʻo e tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha.

(2) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai ke tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo lahi ange ʻi hono fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ke ne fakakakato ʻa e taumuʻa ʻo e tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha, ko e ha pe ʻe hoko.

(3) ʻOku ʻikai ngaueʻaki ʻa e kupu ni ki he fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he lao ke tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui.

#### 33 Ngaueʻaki mo e fakaha ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui

Kuo pauʻeʻikai ngaueʻakipe fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatalakihemoʻui ʻoku netanaki ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo maumau ai ʻa e Lao ni kae ʻoua kuo tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he lao ke ne fakahoko ia.

#### 34 Feinga Paʻanga

(1) Fakatatau ki he kupu siʻi (2), ʻe ngofua ke hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ʻo ha ngaahi ngaue feinga paʻanga ʻo kapau pe –

(a) ʻoku fakangofua fakamahino ʻe he taha ko ia; pe Kupu 35 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 32 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(b) ʻoku fakangofua ʻehe tahako iaʻaki hafakangofua fakamahino peaʻoku kau ʻi he fakamatala ʻa e hingoa pe ʻo e taha ko ia mo e faʻahinga ʻo e fakamatala ʻo e fetuʻutaki ‘a ia ‘e ngofua ke tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(2) Kuo pau ke faipau ʻa e founga ʻa ia ʻoku maʻu ʻaki ʻa e fakangofua ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) mo e ola ʻo e tanaki, ngaueʻaki, pe fakaha ‘o e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ʻo e ngaahi ngaue feinga paʻanga, ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni mo e fakangatangata ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai.

#### 35 Fakamaketi

Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke fakamaketi ha meʻa pe koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa fakatotolo ʻa e maketi tukukehe kapau kuo fakangofua fakamahino ia ʻe he tahako ia pea ʻoku tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e fakamatala, ko e ha pe meʻa ʻe hoko, ʻo fakatatau ki he ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni mo e fakangatangata ko ia, ʻi he meʻa ʻe ngofua ke tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

#### 36 Ngaahi fika falemahaki

(1) ʻI he kupu ni –

“fika falemahaki” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ha fika falemahaki kuo ʻoatu ki ha taha ʻi he Lao ki he Ngaahi Ngaue ki he Moʻui [Vahe 12.06]; mo e

“ngaue ki he moʻui” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ha ngaue ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku fakahoko ʻi he Lao ki he Ngaahi Ngaue ki he Moʻui [Vahe 12.06].

(2) Neongo ʻa e kupu 53(1) ‘o e Lao ni, kuo pau ʻe ʻikai tanaki pe ngaueʻaki ʻe ha taha ʻa ia ʻoku ʻikai ko ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui pe ʻoku fakahoko fatongia ko ha fakafofonga ʻo ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fika falemahaki ʻa ha taha kehe tukukehe –

(a) koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa felaveʻi mo hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaahi ngaue ki he moʻui ki he taha kehe ko ia;

(b) koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻa ia kuo fakaha ai ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fika ki he taha ko ia;

(c) kapau kuo tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e taha ko ia ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni pea ʻoku ne tanaki pe ngaueʻaki ʻa e fika falemahaki, ko e hā pe ‘e hoko, ʻaki ʻa e fakangofua mahino ʻa e taha kehe, koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke tonu hono fakahaaʻi ʻo e ngaahi lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo e taha kehe ko ia, fakamoʻoniʻi honau fakahaaʻi pe fakafehokotaki ʻenau ngaahi lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, ‘o fakatatau ki he Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 36 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 33 ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni fakalahi, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni;

(d) kapau ko e taha ko ia naʻe fokotuʻu ʻe he Poate ʻi he Lao ki he Kau Toketa, Kau Ngaue Faitoʻo ki he Nifo mo e Kau Ngaue ki he Moʻui 2021, ko e Lao ki he Kau Neesi, Kau Neesi Fakafaitoʻo mo e Kau Maʻuli Fakapolofesinale 2021 pe ko e Lao ki he Huʻivai [Vahe 12.14], pea ʻoku ne tanaki pe ngaueʻaki ʻa e ngaahi fika falemahaki koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻoku felaveʻi mo hono ngaahi fatongia pe mafai;

(e) kapau ko e taha ko ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni pea ʻoku ne tanaki pe ngaueʻaki ʻa e fika falemahaki, koe ha pe ‘e hoko, koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻaʻoku felaveʻimo e puleʻi ʻo e moʻui lelei, palani ʻo e moʻui lelei, fakatotolo ki he moʻui pe ngaahi ako ki he mafola ʻa ha mahaki; pe

(f) kapau ko e taha kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni pea ʻoku ne tanaki pe ngaueʻaki ʻa e fika falemahaki, ko e ha pe meʻa, koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻoku felaveʻi mo e NHIS, ʻi hono fakaʻuhinga ʻi he Konga 7 ‘o e Lao ni.

(3) Neongo ʻa e kupu 53(1) ‘o e Lao ni, pea fakatatau ki he ngaahi fakaʻatā mo e tuʻutuʻuni fakalahi, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, kuo pau ʻe ʻikai fakaha ʻe ha taha ʻoku ʻikai ko ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui pea ʻikai fakahoko fatongia ko ha fakafofonga ʻo ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui, ʻa e fika falemahaki tukukehe ʻi hano tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he lao.

(4) ʻOku ʻikai ngaueʻaki ʻa e kupu siʻi (2) mo e (3) ki ha –

(a) taha ‘oku ne tanaki, ngaue’aki pe fakaha ha fika falemahaki koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘o ha fakatonutonu fakalao;

(b) sino kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he kupu 49 (1) ‘o e Lao ni ke ne tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻa e fika falemahaki ʻi hono fakahoko hono ngaahi fatongia ʻi he kupu ko ia; pe

(c) sino ʻoku ne ngaueʻaki ʻa e fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia kuo tali ʻe he Minisita ʻi he kupu 51(8) ‘o e Lao ni pea ʻoku ne tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻa e fika falemahaki ʻi hono fakahoko hono ngaahi fatongia ʻi he kupu 51 mo e 52 ‘o e Lao ni.

(5) Fakatatau kihe ngaahi tuʻutuʻunifakalahi, kapauʻokuʻi ai, ʻa ia kuotuʻutuʻuni

‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, ʻe ngofua ke tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻoku ne fakahoko ʻa e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ki ha taha, ʻa e fika falemahaki ʻo e taha ko ia ʻi he loto ki ai ʻa e taha ko ia koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke fakahaaʻi totonu ʻa e ngaahi lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo e taha ko ia, fakapapauʻi hono fakahaaʻi pe fakafehokotaki ʻenau ngaahi lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, ʻo tatau kapau ʻoku ʻikai fakahoko ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha ngaue ki he moʻui.

Kupu 37 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 34 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(6) Fakatatau kihe ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni fakalahi, kapau ʻokuʻi ai, ʻa ia kuotuʻutuʻuni

‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, ʻe ngofua ke ngaueʻaki ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia kuo ne tanaki ha fika falemahaki koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻoku felaveʻi mo hono fakahoko ʻo ha ngaue ki he moʻui ki ha taha, ʻa e fika falemahaki koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke fakahaaʻi totonu ʻa e ngaahi lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa e taha ko ia, fakamoʻoniʻi honau fakahaaʻi pe fakafehokotaki ʻenau ngaahi lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui.

(7) ʻE ʻikai ha meʻa ʻi he kupu siʻi (5) pe (6) te ne fakangatangata ʻa e mafai ʻo ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke ne tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ha fika falemahaki kapau kuo fakangofua kehe ia pe tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Lao ni.

#### 37 Ngaahi totongi ki he fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui

(1) ‘E ngofua ke hilifaki ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha totongi ki ha taha ki hono tanaki pe ngaueʻaki ʻoe fakamatala taautaha ki hemoʻui tukukehe ʻi hono tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(2) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai hilifaki ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ki ha taha ha ngaahi totongi ʻihonofakaha ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui,ʻaiaʻokulaka hake ʻi he mahuʻinga kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni pe ko e mahuʻinga ʻo e tanaki moʻua fakapotopoto, kapau ʻoku ʻikai tuʻutuʻuni ha mahuʻinga ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

VAHE 2 – TANAKI

#### 38 Tanaki ʻikai fakahangatonu

(1) ʻE ngofua ke tanaki ʻikai fakahangatonu ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha kapau –

(a) ʻoku fakangofua ʻe he taha ko ia ʻa hono fakahoko ʻo e tanaki ʻo ʻikai fakahangatonu;

(b) ko e fakamatala ke tanaki ʻoku fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ki hono fakahoko ʻoengauetokangaekinaʻoemoʻuipetokoniʻihonofakahokoʻoengaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ki he taha ko ia pea ʻoku ʻikai malava fakapotopoto ke tanaki, fakahangatonu mei he taha ko ia ha –

(i) fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻe lava fakapotopoto ke fakafalala ki ai ʻoku kakato mo tonu koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻa ia ʻe fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ki ai ʻa e fakamatala; pe

(ii) fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he taimi totonu;

(c) ʻoku tanaki ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e fakamatala koeʻuhi ko ha taumuʻa ʻoku felaveʻi mo e – Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 39 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 35

(i) fakatotoloʻi ʻo ha maumauʻi ʻo ha aleapau pe ha maumau lao pe ha maumauʻi ʻo e ngaahi lao,

(ii) fakahoko ʻo ha fakatonutonu fakalao pe ko ha fakatonutonu fakalao ʻe ala fakahoko, pe

(iii) fatongia fakalao ʻo e tauhi fakamatala;

(d) ʻoku tanaki ʻe he tauhi fakamatala mei he taha ko ia ʻa ia ʻoku ʻikai ko ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa hono fakahoko ʻo ha fakatotolo ʻoku fakahoko ʻo fakatatau mo e kupu 41(3) pe ha fakatotolo ʻa ia kuo tali ʻe he poate puleʻi ʻo e fakatotolo ʻi he kupu 48, tukukehe kapau ʻoku tapui ʻa e taha ko ia ʻe ha Lao mei hono fakaha ʻo e fakamatala ki he tauhi fakamatala;

(e) ko e tauhi fakamatala ko ha sino ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ʻoku lave ki ai ʻi he kupu 49(1) ‘o e Lao pea ko e tauhi fakamatala ʻoku ne tanaki ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui mei ha taha ʻa ia ʻoku ʻikai ko ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ʻo e kupu siʻi ko ia;

(f) ʻoku tanaki ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e fakamatala mei ha taha ʻa ia ʻoku fakangofua pe kuo tuʻutuʻuni ʻe ha Lao pe ha talite, aleapau pe ha fokotuʻutuʻu kuo fakahoko ʻi ha Lao ke ne fakaha ia ki he tauhi fakamatala; pe

(g) fakatataukihengaahituʻutuʻunimoengaahifakangatangata,kapauʻoku ʻi ai, ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, ʻoku fakangofua pe tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻe ha Lao pe ʻe ha talite, aleapau pe ha fokotuʻutuʻu kuo fakahoko ʻi ha Lao ke ne tanaki ʻa e fakamatala ʻo ʻikai fakahangatonu.

(2) ʻE ngofua ke tanaki ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha fakangatonu mei he taha ko ia, ʻo tatau ai pe kapau ʻoku ʻikai lava ʻa e taha ko ia ke ne fakangofua, kapau ko hono tanaki ʻoku fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ki hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui pea ʻe ʻikai lava fakapotopoto ke maʻu ʻa e fakangofua ʻi he taimi totonu.

#### 39 Taumuʻa ‘o e lekooti

Kuo pau ke lekooti ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku ne tanaki ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, ʻo kehe ange mei he taumuʻa ke fakahoko ʻa e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui pe tokoni ʻi hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui, ʻa e taumuʻa ʻa ia ʻoku tanaki ai ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e fakamatala.

#### 40 Tanaki ʻo e fakamatala faʻunga tupuʻanga

Kuo pau ke tauhi ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala ʻo e sivi faʻunga tupuʻanga ʻa ia ʻoku felaveʻi mo ha taha ʻi ha lekooti mavahe mei he toenga ʻo e lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa e taha ko ia.

Kupu 41 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 36 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to VAHE 3 – NGAUEʻAKI

#### 41 Ngaueʻaki ʻoku fakangofua

(1) ʻE ngofua ke toki ngaueʻaki pe ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha –

(a) koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa naʻe tanaki pe faʻu ʻaki ʻa e fakamatala pea ki he kotoa ʻo e ngaahi fatongia ʻoku fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ki hono fakahoko ʻo e taumuʻa ko ia, kae ʻikai kapau naʻe tanaki ʻa e fakamatala ʻaki ʻa e fakangofua ʻa e taha ko ia pe ʻi he kupu 38(1)(b) ‘o e Lao ni pea naʻe tuʻutuʻuni fakamahino ʻe he taha ko ia ke taʻofi;

(b) koeʻuhi ko ha tuamuʻa ʻa ia ʻoku fakangofua pe tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Lao ni pe ha Lao kehe ha taha ke ne fakaha ia ki he tauhi fakamatala;

(c) ki hono palani pe fakahoko ʻo e ngaahi polokalama pe ngaue ʻoku fakahoko ʻe he tauhi fakamatala pe ʻoku fakapaʻanga kakato pe fakakonga ʻe he tauhi fakamatala, vahevahe ki ha taha ʻo kinautolu ʻa e ngaahi naunau, sivi pe vakaiʻi ha taha ʻo kinautolu pe ko hono fakatotoloʻi, vakaiʻi pe taʻofi ʻa e kākā ʻi he paʻanga pe ko hono maʻu ʻo ʻikai hafakamafai ʻoe ngaahi ngaue pemonuʻiaʻokufelaveʻimo hataha ʻo kinautolu;

(d) koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke puleʻi ʻa e fakatamaki, puleʻi ʻo e fehalaaki pe koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ʻo ha ngaahi ngaue ke fakalakalaka pe tauhi ʻa e tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina pe ke fakalakalaka pe tauhi ʻa e tuʻunga ʻo ha ngaahi polokalama pe ha sevesi felaveʻi ʻa e tauhi fakamatala;

(e) ki hono akoʻi ʻo e kau fakafofonga ke nau fakahoko ʻa e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui;

(f) ʻi ha natula ʻoku faitatau mo e Konga 2, koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke fakaʻauha ʻa e fakamatala pe liliu ʻa e fakamatala ke lava ʻo fakapuliki ʻa e fakahaaʻi ʻo e taha;

(g) koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke kole ʻa e fakangofua ʻa e taha ko ia, pe ko e fakangofua ʻa e taha fetongi ʻo e taha faituʻutuʻuni ʻo e taha ko ia, ʻoku fakangatangata ki he hingoa mo e fakamatala ʻo e fetuʻutaki ʻo e fetongi ʻo e taha faituʻutuʻuni ʻo e taha ko ia, ʻi he taimi ʻoku ngaueʻaki ai ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he taumuʻa ko ʻeni, kapau ʻe kaunga ki ai;

(h) koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ‘o ha fakatonutonu fakalao pe ha fakatonutonu fakalao ‘oku ‘amanaki fakahoko ‘a ia ko e tauhi fakamatala pe fakafofonga pe ha fakafofonga kimuʻa ʻo e tauhi fakamatala, pe ʻoku ʻamanaki,kohafaʻahipefakamoʻoniia,kapauʻefelaveʻi ʻaefakamatala ki hepeko hameʻa ʻe fehuʻiaʻihefakatonutonufakalao pefakatonutonu fakalao ʻoku ʻamanaki fakahoko;

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 42 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 37

(i) koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke maʻu ʻa e totongi pe ngaueʻi, sivi, fakamoʻoniʻi pe totongi fakafoki ʻa e ngaahi ‘eke ʻo e totongi ki hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui pe ngaahi koloa pe ngaue felaveʻi mo ia;

(j) koeʻuhi ko ha fakatotolo ʻoku fakahoko ʻe he tauhi fakamatala, fakatatau ki he kupu siʻi (3); pe

(k) fakatataukihengaahituʻutuʻunimoengaahifakangatangata,kapauʻoku ʻi ai, ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, kapau kuo fakangofua pe tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he lao pe ha talite, aleapau pe fokotuʻutuʻu kuo faʻu ʻi ha Lao.

(2) Kapau ʻoku fakamafai ʻe he kupu siʻi (1) ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke ne ngaueʻaki ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui koeʻuhi ko ha taumuʻa, ʻe ngofua ke ʻoatu ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e fakamatala ki ha fakafofonga ʻo e tauhi fakamatala ʻa ia te ne ala ngaueʻaki ia koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ko ia ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e tauhi fakamatala.

(3) ʻE ngofua ke ngaueʻaki ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu siʻi (1)(j), ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha kapau pe ʻoku teuteuʻi ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ha fokotuʻutuʻu ki he fakatotolo pea ʻoku tali ia ʻe ha poate puleʻi ʻo e fakatotolo pea koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ko ia ke ngaueʻaki ʻa e kupu 48(2) ki he (4) mo e kupu 48(6)(a) ki he (f) ‘o e Lao ni ki hono ngaueʻaki ʻo hange pe ia ha fakaha.

VAHE 4 – FAKAHA

#### 42 Fakaha ʻoku felaveʻi mo hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e

moʻui

(1) ʻE ngofua ke fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha –

(a) ki ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu 3(1)(a),(b), (c) pe (d)ʻo e ʻuhingaʻi lea ʻo e “tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui”, kapau ʻoku fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ʻa e fakaha ki hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui pea ʻoku ʻikai lava fakapotopoto ke maʻu ʻa efakangofua ʻae taha ko iaʻi hataimi feʻunga, ka ʻe ʻikai fakaha kapau kuo tuʻutuʻuni mahino ʻe he taha ko ia ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ke ʻoua ʻe fakahoko ʻa e fakaha;

(b) ke lava ʻa e Minisita pe ko ha tauhi fakamatala ʻe taha ki he moʻui, ke fakapapauʻi pe ʻoatu hono fakapaʻanga pe ko e totongi ki he tauhi fakamatala ki hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui; pe

(c) koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke fetuʻutaki ki ha famili, kaungameʻa pe ha taha te ne ala fetongi ʻa e taha faituʻutuʻuni maʻae taha ko ia, kapau ʻoku lavea, faingataʻaʻia fakaesino pe puke ʻa e taha ko ia pea ʻikai lava ʻo ʻoatu fakahangatonu ʻe ne fakangofua.

Kupu 43 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 38 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(2) Kapau ʻe fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ʻi he kupu 3(1)(a) pea kapau ʻoku taʻofi ʻe ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e taha ko ia ʻa ia naʻe faʻu ʻi he kupu ko ia hono fakaha ʻe tauhi fakamatala ʻa e kotoa ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku pehe ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻoku fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ke ne fakaha koeʻuhi ko hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui pe tokoni ʻi hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ki he taha ko ia, kuo pau ke fakaha ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e taha ko ia ʻoku ne fakahoko ki ai ʻa fakaha ʻo fekauʻaki mo e moʻoniʻi meʻa ko ia.

(3) ʻE ngofua ke fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia ko ha feituʻu pau ʻoku ne fakahoko ʻae ngauetokangaekinaʻo emoʻui, ki ha tahaʻa efakamatala taautaha ko ʻeni ki he moʻui ʻo felaveʻi mo ha taha ʻa ia ko ha taha mahaki pe ko ha taha nofo ʻi he feituʻu pau, kapau ʻoku fakaʻatā ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he taha ko ia ʻa e faingamalie ko ia, ʻi he fuofua faingamalie fakapotopoto hili ʻa e hū ki he feituʻu, ke ʻoua te ne tali ʻa e ngaahi fakaha ko ia pea kapau ʻe ʻikai fakahoko pehe ʻe he taha ko ia –

(a) ko e taha ko ia ko ha taha mahaki pe ʻoku nofo ʻi he feituʻu pau ko ia;

(b) ko e tuʻunga moʻui lelei fakalukufua ʻa e taha ko ia ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ko e fakatuʻutamaki ‘aupito, ‘ikai fakafiemalie pe fakafiemalie ʻi ha ngaahi lea tatau mo ia; pe

(c) ko e feituʻu ‘oku ‘i ai ʻa e taha ko ia ʻi he feituʻu pau ko ia.

(4) ʻE ngofua ke fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ʻa ia kuo pekia, pe kuo mahaloʻi fakapotopoto kuo ne pekia –

(a) koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke fakahaaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia;

(b) koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke fakaha ki ha taha ʻa ia ʻoku fakapotopoto ke fakaha ʻi he ngaahi tukunga –

(i) kuo pekia pe ʻoku mahaloʻi fakapotopoto kuo ne pekia, mo e

(ii) ʻo e pekia, ʻi he meʻa ʻoku totonu; pe

(c) ki he mali, tokoua pe tamasiʻi ʻa e taha ko ia kapau ʻoku fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ʻe kinautolu ʻoku nau maʻu ʻa e fakamatala ke fakahoko ʻaki ʻa e ngaahi faituʻutuʻuni fekauʻaki mo ʻenau ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ʻakinautolu pe, pe ko e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ʻenau fanau pe ko honau fanga tokoua.

#### 43 Fakaha koeʻuhi ko e moʻui pe ha ngaahi polokalama kehe

(1) Fakatatau ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni mo e ngaahi fakangatangata, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, ʻe ngofua ke fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha – Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 43 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 39

(a) koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke fakapapauʻi pe fakamoʻoniʻi ʻa e taau ʻa e taha ko ia ke maʻu ʻa e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui pe ha ngaahi koloa, ngaue pe monuʻia felaveʻi mo ia ʻi ha Lao pea ʻoku fakapaʻanga kakato pe fakakonga ia ʻe he Puleʻanga pe ʻe ha kautaha tokoni;

(b) ki hataha ʻaia kuotuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻunipea ʻoku ne faipau pe tauhi ha lesisita ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui koeʻuhi ko e ngaahitaumuʻakefakahokopefakalakalakaʻahonofakahokoʻoengaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui pe ko ia ʻoku felaveʻi mo hono tauhi pe foaki ʻo e ngaahi konga ʻo e sino pe ngaahi meʻa ʻo e sino ʻi he the Lao ki he Fokotuʻu Foʻimata mo e Kanomate [Vahe 12.02]; pe

(c) kapau –

(i) ko e fakaha ʻoku fakahokoki ha tauhi fakamatala ʻe taha ʻa ia ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu 3(1)(a), (b), (c) pe (d) ʻo e ʻuhingaʻi lea ʻo e “tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui”;

(ii) ko e taha ʻoku felaveʻi mo e fakamatala ko ha taha ʻa ia ʻoku fakahoko fakatouʻosi ki ai ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻoku ne fakaha mo e tauhi fakamatala ʻoku ne maʻu ʻa e fakamatala, ʻa e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui pe tokoniʻi ʻi hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui pe naʻa na fakahoko ki ai ʻa e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui pe tokoniʻi ʻi hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui; pea

(iii) ko e fakaha koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ʻo e ngaahi ngaue ke fakalelei pe tauhi ʻa e tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻoku fakahoko ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻoku ne maʻu ki he taha ʻa ia ʻoku felaveʻi mo e fakamatala pe niʻihiʻoku fakahoko kia nautolu ʻa e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui tatau.

(2) ʻE ngofua ke fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha –

(a) ki ha ʻofisa moʻui pe ha ‘ofisa fakamafai kuo fokotuʻu ‘i he Lao ki he Moʻui Lelei ‘a e Kakai [Vahe 12.15]:

(b) ʻi heʻene fekauʻaki mo e huhu maluʻi, ki ha taha fakahoko huhu maluʻi ʻi he Lao ki he Faitoʻo Tafa [Vahe 12.19]; pe

(c) ha maʻu mafai ki he moʻui ʻa e kakai ʻa ia ʻoku tatau mo e niʻihi ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he palakalafi (a) pea kuo fokotuʻu ʻi he ngaahi lao ʻo ha maʻu mafai ʻe taha, kapau kuo fakahoko ʻa e fakaha ki ha taumuʻa ʻa ia ʻoku matuʻaki tatau mo ha taumuʻa ʻo e Lao ki he Moʻui Lelei ʻa e Kakai [Vahe 12.15]pe ko e Lao ki he Faitoʻo Tafa [Vahe 12.19].

(3) ʻOku fakamafai ha taha ʻa ia ʻoku ʻikai ko ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke ne tanaki ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻe ngofua ke fakaha ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ki he taha ko ia ʻi he kupu siʻi (1)(c).

Kupu 44 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 40 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

#### 44 Fakaha ʻoku felaveʻi mo e ngaahi fakatuʻutamaki

(1) ʻE ngofua ke fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha kapau ʻoku tui ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ʻi ha ngaahi ʻuhinga fakapotopoto ʻoku ʻaonga ʻa e fakaha koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke taʻofi pe fakasiʻisiʻi ha fakatuʻutamaki lahi ʻi ha hoko ha fakalavea lahi ki ha taha pe ha falukunga kakai.

(2) ʻE ngofua ke fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ki he Komisiona Pilisone pe Komisiona Polisi, ko e hā pe ‘e hoko, ʻa ia ʻoku tauhi fakalao ai ʻa e taha ko ia, ki he ʻofisa pule ʻo ha fale fakalelei ʻatamai ‘o fakatatau ki he Lao ki he Fakalelei ʻAtamai [Vahe 12.10] ʻa ia ʻoku tauhi fakalao ai ʻa e taha ko ia pe ki ha ʻofisa tali fakatuʻutamaki fakafaitoʻo koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e kupu 187 ʻo e Lao ki he Moʻui ʻa e Kakai, koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi (3).
