(3) ʻE ngofua ke fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ʻi he kupu siʻi (2) ke tokoni ki he Pilisone Tonga, Polisi Tonga, pe ha fale ʻi he faituʻutuʻuni fekauʻaki mo e –

(a) ngaahi fokotuʻutuʻu ki hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ki he taha ko ia; pe

(b) ʻave ʻo e taha ko ia ke tauhi, tauhi fakamalohi, tukuange, tukuange ʻi he ngaahi makatuʻunga, fakaʻatā pe fakaʻatā ʻi he ngaahi makatuʻunga ʻi he Lao ki he Fakalelei ʻAtamai [Vahe 12.10] pe ʻi he kupu 187 ʻo e Lao ki he Moʻui ʻa e Kakai [Vahe 12.15].

#### 45 Fakaha koeʻuhi ko ha ngaahi fakatonutonu fakalao

(1) ʻE ngofua ke fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha –

(a) fakatatau ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni mo e fakangatangata, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ʻo ha fakatonutonu fakalao pe ha fakatonutonu fakalao ʻoku ʻamanaki fakahoko ʻa ia ko e tauhi fakamatala pe fakafofonga pe ha fakafofonga kimuʻa ʻo e tauhi fakamatala, pe ʻoku ʻamanaki, ko ha faʻahi pe fakamoʻoni ia, kapau ʻe felaveʻi ʻa e fakamatala ki he pe ko ha meʻa ʻe fehuʻia ʻi he fakatonutonu fakalao pe fakatonutonu fakalao ʻoku ʻamanaki fakahoko;

(b) ki ha tauhi fakalao kuo fokotuʻu ʻo e taha ko ia ʻi he kupu 8 ʻo e Lao ki hono Tauhia ʻo e Fanau, koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke fokotuʻu ʻa e taha ko ia ki ai;

(c) ki he fakafofonga lao ʻi he kupu 18 ʻo e Lao ki hono Tauhia ʻo e Fanau koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke fokotuʻu ʻe he Fakamaauʻanga ʻa e taha ko ia ke Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 46 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 41 tokoni ki he Fakamaauʻanga pe fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia ʻi ha fakatonutonu fakalao ʻi he Lao ko ia; pe

(d) koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke faipau ki ha –

(i) tohi fekau, tuʻutuʻuni pe ha tuʻutuʻuni tatau mo ia kuo tuku atu ʻi ha fakatonutonu fakalao ʻe ha taha ʻoku ʻi ai hano mafai ke tuʻutuʻuniʻi ʻa hono ʻoatu ʻo ha fakamatala, pe

(ii) tuʻutuʻunifakangaueʻokufelaveʻimohonoʻoatuʻohafakamatala ʻi ha fakatonutonu fakalao.

(2) ʻE ngofua ke fakaha ʻe ha fakafofonga pe ha fakafofonga kimuʻa ʻa ia kuo ne maʻu ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) pe ʻi he kupu 41(2) koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo ha fakatonutonu fakalao pe ha fakatonutonu fakalao ʻoku ʻamanaki fakahoko, ʻa e fakamatala ki he faleʻi fakapalofesinale ʻa e fakafofonga pe fakafofonga kimuʻa koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa hono ʻoatu ʻo e faleʻi pe fakamatala ki he fakafofonga pe fakafofonga kimuʻa, kapau ko e taha faleʻi ko ia ʻoku ʻi he malumalu ʻo e fatongia fakapalofesinale ʻo e tauhi ke fakapulipuli.

#### 46 Fakaha ki he hoko fakalao

(1) ʻI he kupu ni, ʻoku ʻuhinga ʻa e “taha ʻe ngali ko e hoko fakalao” ki ha taha ʻe ngali ko e hoko fakalao pe ko ha hoko fakalao ʻa ia ko ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui pe ʻe hoko ko ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui kapau te ne hoko ko e hoko fakalao.

(2) ʻE ngofua ke fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ki ha taha ʻe ngali ko e hoko fakalao ʻo e tauhi fakamatala,koeʻuhikoetaumuʻakefakaʻatāʻaetahaʻengalikoehokofakalao ke ne sivi mo vakaiʻi ʻa e ngaahi ngaue ʻa e tauhi fakamatala, kapau ʻe fuofua kau ʻa e taha ʻe ngali ko e hoko fakalao ki ha aleapau mo e tauhi fakamatala ke tauhi ke fakapulipuli mo malu ʻo e fakamatala pea ʻoua ʻe tauhi ha fakamatala ʻo loloa ange ʻi he taimi ʻoku fie maʻu ai koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ʻo e sivi pe vakaiʻi.

(3) ʻE ngofua ke ʻave ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e ngaahi lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo e taha ko ia ki he taha ʻe hoko fakalao ʻo e tauhi fakamatala kapau ʻe fakahoko ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ha ngaahi feinga fakapotopoto ke ʻoatu ha fanongonongo ki he taha ko ia kimuʻa pea ʻave ʻa e ngaahi lekooti pe, kapau ʻe ʻikai lava fakapotopoto ia, ʻi he vave taha ʻe ala lava hili hono ʻave ʻa e ngaahi lekooti.

(4) Fakatatau ki he loto ki ai ʻa e taha ʻa ia te ne tali hono ʻave ʻo e fakamatala, ʻe ngofua ki ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke ne ʻave ha ngaahi lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ʻi he ngaahi tukunga ko ia ʻe ala tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, ki ha taha kuo tuʻutuʻuni ʻa ia ko hono fatongia ʻoku kau ki ai hono tanaki mo fakatolonga ʻa e ngaahi lekooti Kupu 47 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 42 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to ʻoku mahuʻinga fakahisitolia, kapau kuo fakahoko ʻa e fakaha koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ʻo e fatongia ko ia.

#### 47 Ngaahi fakaha ʻoku felaveʻi mo e Lao ni pe ha ngaahi Lao kehe

(1) ‘E ngofua ke fakaha ‘e ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha –

(a) koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke fakapapauʻi, sivi pe fakapapauʻi ʻa e ivi malava ʻo e taha ko ia ʻi he Fakamaauʻanga;

(b) ki ha Poate ‘o fakatatau ki he Lao ki he Kau Toketa, Kau Ngaue Faitoʻo ki he Nifo mo e Kau Ngaue ki he Moʻui 2021, ko e Lao ki he Kau Neesi, Kau Neesi Fakafaitoʻo mo e Kau Maʻuli Fakapolofesinale 2021 pe ko e Lao ki he Huʻivai [Vahe 12.14] koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ki hono puleʻi pe fakahoko ʻa e Lao;

(c) fakatatau ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni mo e fakangatangata, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, ki ha taha ʻoku ne fakahoko ha sivi,fakatotolope hafounga ngaue tatau ʻaia kuo fakamafai ʻe hatohi fekau ʻi he Lao ni pe ha Lao kehe koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke faipau ki he tohi fekau pe ko e taumuʻa ke fakahoko ʻa e sivi, fakatotolo pe ha founga ngaue tatau mo ia; pe

(d) fakatatau ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni mo e fakangatangata, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni, ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni kapau ʻoku fakangofua pe tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he lao pe ʻe ha talite, aleapau pe ha fokotuʻutuʻu kuo faʻu ʻi he Lao.

(2) Koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e kupu siʻi (1)(d) pea fakatatau ki he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, kapau ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni pau ʻe ha Lao pe ha Tuʻutuʻuni kuo faʻu ʻi he tahaʻoe ngaahi Laokoia ʻoku fakaʻatāʻa efakamatala,ʻi hengaahi tukunga kuo fakaha, mei ha tuʻutuʻuni ke tauhi ke fakapulipuli pe fakapulipuli, kuo pau ke lau kuofakangofuaʻe hekupu koia honofakaha ʻo efakamatalaʻi he ngaahi tukunga kuo fakaha.

#### 48 Fakaha koeʻuhi ko ha fakatotolo

(1) ʻE ngofua ke fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ki ha taha fakatotolo kapau ko e taha fakatotolo –

(a) ʻoku ne fakahū atu ki he tauhi fakamatala –

(i) ha tohi kole ʻi ha tohi;

(ii) ha fokotuʻutuʻu ki he fakatotolo ʻa ia ʻoku ne fakakakato ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻo e kupu siʻi (2), mo

(iii) ha tatau ʻo e tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e poate puleʻi ʻo e fakatotolo ʻa ia ʻoku ne tali ʻa e fokotuʻutuʻu ki he fakatotolo; pea Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 48 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 43

(b) ʻoku ne kau ki he aleapau ʻa ia ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he kupu siʻi (5).

(2) Kuo pau ko e fokotuʻutuʻu ki he fakatotolo ʻe fakahoko ia ʻi ha tohi pea fakaha ʻi ai ʻa e –

(a) fekauʻaki ʻa e taha takitaha ʻoku kau ʻi he fakatotolo;

(b) natula mo e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e fakatotolo mo e lelei ʻo e fakatotolo ki he kakai pe fakasaienisi ʻa ia ʻoku ʻamanaki ki ai ʻa e taha fakatotolo; mo e

(c) kotoa ʻo e ngaahi meʻa kehe ʻa ia ʻe ala tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni ʻoku felaveʻi mo e fakatotolo.

(3) Kuo pau ke vakaiʻi ʻe he poate puleʻi ʻo e fakatotolo ʻi heʻene fakakaukauʻi pe ʻetalihafokotuʻutuʻukihefakatotolokuofakahūatukiai ʻehetahafakatotolo, ʻa e ngaahi meʻa ʻa ia ʻoku ne pehe ʻoku mahuʻinga, kau ki ai –

(a) pe ʻe malava fakapotopoto ʻo aʻusia ʻa e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e fakatotolo ʻo ʻikai ngaueʻaki ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku ʻamanaki ke fakaha;

(b) pe, ʻi he taimi ʻe fakahoko ai ʻa e fakatotolo, ʻe ʻi ai ha ngaahi maluʻi feʻunga ke ne maluʻi ʻa e totonu ʻa e kakai ki he ‘enaumeʻa fakafoʻituitui ʻa ia ko ʻenau fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku fakaha mo tauhi ʻa e fakapulipuli ʻo e fakamatala;

(c) ʻa e lelei fakalukufua ʻa e kakai ʻi hono fakahoko ʻa e fakatotolo mo e lelei fakalukufua ʻa e kakai ʻi hono maluʻi ʻo e totonu ʻa e kakai ki he

‘enau meʻa fakafoʻituitui ʻa ia ko ʻenau fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku fakaha; mo e

(d) pe ʻe ʻikai malava ʻa hono maʻu ʻa e fakangofua ʻa e kakai ʻa ia ko ʻenau fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku fakaha.

(4) Hili hono toe vakaiʻi ha fokotuʻutuʻu ki he fakatotolo ʻa ia kuo fakahū atu ki ai ʻe ha taha fakatotolo, kuo pau ke ʻoatu ʻe he poate puleʻi ʻo e fakatotolo ki he taha fakatotolo ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he tohi, fakataha mo e ngaahi ʻuhinga, ʻo fakaha ʻi ai pe kuo tali ʻe he poate ʻa e palani, pea ʻoku fakafalala ʻa e fakangofua ʻi ha ngaahi makatuʻunga, ʻa ia kuo pau ke fakamahino pau ʻi he tuʻutuʻuni.

(5) Kuo pau ke kau ʻa e taha fakatotolo ki ha aleapau mo e tauhi fakamatala kimuʻa pea fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ki ha taha fakatotolo ʻi he kupu siʻi (1), ʻa ia ʻe loto ai ʻa e taha fakatotolo ke faipau ki he ngaahi makatuʻunga mo e fakangatangata, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻa ia ʻe hilifaki ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻo felaveʻi mo hono ngaueʻaki, maluʻi, fakaha, fakafoki pe fakaʻauha ʻo e fakamatala.

(6) Ka ʻi ai ha taha fakatotolo kuo ne maʻu ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha mei he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) kuo pau – Kupu 48 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 44 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(a) ke ne faipau ki he ngaahi makatuʻunga, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻa ia kuo fakamahino pau ʻe he poate puleʻi ʻo e fakatotolo ʻo fekauʻaki mo e fokotuʻutuʻu ki he fakatotolo;

(b) ke ne ngaueʻaki ʻa e fakamatala ki he ngaahi ʻuhinga pe ʻa ia kuo fakaha ʻi he fokotuʻutuʻu ki he fakatotolo ʻi hono tali ʻe he poate puleʻi ʻo e fakatotolo;

(c) ʻe ʻikai ke ne pulusi ʻa e fakamatala ʻi ha fotunga ʻa ia ʻe lava fakapotopoto ha taha ʻo fakapapauʻi ʻa e fakahaaʻi ʻo e taha ko ia;

(d) neongo ʻa e kupu 53(1), ʻe ʻikai ke ne fakaha ʻa e fakamatala tukukehe ʻi hono tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he lao pea fakatatau ki he ngaahi fakaʻatā mo ha ngaahituʻutuʻunimakehe,kapauʻeʻiai,ʻaiakuotuʻutuʻuni‘eheNgaahi Tuʻutuʻuni;

(e) ʻe ʻikai ke ne fetuʻutaki pe feinga ke fetuʻutaki mo e taha ko ia, fakahangatonu pe ʻikai fakahangatonu, tukukehe ʻi hano tomuʻa maʻu ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e fakangofua ʻa e taha ko ia ki ha fetuʻutaki ange;

(f) ke ne fakaha ʻi he vave taha ki he tauhi fakamatala ʻi ha tohi kapau ʻoku ʻilo ʻe he taha fakatotolo hano maumauʻi ʻo e kupu siʻi ni pe ko e aleapau kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi (5); pea

(g) ke faipau ki he aleapau kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi (5).

(7) Neongo ha Lao kehe ʻoku ne fakangofua ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke ne fakaha ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ki ha taha fakatotolo koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa hono fakahoko ʻo e fakatotolo, ʻoku ngaueʻaki ʻa e kupu ni ki hono fakaha ʻo hange pe ko hano fakaha koeʻuhi ko e fakatotolo ʻi he kupu ni tukukehe ka tuʻutuʻuni kehe ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(8) Fakatatau ki he kupu siʻi (9), ʻe ngofua ke fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ki ha taha fakatotolo pe te ne ngaueʻaki ʻa e fakamatala ke fakahoko ha fakatotolo kapau –

(a) ʻoku kau ʻi he fakatotolo ʻa hono ngaueʻaki ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ko hono tupuʻanga kotoa pe ko hano konga ʻi tuʻa ʻi Tonga;

(b) kuo maʻu ʻa e fakangofua kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni ki he fakatotolo mei ha sino ʻi tuʻa ʻi Tonga ʻa ia ko hono fatongia ʻa hono fakangofua ʻo e fakatotolo; mo e

(c) kuo fakakakato ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuuni ko ia ʻe ngofua ke tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(9) ʻOku ʻikai ngaueʻaki ʻa e kupu siʻi (1) ki he (4) mo e kupu siʻi (6)(a) mo e (b) ki ha fakaha pe ngaueʻaki kuo fakahoko ʻi he kupu siʻi ni.

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 49 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 45

#### 49 Fakaha ʻo e fakamatala koeʻuhi ko hono palani mo puleʻi ʻo e ngaue ki

he moʻui

(1) ʻE ngofua ke fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ki ha sino ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke ʻanalaiso pe fakatahatahaʻi ʻa e fakamatala fakasitesitika ʻo fekauʻaki mo hono puleʻi, sivi pe tokangaekina ‘o e, ko hono vahevahe ‘o e ngaahi naunau ki he, pe fakahoko ʻo e palani ki he kotoa pe ha konga ʻo e ngaue ki he moʻui, kau ki ai hono ʻoatu ʻo e ngaahi ngaue, kapau ʻoku fakakakato ʻe he sino ko ia ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he kupu siʻi (3).

(2) ʻOku ʻikai ngaueʻaki ʻa e kupu siʻi (1) ki he –

(a) ngaahi fakamatala fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo ha taha ʻa ia ʻoku lekooti ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui pea ʻoku hiki ʻi ai ʻa e kakano ʻo ha ngaahi fepotalanoaʻaki lolotonga ha taimi faleʻi fakafoʻituitui, pe ko ha taimi faleʻi fakafoʻituitui ʻa ha kulupu, fakatahataha pe fakafamili; pe

(b) fakamatala ʻa ia ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he ngaahi tukunga ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(3) ʻE ngofua ke fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ki ha sino ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) kapau ‘oku ʻi ai ha ngaahi ngaue mo e founga ngaue ʻa e sino ko ia ke maluʻi ʻa e totonu ʻa e kakai ki he ‘enau meʻa fakafoʻituitui ʻa ia ʻoku ne maʻu ʻenau fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui mo tauhi ʻaki ʻa e fakapulipuli ʻo e fakamatala.

(4) ʻOku fakamafai ha sino ʻa ia ʻoku ʻikai ko ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke ne tanaki ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻe ngofua ke fakaha ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ki he sino ko ia ʻi he kupu siʻi (1).

(5) Fakatatau ki he ngaahi fakaʻatā mo ha tuʻutuʻuni kuo tanaki atu, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni pea neongo ʻa e kupu 53(1), kuo pau ʻe ʻikai ngaueʻaki ʻe ha sino ʻoku ne maʻu ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) ʻa e fakamatala tukukehe ki he ngaahi taumuʻa ʻa ia naʻe tuʻunga ai ʻene maʻu ʻa e fakamatala pea kuo pau ʻe ʻikai ke ne fakaha ʻa e fakamatala tukukehe ʻi hano tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he lao.

#### 50 Ngaahi totongi ki hono tokangaekina ‘o e moʻui

(1) Kapau ʻe kolekiai ‘ehataha ‘aiakuohiki‘i he kupu siʻi(2),kuopau ke fakaha ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui, ki he taha ko ia kuo ne kole, ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui koeʻuhi ke fakapapauʻi, fakahoko ʻaki, puleʻi mo fakamoʻoniʻi ʻaki hono totongi pe ko hono fakapaʻanga kakato pe fakakonga ʻo e tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ʻe he taha ko ia, pe ko e ngaahi koloa naʻe ngaueʻaki ki hono tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ʻa ia naʻe fakapaʻanga kakato pe fakakonga ʻe he taha ko ia.

Kupu 51 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 46 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(2) Koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e kupu siʻi (1), ‘oku ‘uhinga ha taha –

(a) ki he Minisita;

(b) ki he Minisita ʻo ha Potungaue kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni; pe

(c) ko ha kautaha tokoni kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(3) ‘E ngofua ke fakaha ‘e ha taha kuo hiki ‘i he kupu siʻi (2), ha fakamatala kuo tanaki ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) ki ha taha pe koeʻuhi ko ha taumuʻa kuo fokotuʻu ʻi he kupu siʻi ko ia kapau ʻoku totonu hono fiemaʻu ia ki he taumuʻa ko ia.

(4) Kuo pau ‘e ‘ikai hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻe ha taha kuo hiki ʻi he kupu siʻi (2), ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia kuo ne kole ki ai ʻi he kupu siʻi

(1), kapau ʻe fakakakato ʻe ha fakamatala kehe ia ʻa e taumuʻa ‘o e hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo e fakamatala.

(5) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻe ha taha kuo hiki ʻi he kupu siʻi (2), ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia kuo ne kole ki ai ʻi he kupu siʻi

(1) ‘o lahi ange ʻi hono fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ke fakakakato ʻa e taumuʻa ‘a e hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha, ko e ha pe ʻe hoko.

#### 51 Fakaha ke ʻanalaiso ʻa e ngaue ki he moʻui

(1) Fakatatau ki he ngaahi fakangatangata, ‘a ia kuo tu’utu’uni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, kuo pau ke fakaha ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui, ʻi hano kole ʻe he Minisita, ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ki ha sino ʻoku ne ngaueʻaki ʻa e fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia kuo fakangofua ʻe he Minisita ʻi he kupu siʻi (3) koeʻuhi ko e ʻanalaiso ʻo fekauʻaki mo hono puleʻi, vakaiʻi pe siofi ʻo e, vahevahe ʻo e ngaahi naunau pe palani ki he kotoa pe ha konga ʻo e sisitemi fakafalemahaki, kau ki ai hono ʻoatu ʻo e ngaahi ngaue, kapau kuo fakakakato ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻo e kupu ni.

(2) ʻE ngofua ke fakamahino pau ʻe he Minisita ʻa e fotunga mo e founga mo e taimi ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni ki he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke ne fakaha ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu siʻi (1).

(3) ʻE ngofua ke fakangofua ʻe he Minisita ha sino ʻoku ne ngaueʻaki ʻa e fakamatala ki he moʻui koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo ha fakaha kuo fakahoko ʻi he kupu ni kapau –

(a) ko e ngaahi taumuʻa fakakautaha ʻa e sino ko ia ʻoku kau ki ai hono fakahoko ʻo e ʻanalaiso ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, fakafehokotaki ʻo e fakamatala mo ha fakamatala kehe mo hono fakapuliki ʻa e taha ʻoku ne ʻoʻona ʻa e fakamatala maʻae Minisita; pea

(b) ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e ngaahi founga mo e founga ngaue ʻa e sino ko ia ki hono maluʻi ʻo e totonu ki he meʻa fakafoʻituitui ʻa e niʻihi ʻa ia ko ʻenau fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku ne maʻu mo hono tauhi ʻo e fakapulipuli ʻo e fakamatala.

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 52 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 47

(4) Kapau ʻe toʻo ʻe he Minisita ʻa hono fakangofua ʻo ha sino ʻoku ne ngaueʻaki ʻa e fakamatala ki he moʻui, kuo pau ki he sino ko ia –

(a) ke ʻoua te ne toe ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui kuo fakaha ki ai ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) pe ha fakamatala kuo maʻu mei he fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ko ia; pea

(b) ke faipau ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni kuo tohi ʻe he Minisita ʻo fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he palakalafi (a).

(5) Kapau kuo ‘ikai toe ngaue ha sino, ʻoku ne ngaueʻaki ʻa e fakamatala ki he moʻui, kuo pau ke faipau ʻa e niʻihi ʻoku nau maʻu ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia naʻe maʻu ʻe he sino ko ia ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) pea tauhi ʻi he ʻene

‘ikai toe ngaue, ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e Minisita ʻo fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala ko ia.

(6) ʻE ngofua ke fakaha ʻe he Minisita ki he sino ʻoku ne ngaueʻaki ʻa e fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku ne maʻu ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) ha fakamatala taautaha kehe ki he moʻui koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ke ʻanalaiso mo fakafehokotaki ʻa ia ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Minisita kapau ʻoku kau ʻa e fakaha ʻi he fokotuʻu ʻa e Minisita.

(7) Kuo pau ki he sino ʻoku ne ngaueʻaki ʻa e fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku ne maʻu ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) pe (6) ke –

(a) muimui ʻi he ngaahi founga mo e founga ngaue kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi (3)(b);

(b) fakahoko ʻa e ʻanalaiso mo e fakafehokotaki mo e fakamatala kehe ʻa ia ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Minisita;

(c) fakapuliki ʻa e fakamatala;

(d) ʻoatu ʻa e ngaahi ola ʻo e ʻanalaiso mo e fakafehokotaki, ʻo ngaueʻaki pe ʻa e fakamatala kuo fakapuliki, ki he Minisita pe ki he niʻihi ʻoku fakangofua ʻe he Minisita;

(e) ʻoua te ne fakaha ʻa e fakamatala ki he Minisita pe ki he niʻihi ʻoku fakangofua ʻe he Minisita tukukehe ʻi ha fotunga ʻoku fakapuliki; pea

(f) fakatatatau ki he palakalafi (d) mo e (e), ʻoua ʻe fakaha ʻa e fakamatala ki ha niʻihi pe, ʻo tatau pe ʻoku ʻi ha fotunga kuo fakapuliki, pe ha fakamatala kuo maʻu mei he fakamatala ko ia.

(8) Kuo pau ke ‘aofangatuku ha tuʻutuʻuni‘e fakahoko ‘ehe Minisita ‘i he kupu ni.

#### 52 Fakaha ‘i he fakangofua ʻa e Minisita

(1) Ko ha sino ʻoku ne ngaueʻaki ʻa e fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia kuo fakaha ki ai ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu 51, kuo pau, ʻi hano kole ʻe he Minisita, ke ne fakaha ʻa e fakamatala ki he Minisita pe ha taha kehe kuo fakangofua ʻe he Minisita kapau ʻoku pehe ʻe Kupu 53 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 48 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to he Minisita ʻoku ʻi he lelei fakalukufua ke kole ʻa e fakaha ko ia pea kuo fakakakato ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻo e kupu ko ʻeni.

(2) Ko e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) ʻoku ʻikai –

(a) ko ha fakamatala ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ʻa ia ʻoku lekooti ʻe ha tauhi ʻo e fakamatala ki he moʻui pea mo ia ʻoku ne hiki ʻa e ngaahi kakano ʻo e ngaahi talanoa lolotonga ha taimi faleʻi fakafoʻituitui pe ha ngaahi faleʻi fakafoʻituitui fakakulupu, fakatahataha pe fakafamili; pe

(b) ko ha fakamatala kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

#### 53 Ngaahi fakangatangata ki he niʻihi ʻoku nau maʻu ʻa e fakamatala

(1) Tukukehe ʻi hano fakangofua pe tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he lao pea fakatatau ki he ngaahi fakaʻatā mo e tuʻutuʻuni makehe, ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ka ʻi ai ha taha ʻa ia ʻoku ʻikai ko ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui pea ko ia kuo fakaha ki ai ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, kuo pau ʻe ʻikai ke ne ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻa e fakamatala koeʻuhi ko ha taumuʻa kehe mei he –

(a) taumuʻa ʻa ia naʻa ne fakangofua ai ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke ne fakaha ʻa e fakamatala ʻi he Lao ni; pe

(b) taumuʻa hono fakahoko ha fatongia fokotuʻu ‘e he lao pe fakalao.

(2) Fakatatau ki he ngaahi fakaʻatā mo e tuʻutuʻuni makehe, kapau ‘oku ‘i ai, ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, ka ʻi ai ha taha ʻa ia ʻoku ʻikai ko ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui, pea ko ia kuo fakaha ki ai ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, kuo pau ʻe ʻikai ke ne ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻa e fakamatala ʻo lahi ange ʻi hono fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ke fakakakato ʻa e taumuʻa hono ngaueʻaki pe fakaha, ʻi ha meʻa pe ʻe hoko, tukukehe kapau ko hono ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ‘oku tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he lao.

(3) Tukukehe ʻi hono fakangofua pe tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he lao pea fakatatau ki he ngaahi fakaʻatā mo ha tuʻutuʻuni makehe, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, kapau ʻe fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala ki ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui kehe pea ko e fakamatala ko ia ko e fakamatala ʻoku fakahaaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia, ʻoku ʻi he tauhi pe pule ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ʻoku ne maʻu, kuo pau ko e tauhi fakamatala ʻoku ne maʻu ʻe ʻikai ke ne –

(a) ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻa e fakamatala ki ha taumuʻa kehe ange mei he –

(i) taumuʻaʻaianaʻefakangofua aiʻaetauhi fakamatalake nefakaha ʻa e fakamatala ʻi he Lao ni; pe

(ii) taumuʻa hono fakahoko ʻo ha fatongia fokotuʻu ‘e he lao pe fakalao; pe Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 54 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 49

(b) ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻa e fakamatala ʻo lahi ange ʻi hono fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ke fakakakato ʻa e taumuʻa ‘a hono ngaueʻaki pe fakaha, ko e ha pe ʻe hoko,

‘e ngaueʻaki ʻa e ngaahi fakangatangata kuo fokotuʻu ʻi he kupu siʻi ni ki ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻoku ne maʻu ʻa e fakamatala ‘oku fakahaaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi ko eni ʻo tatau ai pe kapau naʻe maʻu ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e fakamatala kimuʻa ʻi he ʻaho naʻe kamata ngaueʻaki ai ʻa e Lao ni.

#### 54 Fakaha ki tuʻa mei Tonga

(1) ʻE ngofua ke fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ʻa ia kuo tanaki ʻi Tonga ki ha taha ʻi tuʻa ʻi Tonga ʻo kapau pe –

(a) ʻoku loto ʻa e taha ko ia ki he fakaha;

(b) ʻoku fakangofua ʻe he Lao ni ʻa e fakaha;

(c) ko e taha ʻoku ne maʻu ʻa e fakamatala ʻoku ne fakahoko ʻa e ngaahi fatongia ʻoku faitatau mo e ngaahi fatongia ʻoku fakahoko ʻe ha taha ʻa ia ʻe ala fakangofua ʻe he Lao ni ke fakaha ki ai ‘e he tauhi fakamatala ‘a e fakamatala ‘i Tonga ‘i he kupu 44(2) ‘o e Lao ni pe kupu 47(1)(b) pe

(c) ‘o e Lao ni;

(d) ʻoku fakakakato ʻa e ngaahi makatuʻunga ko ʻeni –

(i) ko e tauhi fakamatala ko ha sino ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e kupu 49(1) pea kuo tuʻutuʻuni‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni koeʻuhi ko etaumuʻa ʻo e kupu siʻi ni;

(ii) ko e fakaha koʻeuhi koe taumuʻa ki he palani ki he moʻui pe tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui;

(iii) ʻoku felaveʻi ʻa e fakamatala mo hono tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku fakahoko ʻi Tonga ki ha taha ʻa ia kuo ne nofo ʻi tuʻa mei Tonga ‘i he taimi ni; pea

(iv) ʻoku fakahoko hono fakaha ʻo e fakamatala ki he puleʻanga ʻo e feituʻu ʻoku nofo ai ʻa e taha ko ia;

(e) ʻoku fiemaʻu fakapotopoto hono fakaha ʻo e fakamatala koeʻuhi ko hono fakahoko ʻo e tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ki he taha ko ia, kae ʻikai kapau kuo tuʻutuʻuni mahino ʻe he taha ko ia ke ʻoua ʻe fakahoko ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e fakaha; pe

(f) ʻoku fiemaʻu fakapotopoto hono fakaha ʻo e fakamatala ki hono puleʻi ʻo e ngaahi totongi fekauʻaki mo hono fakahoko ʻo e tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ki he taha ko ia pe ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni aleapau pe fakalao ʻi he fekauʻaki mo ia.

Kupu 55 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 50 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(2) Kapau ʻe fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ʻi he kupu siʻi (1)(e) pea kapau ʻoku taʻofi ʻe ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e taha ko ia ʻi he kupu siʻi ko ia mei hono fakaha ʻa e kotoa ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku pehe ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻoku fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ki hono fakahoko ʻo e tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ki he taha ko ia, kuo paukefakahaʻehetauhifakamatalaki hetaha ʻaiaʻoku nefakahoko hono fakaha ʻo e meʻa ko ia.

#### 55 Fakamatala pelepelengesi taautaha ki he moʻui

(1) ʻI he kupu ni ʻoku ʻuhinga ʻa e “fakamatala pelepelengesi taautaha ki he moʻui” ki he fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia kuo fakahaaʻi ʻe ha taha ki he tauhi fakamatala ko ha fakamatala ʻoku pehe ʻe he taha ko ia ʻoku natula pelepelengesi pe ko e –

(a) fakamatala fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala ʻo e sivi faʻunga tupuʻanga ʻo ha taha;

(b) fakamatala fekauʻaki mo e fokoutua ʻo ha taha ʻi heʻene fetuʻutaki mo e HIV pe AIDS;

(c) fakamatala fekauʻaki mo e fokoutua ʻo ha taha ʻi heʻene fetuʻutaki mo e mahaki ʻoku fakatupu ‘e he feʻauaki;

(d) fakamatala fekauʻaki mo e maveuveu fakaʻatamai;

(e) fakamatala fekauʻaki mo e feitama ʻo ha taha; pe

(f) fakamatala fekauʻaki mo hono fakamamahi fakalielia pe fakamamahi ʻo ha taha;

(2) Kapau ʻoku fakaha ʻe ha taha ki he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku tauhi pe puleʻi ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo e taha ko ia ʻa ia ʻoku ne pehe ko e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku natula pelepelengesi, kuo pau ke fokotuʻu ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala ʻi he lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, pe fakafelaveʻi ha fakamatala ki he lekooti ko ia, ke fakaha ʻa e konga ʻo e lekooti ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e fakamatala pelepelengesi taautaha ki he moʻui.

(3) Fakatatau ki he kupu siʻi (4), kimuʻa pea fakaha ha fakamatala pelepelengesi taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha, ko e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui kuo pau ke ne –

(a) fakaha ki he taha ʻa ia ʻoku felaveʻi mo ia ʻa e fakamatala, ʻa e taha ʻoku fakaha ki ai mo e ʻuhinga ʻoku pehe ai ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻoku fiemaʻu ke fakahoko ʻa e fakaha;

(b) vakai ki he ngaahi fakakaukau, ʻa e taha ko ia, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, felaveʻi mo e fakaha kuo fokotuʻu;

(c) fokotuʻu ha fakamatala ʻi he lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo e taha ko ia, pe fakafelaveʻi ha fakamatala ki he lekooti, ke fakaha kuo Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 56 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 51 faipau ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he kupu ni pe koe ʻuhinga ʻoku pehe ai ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻoku ʻikai ke ngaueʻaki ʻa e kupu ni ki ai; mo

(d) fokotuʻu ha fakamatala ʻi he lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo e taha ko ia, pe fakafelaveʻi ha fakamatala ki he lekooti, ke fakaha ʻa e ʻuhinga naʻe fiemaʻu ai ʻa hono fakaha ʻo e fakamatala pelepelengesi taautaha ki he moʻui.

(4) ʻOku ʻikai ngaueʻaki ʻa e kupu siʻi (3)(a) mo e (b) kapau –

(a) ʻoku ʻi ai ha fiemaʻu fakavavevave, ʻi he fakakaukau ʻa e tauhi fakamatala, ke fakaha ʻa e fakamatala pelepelengesi taautaha ki he moʻui pea ʻoku ʻikai malava fakapotopoto ke faipau ki he kupu siʻi ko ia;

(b) kuo tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he lao ʻa hono fakaha; pe

(c) kuo loto ʻa e taha ʻa ia ʻoku felaveʻi ki ai ʻa e fakamatala pelepelengesi taautaha ki he moʻui ki he fakaha.

(5) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala pelepelengesi taautaha ki he moʻui kapau ʻe fakakakato ʻe ha fakamatala taautaha kehe ki he moʻui ʻa e taumuʻa tatau.

(6) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ha fakamatala pelepelengesi taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo lahi ange ʻi hono fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ke fakakakato ʻa e taumuʻa ʻo e fakaha.

## KONGA 5 – HŪ KI HE NGAAHI LEKOOTI ‘O E FAKAMATALA

TAAUTAHA KI HE MOʻUI MO HONO FAKATONUTONU VAHE 1 – HŪ

#### 56 Ngaueʻaki ʻo e Konga

(1) ʻOku ʻikai ngaueʻaki ʻa e Konga ni ki ha lekooti ʻoku ʻi ai ha –

(a) fakamatala ki he tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina;

(b) fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui kuo tanaki pe faʻu koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke faipau ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻo ha polokalama fakapapauʻi ʻa e tuʻunga lelei ʻi he Lao ki he Kau Toketa, Kau Ngaue Faitoʻo ki he Nifo mo e Kau Ngaue ki he Moʻui 2021, ko e Lao ki he Kau Neesi, Kau Neesi Fakafaitoʻo mo e Kau Maʻuli Fakapolofesinale 2021 pe ko e Lao ki he Huʻivai [Vahe 12.14];

(c) fakamatala ‘oku teʻeki ai liliu mei he ngaahi sivi pe vakavakaiʻi ʻo e ʻatamai; pe

(d) fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he faʻahinga kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he tauhi pe ‘i he pule ʻa ha faʻahinga pe ngaahi Kupu 57 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 52 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to faʻahinga ʻo e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(2) Neongo ʻa e kupu siʻi (1), ʻoku ngaueʻaki ʻa e Konga ni ki he konga ko ia ʻo ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻe lava ʻo fakamavahevahe fakapotopoto mei he konga ko ia ʻo e lekooti ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e fakamatala kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi (1).

(3) Fakatatau ki ha ngaahi fakaʻatā mo ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni makehe, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, ʻoku ngaueʻaki ʻa e Konga ni ki halekootiʻokutauhipepuleʻiʻehatauhifakamatalakihemoʻuiʻofekauʻaki mo e kotoa pe kongaʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo e taha ko ia ʻa ia ʻoku lava ʻo hū ki ai ʻo fou ʻi he Sisitemi Fakafonua ‘o e Fakamatala ki he Moʻui ʻoku vakai ki ai, tokangaʻi pe kehe ange ‘a e ngaue ki ai ʻa e tauhi fakamatala.

#### 57 Totonu ʻa ha taha ke hū

(1) Fakatatau ki he Konga ni, ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e totonu ʻa ha taha ke hū ki ha lekooti ʻo efakamatalataautahakihemoʻuifekauʻakimohatahaʻaiaʻokutauhipepuleʻi ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui tukukehe –

(a) ko e lekooti pe fakamatala ʻi he lekooti ʻoku makatuʻunga ia ʻi ha totonu fakalao ʻoku ne tapui hono fakaha ʻo e lekooti pe fakamatala, ko e hā pe ʻe hoko, ki he taha ko ia;

(b) ʻoku tapui ʻe ha Lao kehe pe ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e fakamaauʻanga hono fakaha ki he taha ko ia ʻa e lekooti pe ko e fakamatala ʻi he lekooti ʻi he ngaahi tukunga;

(c) ko e fakamatala ʻi he lekooti naʻe tanaki pe faʻu ʻo tefito ʻi he ʻamanaki ki ha pe ke ngaueʻaki ʻi ha fakatonutonu fakalao, pea ko e fakatonutonu fakalao,fakataha mo e kotoa ʻo e ngaahi tangi pe founga ngaue kuomaʻu mei ai, ʻoku teʻeki ai ke aofangatuku;

(d) kuo fakakakato ʻa e ngaahi makatuʻunga ko ʻeni –

(i) ko e fakamatala naʻe tanaki pe faʻu ʻi hono fakahoko ʻo ha sivi, fakatotolo pe ha ngaue tatau mo ia naʻe fakangofua ʻe he lao, pe ngaue naʻe fakahoko koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke fakatotolo, vakaiʻi pe taʻofi ʻa hono maʻu pe feinga ha taha ke maʻu ha ngaue pe monuʻia, ʻa ia ʻoku ʻikai totonu ki ai ʻa e taha ko ia ʻi he Lao pe ko ha polokalama ʻoku fakahoko ʻe he Minisita, pe ko ha totongi ʻo ha ngaue pehe pe monuʻia; pea

(ii) ko e sivi, fakatotolo, pe ngaue tatau mo ia, fakataha mo e kotoa ʻo e ngaahi fakatonutonu fakalao, tangi pe ngaue kuo maʻu mei ai, ʻoku teʻeki ai ke aofangatuku; pe

(e) ko hono fakaʻatā ʻo e hū ki he fakamatala ʻe lava ʻo fakafuofua fakapotopoto ʻe –– Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 57 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 53

(i) ʻi ai ha faingamalie ki ha fakatamaki lahi ki he faitoʻo pe fakaakeake ʻa ha taha pe ko ha faingamalie ki ha lavea lahi ki he taha ko ia pe ki ha taha kehe;

(ii) iku ki hono fakahaaʻi ʻo ha taha naʻe tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he lao ke ne ʻoatu ha fakamatala ‘i he lekooti ki he tauhi fakamatala; pe

(iii) iku ki hono fakahaaʻi ʻo ha taha naʻa ne ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala ʻi he lekooti ki he tauhi fakamatala ʻo fakamahino pe ʻikai fakahangatonu ʻi he falala, kapau ʻe pehe ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻoku totonu ʻi he ngaahi tukunga ke tauhi ʻa e fakaʻilonga ʻo e taha ko ia ke fakapulipuli.

(2) Ko e totonu ke hū ki ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku kau ki ai ʻa e totonu ke hū ki he lekooti ʻi ha fotunga fakaʻilekitulonika ʻoku ne fakakakato ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, ʻo fakatatau ki ha ngaahi fakangatangata, tuʻutuʻuni makehe pe ngaahi fakaʻatā ʻe ngofua ke tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(3) Neongo ʻa e kupu siʻi (1), ʻoku ‘i ai ‘a e totonu ke hū ‘a ha taha ki he konga ko ia ʻo ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekau’aki mo e taha ko ia

‘a ia ʻe lava ʻo fakamavahevahe fakapotopoto mei he konga ko ia ʻo e lekooti

‘a ia ‘oku ‘ikai ʻi ai ha totonu ke hū ‘a e taha ko ia koeʻuhi ko e kupu siʻi (1)(a) ki he (e).

(4) Neongo ʻa e kupu siʻi (1), ka ʻi ai ha lekooti ʻoku ʻikai ko ha lekooti ʻoku taumuʻa tāfataha pe ki he fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo e taha ʻoku kole ke hū ki he fakamatala, ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e totonu ʻa e taha ko ia ki he konga pe ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo e taha ʻi he lekooti ʻa ia ʻe lava ʻo fakamavahevahe fakapotopoto mei he lekooti koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke fakaʻatā ʻa e hū.

(5) Kimuʻa pea tuʻutuʻuni ke ʻoua ʻe tali ʻa e hū ʻa ha taha ki ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu siʻi (1)(e)(i), ʻe ngofua ke kumi faleʻi ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ki ha toketa ʻa ia ʻoku lesisita ‘i he Lao ki he Kau Toketa, Kau Ngaue ki he Nifo mo e Kau Ngaue ki he Mo‘ui 2021.

(6) ʻOku ʻikai ha meʻa ʻi he Lao ni te ne taʻofi ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui –

(a) mei hono tali ʻa e hū ʻa ha taha ki ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, ʻa ia ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e totonu ʻa e taha ko ia ke hū ki ai, kapau ʻoku fakahoko ʻe he taha ko ia ha kole ‘i ha lea ke hū ki he fakamatala pe ʻoku ʻikai ke ne fakahoko ha kole ke hū ʻi he kupu 58; pe

(b) fekauʻaki mo e lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e totonu ʻa ha taha ke hū ki ai, ʻo fetuʻutaki mo e taha ko ia pe ko hono fetongi ʻi he faituʻutuʻuni ʻa ia kuo fakamafai ke ne fakangofua ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e taha ko ia ki he hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo e taha ko ia.

(7) ʻOku ʻikai hameʻaʻi he Konga ni tenefakaʻatā ha tauhi fakamatala ki hemoʻui mei he fatongia fakalao ke ne ʻoatu, ʻi ha founga ʻa ia ʻoku ʻikai ke Kupu 58 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 54 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to fehangahangai mo e Lao ni,ʻa e fakamatalataautaha kihe moʻui ʻi he vave taha ʻoku fiemaʻu ki ai koeʻuhi ko hono fakahoko ʻo e tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ki he taha ko ia.

#### 58 Kole ke hū

(1) ʻE ngofua ke ngaueʻaki ʻe ha tahaʻa e totonu ke hū ki ha lekootiʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻaki hono fakahoko ha kole ke hū ‘i ha tohi ki he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻoku ne tauhi pe puleʻi ʻa e fakamatala.

(2) Kuo pau ke kau ʻi he kole ʻa e fakaikiiki feʻunga ke lava ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻo fakahaaʻi mo maʻu ʻa e lekooti ʻaki ha ngaue feʻunga pe.

(3) Kapau ʻoku ʻikai ha fakaikiiki feʻunga ʻi he kole ke lava ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻo fakahaaʻi mo maʻu ʻa e lekooti ʻaki ha ngaue feʻunga pe, kuo pau ke tokoni ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he taha ʻoku ne kole ʻa e hū ki he fakamatala ʻi hono toe faʻu ʻo e kole ke faipau ki he kupu siʻi (2).

#### 59 Tali ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui

(1) Ka ʻi ai ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻoku ne maʻu ha kole mei ha taha ke hū ki ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui kuo pau ke ne –

(a) fakaʻatā ʻa e lekooti ke vakai ki ai ʻa e taha ko ia pea, ʻi hano kole ʻe he taha ko ia, ke ne ʻoatu ha tatau ʻo e lekooti ki he taha ko ia pea kapau ʻoku malava, ʻoatu ha fakamatala ʻo ha lea, fika pe fakanounou ʻoku ngaueʻaki ʻi he lekooti;

(b) ʻoatu ha fanongonongo tohi ki he taha ko ia ʻo fakaha ai, hili ha kumi feʻunga, kuo aofangatuku ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻoku ʻikai ke ʻi ai ha lekooti pehe, pe ‘oku ‘ikai maʻu, pe ʻoku ʻikai ko ha lekooti ia ʻoku ngaueʻaki ki ai ʻa e Konga ni, kapau ko e meʻa ia;

(c) kapau ʻoku fakamafai ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ke ʻikai ke ne tali ha kole, ʻi he fakakatoa pe ʻi ha konga, ʻi hatuʻutuʻuni pe ʻo e Konga ni ʻo kehe mei he kupu 57(c), (d) pe (e), ke ne ʻoatu ha fanongonongo tohi ki he taha ko iaʻofakahaʻokuʻikaitaliʻehetauhifakamatalaʻaekole,ʻihefakakatoa pe ʻi ha konga, ʻoatu mo ha ʻuhinga ki hono ʻikai tali mo fakaha ʻoku ‘i ai ‘ene totonu ke fakahoko ha launga ʻa e taha fekauʻaki mo e ʻikai tali ʻo e kole ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻi he Konga 8 ‘o e Lao ni; pe

(d) fakatatatau ki he kupu siʻi (2), kapau ʻoku fakamafai ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ke ʻikai ke ne tali ha kole, ʻi he fakakatoa pe ʻi ha konga, ʻi he kupu 57(1)(c), (d) pe (e), ke ne fakahoko atu ‘i ha tohi ki he taha ko ia

‘o fakaha ki he taha ko ia ‘oku ‘i ai ‘ene totonu ke fakahoko ha launga fekauʻaki mo hono ‘ikai tali ki he ‘Ofisa Pule Ngaue ‘i he Konga 8, pea ko e tauhi fakamatala ʻoku ʻikai ke ne tali – Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 59 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 55

(i) ʻa e kole, ʻi he fakakatoa pe ʻi ha konga, lolotonga ‘ene lave ki he kupu 57(1)(c), (d) mo e (e) ʻoku ngaueʻaki;

(ii) ʻa e kole, ʻi he fakakatoa pe ʻi ha konga, ʻi ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻe taha pe lahi ange ʻo e kupu 57(1)(c), (d) mo e (e), kae ʻikai lave mahino ki he ngaahi kupu ko ia ʻoku ngaueʻaki; pe

(iii) ke fakapapauʻi pe fakaʻikaiʻi ʻa e ʻi ai ha lekooti ʻo fakatatau ki he kupu 57(1)(c), (d) mo e (e).

(2) Kuo pau ka ʻi ai ha tauhi fakamatala ʻe ngaue ʻi he kupu siʻi (1)(d) ʻe ʻikai ke ne ngaue ʻi he kupu siʻi (1)(d)(i) kapau ko hono fakahoko ia ʻe ʻi ai ha ʻuhinga lelei ke fiemaʻu ʻi he ngaahi tukunga ʻoku ʻilo ki ai ʻa e taha ko ia ʻoku ne fakahoko ʻa e faituʻutuʻuni ʻo fakafofongaʻi ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ke ne fakaha ki he taha ko ia, fakahangatonu pe ʻikai fakahangatonu, ʻa e fakamatala ʻa ia ʻoku ʻikai ke ʻi ai ha totonu ke hū ki ai ʻa e taha ko ia.

(3) Fakatatau ki he kupu siʻi (4), kuo pau ke ʻoatu ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e tali kuo tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he kupu siʻi (1)(a), (b), (c) pe (d) ʻi he vave taha ʻe ala lava ʻi he ngaahi tukunga ʻo ʻikai tomui ange ʻi he ʻaho ʻe 30 hili hono maʻu ʻo e kole.

(4) ʻI loto ʻi he ʻaho ʻe 30 hili hono maʻu ʻo e kole ke hū, ʻe ngofua ke fakaloloa ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakangatangata ki he taimi kuo fokotuʻu ʻi he kupu siʻi (3) ki ha toe vahaʻa taimi ʻo ʻikai lahi hake ʻi he ʻaho ʻe 30 kapau ko e –

(a) tulitulifua ki he fakangatangata ʻi he taimi ʻe uesia ia ʻo ʻikai ha ʻuhinga lelei ʻi he ngaahi ngaue ʻa e tauhi fakamatala koeʻuhi ʻoku ʻi he fakamatala ʻa e ngaahi kongkonga kehekehe ʻo e fakamatala pe ko hono kumi ʻo e fakamatala ʻe fiemaʻu ki ai ha fekumi taimi loloa; pe

(b) ko e taimi kuo tuʻutuʻuni ke fakahoko ʻaki ʻa e ngaahi fealeaʻaki ʻoku fiemaʻu ke fakahoko ʻaki ʻa e tali ki he kole ʻi loto ʻi he ʻaho ʻe 30 hili hono maʻu ia, ʻe ʻikai lava fakapotopoto ke fakahoko ha tali ʻi loto ʻi he taimi ko ia.

(5) ʻI hono fakaloloa ʻo e fakangatangata ki he taimi ʻi he kupu siʻi (4), kuo pau ke ʻoatu ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ki he taha ko ia ʻa e fanongonongo tohi ki he fakaloloa, ʻo fakaha ʻa e loloa ʻo e fakaloloa mo e ʻuhinga ki he fakaloloa.

(6) Neongo ʻa e kupu siʻi (3), kuo pau ke ʻoatu ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e tali ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he kupu siʻi (1)(a), (b), (c) pe (d) ʻi loto ʻi he vahaʻa taimi kuo fakamahino pau ʻe he taha ko ia kapau –

(a) ʻoku ʻoatu ʻe he taha ko ia ki he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e fakamoʻoni ʻoku fakafiemalie ki he tauhi fakamatala, ʻo ne fakahoko ia ʻi ha tuʻunga ʻoku ʻuhingamalie, ʻoku fiemaʻu ʻe he taha ko ia ʻa e hū ki he lekooti kuo kole ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi ha tuʻunga ʻoku fakavavevave ʻi loto ʻi he vahaʻa taimi ko ia; pea Kupu 60 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 56 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(b) ʻoku lava ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ke ʻoatu ʻa e tali kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘i loto ʻi he vahaʻa taimi ko ia.

(7) Ka ʻi ai ha tauhi fakamatala ʻoku ne tui ʻo tuʻunga ʻi he ngaahi ʻuhinga fakapotopoto ʻoku launoa mo fakatupuʻita pe naʻe fakahoko ʻi he loto kovi ha kole ke hū ki ha lekooti fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, ʻe ngofua ke ʻikai te ne tali ke fakangofua ʻa e hū ʻa e taha ko ia ki he lekooti kuo kole.

(8) Kapau ʻe ʻikai tali ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ki he kole ʻi loto ʻi he fakangatangata ʻo e taimi pe kimuʻa ʻi he ngata ʻa e fakaloloa, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, kuo pau ke lau kuo ʻikai tali ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e kole ʻa e taha ko ia ke hū.

(9) Kapau ʻe ʻikai tali pe ʻoku lau kuo ʻikai tali ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e kole, ʻi he fakakatoa pe ʻi ha konga –

(a) ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e totonu ʻa e taha ko ia ke fakahoko ha launga fekauʻaki mo e ʻikai tali ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻi he Konga 8; pea

(b) ʻi he launga, ko e kavenga hono fakamoʻoniʻi fekauʻaki mo e ʻikai ke tali ʻoku ʻi he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui.

(10) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai faʻu ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui pe ko ha konga ʻo ia ʻo fakaʻatā ki ha taha ʻi he Konga ni pe ʻoatu ha tatau ʻo ia ki ha taha ʻi he kupu siʻi (1)(a) ʻo ʻikai tomuʻa fakahoko ʻa e ngaahi sitepu ke fiemalie ki he fakahaaʻi ʻo e taha ko ia.

(11) Ka ʻi ai ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻoku ne faʻu ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui pe ha konga ʻo ia ʻo e fakaʻatā ki ha taha ʻi he Konga ni pe ʻoatu ha tatau ʻo ia ki ha taha ʻi he kupu siʻi (1)(a) ʻe ngofua ke hilifaki ha totongi kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ko ia kapau naʻe tomuʻa ʻoange ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ha fakafuofua ʻo e totongi ki he taha ko ia.

(12) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai laka hake ʻa e lahi ʻo e totongi ʻi he lahi kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni pe ko e lahi ʻo e fakamole totonu ʻe lava toe tanaki mai, kapau ʻoku ʻikai ha mahuʻinga ʻe tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(13) ʻE ngofua ke toʻo ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia kuo lave ki ai ʻi he kupu siʻi (11) ʻa e totongi ʻo e kotoa pe ko ha konga ʻo e totongi ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ke totongi ʻe ha taha ʻi he kupu siʻi ko ia kapau, ʻi he fakakaukau ʻa e tauhi fakamatala, ʻoku totonu mo vahevahe tatau ke fakahoko ia.

VAHE 2 – FAKATONUTONU

#### 60 Fakatonutonu

(1) Ka ʻi ai ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui kuo ne tali ʻa e hū ʻa ha taha ki ha lekooti ʻo ʻene fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui pea kapau ʻoku tui ʻa e taha ko ia ʻoku hala pe ʻikai kakato ʻa e lekooti koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa kuo hū, Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 60 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 57 tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe kuo ngaueʻaki ki ai ʻa e fakamatala ‘e he tauhi fakamatala, ʻe ngofua ke kole ʻi ha tohi ʻe he taha ko ia ke fakatonutonu ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e lekooti.

(2) ʻI he vave taha ʻe ala lava ʻi he ngaahi tukunga ʻo ʻikai toe tomui ange ʻi he ʻaho ʻe 30 hili hono maʻu ha kole ki ha fakatonutonu ʻi he kupu siʻi (1), kuo pau ke tali pe ʻikai tali ʻe he tauhi fakamatala, ʻaki ha fanongonongo tohi ki he taha ko ia, ʻa e kole ʻa e taha ko ia ke fakaloloa ʻa e ʻaho ʻoku ngata ki ai ‘a hono fakahoko ʻo e tali ki ha vahaʻa taimi ʻoku ʻikai lahi hake ʻi he ʻaho ʻe 30 kapau –

(a) ʻe hoko ʻa e tali ki he kole ʻi loto ʻi he ʻaho ʻe 30 ke uesia ʻikai ha ʻuhinga lelei ʻa e ngaahi ngaue ʻa e tauhi fakamatala; pe

(b) ko e taimi kuo tuʻutuʻuni ke fakahoko ʻaki ʻa e ngaahi fealeaʻaki ʻoku fiemaʻu ke fakahoko ʻaki ʻa e tali ki he kole ʻi loto ʻi he ʻaho ʻe 30 ʻe ʻikai lava fakapotopoto ke fakahoko ha tali ʻi loto ʻi he taimi ko ia.

(3) Ka ʻi ai ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻoku ne fakaloloa ʻa e fakangatangata ʻo e taimi ʻi he kupu siʻi (2) kuo pau ke ne –

(a) ʻoatu ki he taha ko ia ʻa e fanongonongo tohi ʻo e fakaloloa ʻo fakaha ʻa e loloa ʻo e fakaloloa mo e ʻuhinga ki he fakaloloa; pea

(b) tali pe ʻikai tali ʻa e kole ʻa e taha ko ia ʻi he vave taha ʻe ala lava ʻi he ngaahi tukunga ‘o ʻikai tomui ange ʻi he ngata ʻa e fakangatangata ʻo e taimi ʻi hono fakaloloa.

(4) Ka ‘i ai ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻoku ʻikai ke ne tali ha kole ki ha fakatonutonu ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) ʻi loto ʻi he taimi kuo tuʻutuʻuni, kuo pau ʻe lau kuo ʻikai ke tali ʻa e kole.

(5) ʻE ngofua ki hatauhi fakamatala ki hemoʻui,‘oku ne tui ʻotuʻungaʻi ha ngaahi ʻuhinga fakapotopoto ko ha kole ki ha fakatonutonu ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) ʻoku launoa mo fakatupuʻita pe naʻe fakahoko ʻi he loto kovi, ke ne ‘ikai tali ‘a e kole ko ia, ʻi he meʻa ko ia, pea kuo pau ke ne ‘oatu ki he taha ko ia ha fanongonongoʻokufokotuʻu ʻi ai ʻa e ngaahi ʻuhinga ki he ʻikai ke tali peaʻoku fokotuʻu ʻi ai ʻa e totonu ʻa e taha ko ia ke fakahoko ha launga fekauʻaki mo e ʻikai tali ki he ‘Ofisa Pule Ngaue ʻi he Konga 8.

(6) ʻOku ʻi ai ʻa e totonu ʻa e taha ko ia ke fakahoko ha launga ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ‘i he Konga 8 fekauʻaki mo e ʻikai tali ʻi he kupu siʻi (5).

(7) Kuo pau ke tali ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha kole ki ha fakatonutonu ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) kapau ʻe fakaha ʻe he taha ko ia, ki he fiemalie ʻa e tauhi fakamatala, ʻoku ʻikai kakato pe hala ʻa e lekooti koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻa ia ʻoku ngaueʻaki ki ai ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e fakamatala pea ʻoatu ki he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e fakamatala feʻunga ke lava ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ‘o fakatonutonu ʻa e lekooti.

(8) Neongo ʻa e kupu siʻi (7), ʻoku ʻikai tuʻutuʻuni ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke ne fakatonutonu ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui kapau – Kupu 60 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 58 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(a) ʻoku ʻi ai ha lekooti ʻa ia naʻe ‘ikai faʻu ʻe he tauhi fakamatala pea ʻoku ʻikai ha ʻilo, taukei mo ha mafai feʻunga ʻa e tauhi fakamatala, ke fakatonutonu ʻaki ʻa e lekooti; pe

(b) ʻoku ʻi ai ha fakakaukau pe vakai fakapalofesinale naʻe fakahoko ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻi he loto lelei fekauʻaki mo e taha ko ia.

(9) ‘I hono tali ha kole ki ha fakatonutonu ‘i he kupu siʻi (1), ko e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui kuo pau ke ne –

(a) fakahoko ʻa e fakatonutonu kuo kole ʻaki hono –

(i) lekooti ʻo e fakamatala totonu ʻi he lekooti pea –

(A) toʻo ʻa e fakamatala hala ʻi ha founga ʻa ia ʻe ʻikai ke tamateʻi ʻa e lekooti; pe

(B) kapau ʻoku ʻikai ke lava ia, leipolo ʻa e fakamatala ʻoku hala, ʻo fakamavahe ʻa e fakamatala hala mei he lekooti, ʻo tauhi makehe ia mei he lekooti mo tauhi ha fakafehokotaki ʻihelekootiʻaiaʻelavahatahakemuimuiʻiʻaefakamatala hala; pe

(ii) kapau ʻoku ʻikai lava ke lekooti ʻa e fakamatala tonu ʻi he lekooti, ʻo fakapapauʻi ʻoku ʻi ai ha sisitemi ngaue ke ne fakaha ki ha taha ʻoku ne hū ki he lekooti koe fakamatala ʻi he lekooti ʻoku hala pea ke fakahinohino ʻa e taha ko ia ki he fakamatala ʻoku tonu;

(b) ʻoatu ʻa e fanongonongo ki he taha ko ia ʻo fekauʻaki mo ia kuo ne fakahoko ʻi he palakalafi (a);

(c) ʻi hano kole ʻe he taha ko ia, ʻoatu ha fanongonongo tohi ʻo e fakatonutonu kuo kole, ki he ngataʻanga ʻe ala lava fakapotopoto, ki he niʻihi ʻa ia kuo fakaha ki ai ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e fakamatala ʻo fekauʻaki mo e kole ʻa e taha ke fakatonutonu ʻa e lekooti, tukukehe kapau ʻoku ʻikai ʻiloʻi ha uesia ʻo e fakatonutonu ki he hokohoko atu hono fakahoko ʻo e tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui pe ngaahi monuʻia kehe ki he taha ko ia.

(10) Ko ha fanongonongo ʻo e ʻikai tali ʻa e kole ʻi he kupu siʻi (4) pe (5) kuo pau ke ʻoatu ʻa e ngaahi ʻuhinga ki he ʻikai tali pea fakaha ki he taha ko e taha ko ia ʻoku ʻi ai ʻene totonu ke –

(a) teuteu ha fakamatala nounou ʻo e faikehekehe ʻa ia ʻoku ne fokotuʻu ʻa e fakatonutonu ʻoku ʻikai tali ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke fakahoko;

(b) tuʻutuʻuni ke fakapipiki ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala ʻo e faikehekehe ko e konga ‘o e ngaahi lekooti ‘oku ne tauhi

‘o e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ‘o e taha ko ia mo fakaha ‘a e fakamatala ‘o e faikehekehe ‘i he taimi pe ‘oku fakaha ai ‘e he tauhi fakamatala ha fakamatala ‘a ia ‘oku felaveʻi mo e fakamatala;

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 61 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 59

(c) tuʻutuʻuni ke fakahoko ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e kotoa ʻo e ngaahi ngaue e ala lava ke fakaha ʻa e fakamatala ʻo e faikehekehe ki ha taha ʻa ia naʻe mei fakaha ki ai ʻi he kupu (10)(c) kapau naʻe tali ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e kole ki he fakatonutonu; pea

(d) fakahoko ha launga fekauʻaki mo e ʻikai ke tali ʻa e kole ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻi he Konga 8.

(11) Kapau ʻe ʻikai tali ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui, ʻi he kupu siʻi (3) pe (4), ha kole ki ha fakatonutonu ʻi he kupu siʻi (1), ʻi he fakakatoa pe ʻi he konga, pe kuo lau kuo ʻikai ke tali ʻa e kole, ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e totonu ʻa e taha ko ia ke fakahoko ʻa e ngaahi meʻa kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi ha taha ʻo e ngaahi kupu siʻi

(10)(a), (b), (c) mo e (d).

(12) Kapau ʻoku fakahoko ʻe he taha ko ia ha taha ʻo e ngaahi meʻa ʻi he kupu (10)(b) pe (c), kuo pau ke faipau ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu ʻoku ngaueʻaki ki ai.

## KONGA 6 – TUʻUNGA ʻO E NGAUE TOKANGAEKINA

61 ʻUhingaʻi Lea

(1) ʻI he Konga ni –

“fakaha” ʻoku ʻuhinga, ʻi heʻene fekauʻaki mo e tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina, ke fakaha pe fakaʻatā ʻa e fakamatala ki ha taha ʻa ia ʻoku ʻikai ko ha memipa ʻo e komiti tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻa ia ʻoku fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala, pea ko e “fakaha” ʻoku fekauʻaki hono ʻuhinga mo ia;

“falemahaki” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ha falemahaki fakatatau ki he Lao ki he Ngaahi Ngaue ki he Moʻui [Vahe 12.06];

“komiti tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ha sino ʻe taha pe tokolahi ange ʻo e kakai –

(a) ʻa ia kuo fokotuʻu, fakanofo pe kuo tali –

(i) ʻe ha falemahaki; pe

(ii) ʻe ha sino ʻoku ne fakahoko ʻa e tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui peaʻaiakuotuʻutuʻuni‘e heNgaahiTuʻutuʻunipeʻokukau ki ha kalasi ʻo e ngaahi sino kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni;

(b) ko ʻene taumuʻa ke –

(i) fakaleleiʻi ʻa e tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ʻoku fakahoko ʻe he falemahaki, ko e sino pe kau ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui; pe Kupu 62 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 60 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(ii) akoʻi ʻa e niʻihi ʻoku nau fakahoko ʻa e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui; pea

(c) ko hono ngaahi fatongia ke sivi mo vakaiʻi hono fakahoko ʻe he falemahaki ʻa e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ki ha taha, ko e sino pe kau ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ʻaki ʻa e ngaahi founga ʻa ia ʻe ala kau ki ai ha vakai fakapapauʻi ʻo e tuʻunga lelei, sivi hono puleʻi ʻo e fehalaaki mo e fakatamaki, toe vakaiʻi ʻe he ngaahi memipa kehe ʻoku taau, vakai ʻo e ngaahi tuʻunga ngaue, toe vakaiʻi hono ngaueʻaki, toe vakaiʻi ‘o e ola mo e vakai fakafaitoʻo mo fakapalofesinale kehe;

“fakamoʻoni” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ha taha, ʻo tatau pe ko ha faʻahi ia ki ha fakatonutonu fakalao, ʻa ia ʻi hono fakahoko ʻo e fakatonutonu fakalao –

(a) ʻoku fakafehuʻi ke ʻilo mei ai ha meʻa, ʻi he lea pe ko e tohi;

(b) ʻoku ne fakahoko ha fuakava; pe

(c) ʻoku feʻunga pe ʻoku lava ʻo fakalotoʻi ke fakafehuʻi pe ke ne fakaha ha tohi ngaue, ʻo tatau pe ʻoku fakafuakavaʻi pe ʻikai.

(2) Koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e Konga ni, ko e fakamatala ki he tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ha fakamatala kapau –

(a) ʻoku tanaki ia pe teuteu maʻa e komiti tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ki he taumuʻa pe ke tokoni ki he komiti ʻi hono fakahoko hono ngaahi fatongia;

(b) naʻe tupu mei ha ngaue naʻe fakahoko ʻe ha komiti tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ko e konga ʻo hono ngaahi fatongia; pe

(c) ko e fakamatala ia ʻoku ne fakakakato ʻa e makatuʻunga kuo tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni ki he fakamatala ki he tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina.

(3) Neongo ʻa e kupu siʻi (2), ʻoku ʻikai kau ki he fakamatala ki he tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻa e ngaahi meʻa ko ʻeni –

(a) fakamatala kuo hū ki ai, tanaki pe ngaueʻaki ki he ngaahi taumuʻa hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ki ha taha; pe

(b) fakamatala kuo tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻoku ʻikai ko ha fakamatala ki he tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina.

#### 62 Fakaha ki he komiti

NeongoʻaengaahituʻutuʻuniʻoeLaoni,ʻengofuakefakahaʻehatahahafakamatala ki hakomiti tuʻunga ʻo engauetokangaekinakoeʻuhi ko e ngaahitaumuʻaʻo ekomiti.

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 63 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 61 63 ʻIkai fakaha ʻo e fakamatala

(1) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai ha taha ke ne fakahoko ha taha ʻo e ngaahi meʻa ko ʻeni, tukukehe ʻi hono fakangofua ʻe he Konga ni –

(a) fakaha ʻa e fakamatala fekauʻaki mo ha ngaue kuo fakahoko ʻe ha komiti tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ko e konga ʻo hono ngaahi fatongia; pe

(b) fakaha ʻo e fakamatala ki he tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina.

(2) Neongo ʻa e kupu siʻi (1), ʻe ngofua ke fakaha ʻe he komiti tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻa e fakamatala ki he tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ki ha taha pe niʻihi tokolahi ange ʻa ia ʻoku nau tokangaʻi ʻa e ngaahi ngaue ʻa e falemahaki pe ko e sino kuo fokotuʻu, fakanofo pe naʻa ne tali ʻa e komiti, kehe ange mei he niʻihi kuo tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni pe ʻoku ʻi ha kalasi kuo tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, kapau ʻoku pehe ʻe he komiti ʻoku fiemaʻu ʻa e fakaha koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ke tamateʻi pe fakasiʻisiʻi ha faingamalie lahi ke hoko ha lavea lahi ki ha niʻihi ʻoku nau maʻu ha ngaahi ngaue mei he falemahaki pe ko e sino.

(3) Neongo ʻa e kupu siʻi (1) ʻe ngofua ke fakaha ʻe ha taha ʻa e fakamatala fekauʻaki mo ha taha, kau ki ai ʻa e fakamatala ki he tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina, ʻo ʻikai ha fakangofua ʻa e taha ko ia kapau ʻoku fiemaʻu ʻa e fakaha koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ke tamateʻi pe fakasiʻisiʻi ha faingamalie lahi ke hoko ha lavea lahi ki ha taha pe ko ha kulupu ʻo ha niʻihi ʻoku lava ʻo ʻiloʻi.

(4) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai ngaueʻaki ʻe ha taha ʻa ia kuo fakaha ki ai ha fakamatala koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa kuo fokotuʻu ʻi he kupu siʻi (2) pe (3) tukukehe pe koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻa ia naʻe fakaha ai ʻa e fakamatala ki he taha ko ia.

(5) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai fakaha ʻe ha taha ʻa ia kuo fakaha ki ai ha fakamatala koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa kuo fokotuʻu ʻi he kupu siʻi (2) pe (3) tukukehe ʻi hono fakangofua ʻi he Konga ni. 64 ʻIkai fakaha ʻi ha fakatonutonu fakalao

(1) Tukukehe ʻi hano fakangofua ʻi he Konga ni, kuo pau ʻe ʻikai ʻeke ʻe ha taha ki ha taha fakamoʻoni pea ʻe ʻikai ha fakamaauʻanga pe ha sino kehe ʻi hano fakahoko ha fakatonutonu fakalao, kehe ange mei ha komiti tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina, kuo pau ke fakangofua pe tuʻutuʻuni ha fakamoʻoni ʻi he fakatonutonu fakalao ke –

(a) fakaha ha fakamatala fekauʻaki mo ha ngaue kuo fakahoko ʻe ha komiti tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ko e konga ʻo hono ngaahi fatongia; pe

(b) fakaha ʻa e fakamatala ki he tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina.

Kupu 65 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 62 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(2) Tukukehe ʻi hono fakangofua ʻe he Konga ni, ʻoku ʻikai tali ʻa e fakamatala ki he tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻi he fakamoʻoni ʻi ha fakatonutonu fakalao.

#### 65 Fakaʻatā

(1) ʻE ʻikai ngofua ke kamata ha fakaʻilo pe ha fakatonutonu fakalao kehe koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi maumau ki ha taha ʻa ia naʻa ne fakaha ʻi he loto lelei ʻa e fakamatala ki ha komiti tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻi he kole ʻa e komiti pe koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ke tokoniʻi ʻa e komiti ʻi hono fakahoko hono ngaahi fatongia.

(2) ʻE ʻikai ngofua ke kamata ha fakaʻilo pe ha fakatonutonu fakalao kehe koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi maumau pea ʻe ʻikai fakaʻilo ki ha hia ʻi he kupu 64 ha –

(a) memipa ʻo ha komiti ʻa ia, naʻa ne fakaha, ʻi he loto lelei, ʻa e fakamatala ki he tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina koeʻuhi ko ha taumuʻa kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu 63(2); pe

(b) taha ʻa ia, naʻa ne fakaha ʻi he loto lelei, ha fakamatala koeʻuhi ko ha taumuʻa kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu 63(3).

(3) ʻE ʻikai ngofua ke kamata fakaʻilo ha memipa ʻo ha komiti pe ha fakatonutonu fakalao kehe koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi maumau ʻo fekauʻaki mo e ʻikai lava ha komiti ke fakahoko ha fakaha ʻa ia ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ai ʻi he kupu 63(2).

## KONGA 7 – SISITEMI FAKAFONUA ʻO E FAKAMATALA KI

HE MOʻUI 66 ʻUhingaʻi Lea

(1) ʻI he Konga ni –

“komiti faleʻi” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki he komiti faleʻi kuo fokotuʻu ʻe he Minisita ʻi he kupu 74;

“NHIS” ʻoku ʻuhinga ki he Sisitemi Fakafonua ʻo e Fakamatala ki he Moʻui ʻa ia kuo faʻu pea tauhi ʻe he Potungaue pea ngaueʻaki ʻe ha pule fakamatala koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa hono fakaʻatā ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke ne hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki mo fakaha ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo ngaueʻaki ʻa e Sisitemi ʻo fakatatau mo e Konga ni mo e Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni kuo faʻu ʻi he Konga ni; mo e

“fakamatala fakaha” ʻoku tatau hono ʻuhinga mo ia ʻi he kupu 3(1).

(2) Neongo ha mea ʻi he kupu 3, koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e Konga ni, ʻoku lau ʻoku hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he ngaahi tukunga ko ʻeni – Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 67 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 63

(a) ʻi he vakai, tokangaʻi pe ngaue ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ki he kotoa pe konga ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ha taha ʻo ngaueʻaki ʻa e NHIS pea ko e fakamatala ko ia naʻe ʻoatu ki he NHIS ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ʻe taha ki he moʻui –

(i) ʻe lau ʻoku tanaki ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui kapau ko e fuofua taimi ia ʻoku ne vakai, tokangaʻi pe ngaue kehe ange ki he fakamatala; pea

(ii) ʻe lau ʻoku ngaueʻaki ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he taimi kotoa pe ʻoku ne hokohoko vakai ai, tokangaʻi pe ngaue kehe ange ki he fakamatala;

(b) ʻi ha taimi pe ʻoku vakai, tokangaʻi pe ngaue kehe ange ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ki he kotoa pe ha konga ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ha taha ʻo fou ʻi he NHIS pea ko e fakamatala ko ia naʻe ʻoatu ki he NHIS ʻe hetauhi fakamatala, ʻe lau ʻoku ngaueʻaki ʻe hetauhi fakamatala ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui; pe

(c) ʻe lau ʻoku fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala ʻi he ʻene ʻoatu ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ki he NHIS ʻi he taimi pe ʻoku tanaki ai ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ʻe taha ki he moʻui ʻa e fakamatala ʻo fou ʻi he NHIS.

(3) Neongo ha meʻa ʻi he kupu 3, ‘i hono ‘oatu ‘e ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui

‘a e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ki he NHIS –

(a) ‘e lau ‘oku ‘ikai fakaha ‘e he tauhi fakamatala ha fakamatala ki he NHIS; pea

(b) ‘e lau ‘oku ‘ikai tanaki ‘e he NHIS ‘a e fakamatala mei he tauhi fakamatala.

#### 67 NHIS

(1) ʻOku maʻuʻe he Potungaue‘a emafai mo e fatongia kefaʻumotauhiʻa e NHIS ʻo fakatatau mo e Konga ni mo e Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni kuo faʻu ʻi he Konga ni.

(2) Kuo pau ke fakahoko ʻe he Potungaue mo hono pule fakamatala ʻa e ngaahi fatongia ko ʻeni –

(a) puleʻi mo fakatahaʻi ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku ne maʻu mei he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui;

(b) fakapapauʻi ʻa e ngaue totonu ʻa e NHIS ʻaki hono monomono ʻo e ngaahi sisitemi fakaʻilekitulonika ʻoku ne poupouʻi ʻa e NHIS;

(c) fakapapauʻi ʻa e tonu mo lelei ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku ne maʻu ʻo ngaueʻaki ʻi he NHIS ʻaki hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaahi ngaue ke fakapapauʻi ʻaki ʻa e lelei ʻo e fakamatala ʻi he fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku ne maʻu mei he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui; mo Kupu 68 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 64 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(d) fakahoko ʻa e ngaahi ʻanalaiso ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku lava ʻo aʻu ki ai ʻo fou ʻi he NHIS kae lava ke tuku atu ha ngaahi fakatokangamohangaahifakamanatukihetauhifakamatalakihemoʻui ki hono ngaueʻaki ʻi hono fakahoko ʻo e tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ki ha niʻihi.

(3) ‘I he tanaki atu ki hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaahi mafai, ngaahi ngaue mo e ngaahi fatongia ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he Konga ni pea ʻi he Konga 5, kuo pau ke fakahoko ʻe he Potungaue ha ngaahi mafai, ngaahi ngaue pe ngaahi fatongia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

#### 68 Ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ki he NHIS

Kuo pau kefaipauʻa e Potungaue ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ko ʻeni ʻihono faʻumotauhi ʻo e NHIS –

(a) ke fakahoko ha ngaahi sitepu feʻunga ke fakangatangata ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku ne maʻu ki he meʻa ʻoku fiemaʻu ki hono faʻu mo tauhi ʻo e NHIS;

(b) kuo pau ʻe ʻikai fakangofua ʻene kau ngaue pe ha taha kehe ʻoku ne fakafofongaʻi ia ke ne vakai, tokangaʻi pe ngaue kehe ange ki he fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui kuo maʻu mei he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui, tuku kehe kapau ʻoku loto ʻa e taha ngaue pe taha ʻoku ne fakafofongaʻi ʻa e Potungaue ke faipau ki he ngaahi fakangatangata ʻoku ngaueʻaki ki he Potungaue;

(c) kuo pau ke ne fakaʻatā ki ha taha pea ki he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui takitaha ʻoku ne ʻomi ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ki ai –

(i) ko ha fakamatala mahino ngofua ʻo e NHIS, kau ai hano fakamatalaʻi fakalukufua ʻo e ngaahi maluʻi fakangaue, fakatekinikale mo fakafisikale kuo fokotuʻu ke –

(A) maluʻi mei ha kaihaʻa, mole, mo e hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻikai fakamafaiʻi ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku lava ʻo hū ki ai ʻo fou ʻi he NHIS;

(B) maluʻi ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku lava ke hū ki ai ʻo fou ʻi he NHIS mei he hiki tatau, liliu pe fakaʻauha ʻikai fakamafaiʻi; pea

(C) maluʻi ʻa e falalaʻanga, malu mo e fakapulipuli ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku lava ʻo hū ki ai ʻo fou ʻi he NHIS; pea

(ii) ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni, fakahinohino mo e tuʻutuʻuni ngaue ʻa e Potungaue ʻoku ngaueʻaki ki he fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku lava ʻo hū ki ai ʻo fou ʻi he NHIS, ki he tuʻunga ʻoku ʻikai ke fakaha ʻe he ngaahi meʻa ko eni ha fefakatauʻaki fakapulipuli pe Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 68 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 65 ko ha fakamatala fakapulipuli fakasaienisi, fakatekinikale, fakakomesiale pe ngaahi vā fakangaue;

(d) kuo pau –

(i) ke tauhi ha lekooti fakaʻilekitulonika ʻo e ngaahi meʻa kotoa pe ʻoku hoko ʻa ia ko e kotoa pe konga ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku lava ʻo hū ki ai ʻo fou ʻi he NHIS, ʻoku vakai ki ai, tokangaʻi pe ngaueʻaki kehe ange, pea fakapapauʻi ʻoku fakamahinoʻi ʻe he lekooti ʻa e taha ʻoku fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala, faʻahinga fakamatala ʻoku mamataʻi, tokangaʻi pe ngaueʻaki kehe ange, ko e kotoa ʻo e kakai kuo nau mamata, fakaʻaongaʻi pe ngaueʻaki kehe ange ʻa e fakamatala, mo e ʻaho, taimi mo e feituʻu ke mamataʻi ai, tokangaʻi, pe ngaueʻaki; pea

(ii) ʻi hano kole ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke ʻoatu ʻe he Potungaue ki he tauhi fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku lava ʻo aʻu ki ai ʻo fou ʻi he NHIS, ke tauhi ha lekooti fakaʻilekitulonika ʻo e ngaahi meʻa kotoa pe pea ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ki he tauhi fakamatala ʻo fou ʻi he NHIS, pea fakapapauʻi ʻoku fakamahinoʻi ʻe he lekooti ʻa e taha ko ia ko e tauhi fakamatala naʻa ne kole ʻa e fakamatala, ko e ʻaho mo e taimi naʻe ʻave ai ʻa e fakamatala, mo e feituʻu ʻoku ʻave ki ai ʻa e fakamatala;

(e) kuo pau ke ne ʻaotitaʻi mo vakaiʻi ʻa e ngaahi lekooti fakaʻilekitulonika ʻa ia ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ke ne tauhi ʻi he palakalafi (d);

(f) kuo pau, ʻi hano kole ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue, koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e Konga 8, ʻa e ngaahi lekooti fakaʻilekitulonika ʻoku tauhi ʻi he palakalafi (d);

(g) kuo pau, ʻi hano kole ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻoku ne fiemaʻu ʻa hono ʻaotitaʻi mo vakaiʻi ʻo ʻene faipau ki he Lao ni, ke ne ʻoatu ki he tauhi fakamatala pe ko ha fakafofonga ʻoku ne fakafofongaʻi ʻa e tauhi fakamatala, ʻa e ngaahi lekooti ʻoku tauhi ʻi he palakalafi (d);

(h) kuo pau ke ne fakahoko hano siviʻi ʻo e NHIS ʻo fekauʻaki mo e –

(i) ngaahi fakamanamana, matavaivai mo e ngaahi fakatuʻutamaki ki hemalumoefalalaʻangaʻoefakamatalataautahakihemoʻui; pea

(ii) founga ʻe ala uesia ai ʻe he NHIS ʻa e totonu ki he meʻa fakafoʻituitui ʻa e niʻihi ʻoku felaveʻi mo kinautolu ʻa e fakamatala;

(i) kuo pau ke ne fakaha, ʻi he ʻuluaki faingamalie fakapotopoto, ki he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui takitaha naʻa ne ʻoatu ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ki he Potungaue pe ko e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui naʻe ʻoatu ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui kuo kaihaʻasi pe mole pe kapau naʻe hū ki ai, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo ʻikai fakamafaiʻi;

(j) kuo pau ke ne – Kupu 69 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 66 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(i) fakaʻatā ke maʻu ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui takitaha naʻa ne ʻoatu ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ki he Potungaue ha tatau ʻo e ngaahi ola ʻo e sivi naʻe fakahoko ʻi he palakalafi (h) ʻa ia ʻoku felaveʻi mo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui naʻe ʻoatu ʻe he tauhi fakamatala; pea

(ii) fakaʻatā ki he kakai ha fakamatala fakanounou ʻo e ngaahi ola ʻo e sivi naʻe fakahoko ʻi he palakalafi (h);

(k) kuo pau ke ne fakapapauʻi ko ha faʻahi hono tolu ʻoku ne tauhi ke tokoni ʻi hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaahi ngaue ki he taumuʻa hono faʻu pe tauhi ʻo e NHIS ʻoku loto ke faipau ki he ngaahi fakangatangata mo e ngaahi makatuʻunga ʻoku fiemaʻu ke lava ʻa e Potungaue ke faipau ki he ngaahi fiemaʻu kotoa ko ʻeni;

(l) kuo pau ke ‘i ai pea faipau ki he ngaahi ngaue mo e ngaahi founga ngaue ki hetaumuʻa ke maluʻi‘aetotonu fakafoʻituitui‘o eniʻihi ‘a iako ‘enau fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ‘oku maʻu pea ki hono tauhi ‘a e fakapulipuli ‘o e fakamatala;

(m) kuo pau ke ʻi ai pea faipau ki he ngaahi ngaue mo e ngaahi founga ngaue kuo fakangofua ʻe he Minisita ke tali ʻaki ki he pe tokoniʻi ʻaki ha ngaue ki ha kole kuo fakahoko ʻe ha taha ʻi he Konga 5 ʻo fekauʻaki mo e lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ha taha ʻa ia ʻoku lava ʻa e hū ki ai ʻo fou ʻi he NHIS; pea

(n) kuo pau ke faipau ia ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni kehe ʻi hono tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

#### 69 Ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e Minisita

(1) ʻE ngofua ke fakahoko ʻe he Minisita ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻo fekauʻaki mo hono fakahoko hono ngaahi mafai, ngaahi ngaue mo e ngaahi fatongia ‘i he Konga ni, pea kuo pau ke faipau ʻa e Potungaue ki he ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e Minisita.

(2) Kimuʻa pea fakahoko ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he kupu siʻi (1), kuo pau ko e Minisita ke ne –

(a) fakahū atu ha fakaangaanga ʻo e tuʻutuʻuni ki he komiti faleʻi koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke toe vakaiʻi mo fakahoko ha ngaahi fokotuʻu ʻi he tuʻutuʻuni fakaangaanga; pea

(b) fakakaukau ki he ngaahi fokotuʻu, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻa ia naʻe faʻu ʻe he komiti faleʻi pea liliu ʻa e tuʻutuʻuni kapau ʻoku pehe ʻe he Minisita ʻoku feʻunga ke fakahoko ia.

(3) Kuo pau ‘e fakaʻatā ʻe he Minisita ʻa e komiti faleʻi ha vahaʻa taimi ʻo ʻikai toe siʻi hifo ʻi he ʻaho ʻe 30 ki he ngaahi taumuʻa toe vakaiʻi mo e fokotuʻu ʻi he kupu siʻi(2),tuku kehekapau ʻokutui ʻa eMinisitaʻoku ʻi ai ha ngaahi tukunga fakavavevave ʻoku kau ai ha fakatuʻutamaki ki he totonu ki he meʻa Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 70 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 67 fakafoʻituitui pe ko e fakapulipuli ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, ʻa ia ʻe ngofua ki he Minisita ke ne fakanounouʻi ʻa e vahaʻa taimi toe vakaiʻi ki he komiti faleʻi ‘o ʻoua ‘e siʻi hifo ʻi he ‘aho ngaue ʻe nima.

VAHE 1 – HŪ, TANAKI, NGAUEʻAKI, FAKAHA ʻE HE TAUHI FAKAMATALA

#### 70 Ngaahi fakangatangata ʻi he tanaki

(1) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai tanaki ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo fou ʻi he NHIS tuku kehe ki he taumuʻa –

(a) ke ʻoatu pe tokoni ʻi hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ki he taha ʻoku fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala; pe

(b) ke tamateʻi pe fakasiʻisiʻi ʻa e faingamalie lahi ke hoko ha lavea lahi ki ha taha pe niʻihi, ʻa ia ʻoku tui ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ʻi he ngaahi ʻuhinga lelei ʻoku fiemaʻu ʻa e tanaki fakamatala ki he taumuʻa ko ʻeni

(2) ʻE ngofua ke hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki mo fakaha ʻa e ngaahi ʻelemeniti ʻo e fakamatala kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke fakahaaʻi makehe ha taha kae lava ke tanaki ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu siʻi (1).

(3) ʻE ngofua ke ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ‘a e fakamatala ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻoku ne tanaki ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu siʻi (1)(a) koeʻuhi ko ha taumuʻa ʻa ia ʻoku fakangofua pe tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Lao ni ʻa e tauhi fakamatala, ke ne ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui.

(4) Neongo ha tuʻutuʻuni kehe ʻi he Lao ni pe ko e Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, ʻe ngofua ke toki ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻoku ne tanaki ha fakamatalataautaha ki he moʻuiʻihe kupu siʻi (1)(b) ʻa efakamatala koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ʻa ia naʻe tanaki ai ʻa e fakamatala.

(5) Kapau ʻe kole ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke ʻoange ʻe he Potungaue ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ki he tauhi fakamatala ʻo fou ʻi he NHIS pea

‘oku ‘oatu ‘e he Potungaue ‘a e fakamatala naʻe kole, kuo pau ke faipau ‘a e tauhi fakamatala ki he ngaahi fatongia ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ai ʻi he kupu 14(1) ʻo fekauʻaki mo e fakamatala kuo ʻave, ʻo tatau pe kuo vakai ki ai, tokangaʻi pe ngaueʻaki kehe ange ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻa e fakamatala.

(6) Fakatatau ki he ngaahi fakaʻatā mo ha ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni makehe, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, pea tanaki atu ki ha fanongonongo kuo tuʻutuʻuni ke fakahoko ʻi ha hoko ha ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo ʻikai fakamafaiʻi ʻi he kupu 14(2) mo e (3), kapau ʻoku tanaki ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha ʻo ʻikai ha mafai ʻo fou ʻi he NHIS, kuo pau ko e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui naʻa ne fakahoko ʻa e tanaki fakamatala ʻo ʻikai fakamafai ke ne fakaha ia ki he taha ʻi he ʻuluaki Kupu 71 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 68 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to faingamalie lelei ʻa e tanaki fakamatala ʻo ʻikai ha fakamafai, pea fakakau ʻi he fanongonongo ha fakamatala ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e totonu ʻa e taha ko ia ke fakahoko ha launga ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻi he Konga 8.

#### 71 Vakaiʻi ʻo e tuʻunga malu fakafaitoʻo

Neongo ʻa e kakano ʻo ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, ʻe ngofua ke ngaueʻaki ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻe ha sisitemi ʻoku tauhi ʻe he Potungaue pea ʻa ia ʻoku ne toʻo mai, ngaueʻaki pe fakatahatahaʻi ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku lava ke hū ki ai ʻo fou ʻi he NHIS ke ʻoatu ʻaki ʻa e ngaahi fakatokanga ki he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻo fekauʻaki mo e faingamalie ke fakatuʻutamaki ʻa e ngaahi fekauʻaki fakafaitoʻo.

#### 72 Fakaha ʻo e fakamatala ki he kau polisi

(1) Kapau kuo fakakakato, ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni kuo tuʻutuuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻe ngofua ke ʻoatu ʻe he Potungaue ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku lava ʻo hū ki ai ʻo fou ʻi he NHIS ki he kau polisi ʻo felaveʻi mo ha fakatotolo ʻoku fakahoko ʻi he Lao ki he Ngaahi Hia [Vahe 4.04] kapau ʻoku ʻi ai ha mate ʻoku ʻikai fakapapauʻi hono tupuʻanga.

(2) ʻE ngofua ke tanaki ʻe ha ʻOfisa Faitoʻo Pule ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ‘o fou ‘i he NHIS koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ‘oku felaveʻi ki he ‘enau ngaahi ngaue ʻi he Lao ki he Moʻui ʻa e Kakai [Vahe 12.15].

(3) ʻE ngofua ke ʻoatu pe tanaki ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo fakatatau ki he kupu siʻi (1) pe (2) neongo ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he kupu 70, 71 mo e 72 ‘o e Lao ni. 73 ʻOatu ʻo e fakamatala koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa kehe mei he tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui

(1) Neongo ha tuʻutuʻuni kehe ʻi he Lao ni pe ko e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni, ʻe ngofua ke tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Minisita ʻa hono fakaha ‘o e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui

‘a ia ‘oku lava ke hū ki ai ‘o fou ‘i he NHIS ki ha taha, ‘o hange ko e Minisita naʻa ne tauhi pe puleʻi ʻa e fakamatala, kapau –

(a) naʻe kole ʻe ha taha ke fakaha ʻe he Minisita ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo fakatatau mo e kupu 43(1)(b), 43(2), 48 pe 49;

(b) ko e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui naʻe kole ʻe he taha ko ia naʻe ʻoatu ia ki he Potungaue ʻi he Konga ni ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ‘o lahi ange ʻi he toko taha;

(c) ko e Minisita kuo ne –

(i) fakahū atu ʻa e kole ki he komiti faleʻi;

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 74 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 69

(ii) ʻoatu ʻa e ʻaho ʻe 30 ki he komiti faleʻi ke vakai ai ki he kole mo fakahoko ha ngaahi fokotuʻu ki he Minisita; pea

(iii) fakakaukauʻi ʻa e ngaahi fokotuʻu, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻa ia kuo fakahoko ʻe he komiti faleʻi; pea

(d) kuo fakapapauʻi ʻe he Minisita ko hono fakaha ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui kuo pau ke fakatatau ia ki he kupu 43(1)(b), 43(2), 48, pe 49

‘o e Lao ni.

(2) ʻE ngofua ke fakanounou ʻe he Minisita ʻa e vahaʻa taimi ʻi he kupu siʻi

(1)(c)(ii) kapau –

(a) ʻi he lau ʻa e Minisita, ʻoku fiemaʻu ia ʻe he fakavavevave ʻo e meʻa ʻoku hoko; pea

(b) koekolekihonofakahaʻoefakamatalataautahakihemoʻuiʻofakatatau mo e kupu 43(2).

(3) Kuo pau ke faipau ʻa e Potungaue ki ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he kupu ni.

(4) ‘E ngofua ke fakamahino pau ‘e ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he kupu ni ʻa e fotunga, founga mo e vahaʻa taimi ke fakaha ai ʻa e fakamatala ‘a ia ʻoku tuʻunga ai ʻa e tuʻutuʻuni ke fakaha.

(5) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Minisita ʻa hono fakaha ʻo ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu ni kapau ʻe fakakakato ʻe ha fakamatala kehe ʻa e taumuʻa hono fakaha.

(6) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Minisita ʻa hono fakaha ʻo ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo lahi ange ʻi hono fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ke fakakakato ʻa e taumuʻa hono fakaha.

#### 74 Komiti faleʻi

(1) Kuo pau ke fokotuʻu ‘e he Minisita ha komiti faleʻi koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ke fakahoko ʻa e ngaahi fokotuʻu ki he Minisita fekauʻaki mo e –

(a) ngaahi ngaue mo e founga ngaue kuo pau ke fokotuʻu ʻe he Potungaue ke maluʻi ʻaki ʻa e totonu ki he meʻa fakafoʻituitui ʻa e niʻihi ʻa ia ʻoku ne maʻu ʻenau fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui mo tauhi ʻaki ʻa e fakapulipuli ʻo e fakamatala;

(b) ngaahi ngaue mo e founga ngaue kuo pau ke fokotuʻu ʻe he Potungaue ke ne tali ʻaki pe tokoni ki hono tali ʻo ha kole kuo fakahoko ʻe ha taha ʻi he Konga 5 ki ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku felaveʻi mo e taha ko ia pea ʻoku lava ʻo hū ki ai ʻo fou ʻi he NHIS;

(c) ngaahi maluʻi fakangaue, fakatekinikale mo fakafisikale kuo pau ke fokotuʻu ʻe he Potungaue ke maluʻi ʻa e totonu ki he meʻa fakafoʻituitui ʻa e kakai ʻa ia ʻoku ne maʻu ʻenau fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui mo tauhi ʻaki ʻa e fakapulipuli ʻo e fakamatala;

Kupu 75 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 70 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(d) fatongia ʻo e Potungaue ʻi hono tokoniʻi ʻo ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke fakakakato hono ngaahi fatongia ke ʻoatu ʻa e fanongonongo ki ha niʻihi ʻi he kupu 14(2) mo e kupu 70(6) ʻi he taimi ʻoku kaihaʻasi pe mole pe kuo tanaki, ngaueʻaki pefakahaʻo ʻikai hamafai ʻa efakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku lava ʻo hū ki ai ‘i hono ngaueʻaki ‘a e NHIS;

(e) fakahoko ʻo e fanongonongo ʻi he taimi ʻoku kaihaʻasi pe mole pe kuo hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo ʻikai ha mafai ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku lava ʻo hū ki ai ‘aki hono ngaueʻaki ‘a e NHIS;

(f) ngaahi ngaue mo e founga ngaue kuo pau ke fokotuʻu ʻe he Potungaue kengaueatuʻakiki hekaveingaʻoetuʻungatangatapefefineʻealahoko ʻo fekauʻaki mo hono hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui;

(g) ha meʻa ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ai ʻi he Konga ni pe ʻi he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni ʻa ia ʻoku lava ke kaveinga ʻaki ha fokotuʻu ʻa e komiti faleʻi; mo

(h) ha meʻa kehe kuo ʻave ʻe he Minisita ki he komiti faleʻi.

(2) Fakatatau ki he kupu siʻi (5) mo e ngaahi kupu kehe ʻo e Konga ni, kuo pau ke tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Minisita ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e ngaue ʻo e komiti faleʻi, kau ki ai ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ‘o e ngaue ʻo fekauʻaki mo e fepakipaki ʻa e kaunga, memipa ʻi he komiti mo hono fokotuʻutuʻu mo puleʻi ʻo e komiti.

(3) Kuo pau ke fokotuʻu ʻe he Minisita ʻa e kau memipa ʻo e komiti faleʻi ʻo fakatatau mo e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, ʻi hono tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(4) Kuo pau ke ʻoatu ʻe he Potungaue ʻa e tokoni fakangaue ki he komiti faleʻi.

(5) Kuo pau ko e kau memipa ʻo e komiti faleʻi ʻe kau ki ai ʻo ʻikai toe siʻi hifo ʻi he peseti ʻe 50 ko e kakai fefine.

#### 75 Maluʻi ʻo e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui mei ha moʻua

Ka ʻi ai ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui, ʻi heʻene fakahoko fatongia ʻi he loto lelei, ʻoku ne ʻoatu ha fakamatala taautaha ki hemoʻui ki hePotungaue ʻaki hono ngaueʻaki

‘a e NHIS ʻe ʻikai ke ne ala moʻua ki ha ngaahi maumau ko e tupu mei ha –

(a) vakai pe tokangaʻi ʻoku ʻikai fakamafai ʻo e fakamatala kuo ʻoatu, pe ko hano ngaueʻaki ʻoku ʻikai fakamafai ʻo e fakamatala kuo ʻoatu, ʻe he Potungaue, ko ʻene kau ngaue pe ko ha taha kehe ʻoku ne fakafofongaʻi ia; pe

(b) tanaki ʻoku ʻikai fakamafai ʻo e fakamatala kuo ʻoatu ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ʻe taha ki he moʻui.

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 76 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 71

## KONGA 8 – PULEʻI MO HONO FAKAHOKO

VAHE 1 – NGAAHI LAUNGA, VAKAIʻI, MO E NGAAHI SIVI

#### 76 Launga ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue

(1) Ka ʻi ai ha taha ʻoku ʻi ai ha ngaahi ʻuhinga fakapotopoto ke tui ʻoku ʻi ai ha taha kehe kuo ne maumauʻipe ʻoku ʻamanaki ke ne maumauʻi ha kupu ʻo e Lao ni pe ko hono Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni ʻe ngofua ke ne fakahoko ha launga ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue.

(2) Ko ha launga ʻoku fakahoko ʻe ha taha ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) kuo pau ke fakahoko ia ʻi he tohi pea kuo pau ke faile ʻi loto ʻi he –

(a) taʻu ʻe taha hili ʻa e fuofua taimi naʻe fakatokangaʻi ai ʻe he taha launga ʻa e kaveinga ‘o e launga ko ia pe naʻe totonu fakapotopoto ke fakatokangaʻi ʻe he taha launga, ko fe pe ʻoku nounou ange; pe

(b) ko e ha pe ʻa e loloa ʻo e vahaʻa taimi ʻe fakangofua ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue kapau ʻe fiemalie ʻa e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻoku ʻikai ke hoko ai ha maumau ki ha taha.

(3) Kuo pau ko ha launga ʻoku fakahoko ʻe ha taha ʻi he kupu 59(9) pe 60(6) pe 60(11) pea kuo pau ke faile ʻi loto ʻi he mahina ʻe ono mei he taimi ʻoku ʻikai ke tali pe ʻoku lau kuo ʻikai tali ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa e kole ʻa e taha ko ia ʻa ia ʻoku lave ki ai ʻi he kupu siʻi ʻoku ngaueʻaki.

(4) Kuo pau ke fakaha ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia ko e kaveinga ʻo ha launga, ʻa e lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui felaveʻi mo e taha ko ia ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue koeʻuhi ko e taumuʻa ʻo e launga pe ko hono ngaueʻaki, kapau ʻe tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue.

#### 77 Tali ʻa e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue

(1) ʻI hono maʻu ha launga kuo fakahoko ʻi he Lao ni, ʻe ngofua ke fakaha ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ki he taha ko ia ʻoku fekauʻaki mo e launga ʻoku fakahoko, ki he natula ʻo e launga pea –

(a) fakaʻekeʻeke ki he naahi founga, kehe ange mei he launga, ʻa ia ʻoku ngaueʻaki ʻe he taha launga pe kuo ne ngaueʻaki ke solova ʻaki ʻa e kaveinga ʻo e launga; pe

(b) tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he taha launga ke feinga ʻo solova, ʻi loto ʻi he vahaʻa taimi naʻe fakamahino pau ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue, fakataha mo e taha ‘a ia

‘oku fekauʻaki mo ia ‘a e launga.
