(2) Kapau ʻoku fakahoko ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ha ngaue fakalao ‘a ia ‘oku fakamatalaʻi ‘i he kupu siʻi (1)(b) ka ʻoku ʻikai solova ʻi loto ʻi he vahaʻa taimi Kupu 78 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 72 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to kuo fakamahino pau ‘e he ‘Ofisa Pule Ngaue, kuo pau ‘e ‘ikai maumauʻi ‘a e ngaahi totonu mo e ngaahi ngaue ‘a e ngaahi faʻahi ‘i he Lao ni.

(3) Kapau ʻoku ʻikai fakahoko ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ha ngaue fakalao ʻa ia ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi (1), ʻe ngofua ke ne toe vakaiʻi ‘a e kaveinga ʻo ha launga kuo fakahoko ʻi he Lao ni kapau ʻoku ne fiemalie ʻoku ʻi ai ha ngaahi ʻuhinga lelei ke fakahoko ai ia.

(4) ʻE ngofua ke tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ke ʻoua ʻe toe vakaiʻi ha launga koeʻuhi ko e –

(a) taha ʻoku tuʻunga ai hono fakahoko ʻo e launga kuo ne ʻomi ha tali feʻunga ki he launga;

(b) launga kuo fakahoko pe ‘e lava ke fakahoko ngaue lelei ange ki ai, ʻi he kamataʻanga pe fakakatoa, ʻaki ha founga ngaue, kehe mei hono fakahoko ʻo e launga ʻi he Lao ni;

(c) vahaʻa taimi kuo ʻosi ʻi he vahaʻa ʻo e ʻaho naʻe ʻohake ai ʻa e kaveinga ʻo e launga mo e ʻaho naʻe fakahoko ai ʻa e launga ko ha vahaʻa taimi ia kapau ʻe ʻi ai ha toe vakaiʻi ʻi he kupu ni ʻe hoko ai ha maumau lahi ki ha taha;

(d) tahalaungaʻokuʻikaiʻiaihaʻanekaungataautahafeʻungakihekaveinga ʻo e launga; pe

(e) launga ʻoku launoa pe fakatupuʻita pe naʻe fakahoko ʻi he loto kovi.

(5) ʻI hono fakapapauʻi ʻe ʻikai ke toe vakaiʻi ‘a e launga, kuo pau ke ʻoatu ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ha fanongonongo ʻo e tuʻutuʻuni ki he taha launga pea kuo pau ke fakamahino pau ʻi he fanongonongo ʻa e ʻuhinga ki he tuʻutuʻuni ko ia.

(6) ʻI hono fakapapauʻi ke toe vakaiʻi ʻa e launga, kuo pau ke ʻoatu ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ha fanongonongo ʻo e tuʻutuʻuni ki he taha ʻoku tuʻunga ai hono fakahoko ʻo e launga.

#### 78 Ngaahi mafai ke sivi

(1) ʻI hono fakahoko ha toe vakaiʻi ʻi he kupu 77 ‘o e Lao ni, ʻe ngofua ke hū pea sivi ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ha ngaahi feituʻu ʻo fakatatau ki he kupu ni kapau –

(a) ʻoku ʻi ai ha ngaahi ʻuhinga lelei ke tui ʻa e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ko e –

(i) taha ʻa ia ʻoku fakahoko ki ai ʻa e launga pe ko e taha ʻoku ʻoʻona ʻa e ngaahi ngaue ʻoku lolotonga toe vakaiʻi ʻoku ne ngaueʻaki ʻa e ngaahi feituʻu ki ha taumuʻa ʻa ia ʻoku felaveʻi mo e kaveinga ʻo e launga pe ko e toe vakaiʻi, koe ha pe ʻe hoko; pea

(ii) ko e ngaahi feituʻu ʻoku ʻi ai ha ngaahi lekooti pe ngaahi tohi ngaue kehe ʻoku felaveʻi mo e kaveinga ʻo e launga pe ko e toe vakaiʻi, ko e ha pe ʻe hoko; pea Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 78 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 73

(b) ʻokufakahokoʻeheʻOfisaPuleNgauehasivikoeʻuhikoetaumuʻahono fakapapauʻi pe kuo maumauʻi ʻe he taha ko ia pe ʻoku ʻamanaki ke ne maumauʻi ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻo e Lao ni pe ko hono Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(2) ʻI hono fakahoko ha toe vakaiʻi ʻi he kupu 77 ‘o e Lao ni, ʻe ngofua ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ha ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ke ne –

(a) fekaukeʻomaiʻaengaahitohi,lekootipengaahitohingaueʻokufelaveʻi mo e kaveinga ʻo e toe vakaiʻi pe ngaahi tatau ʻo e ngaahi kongokonga mei he ngaahi tohi, lekooti pe ha ngaahi tohi ngaue kehe;

(b) fakaʻekeʻeke ki he kotoa ʻo e fakamatala, ngaahi lekooti, ngaue fakaefakamatala ʻo e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui mo ha ngaahi meʻa kehe ʻa ia ʻoku felaveʻi mo e kaveinga ʻo e toe vakaiʻi;

(c) fekau ke ʻomai ke sivi ha meʻa pe kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he palakalafi (b); pe

(d) ngaueʻaki ha tauhiʻanga fakamatala, sisitemi pe meʻangaue ki hono ngaueʻaki pe toʻo fakamatala ʻa ia ʻoku ʻoʻona ʻe he taha ʻoku fakatotoloʻi ke lava ʻo maʻu ha lekooti ʻi ha fotunga ʻoku lava ʻo lau ʻo e ngaahi lekooti pe ha ngaahi tohi ngaue ʻoku fekauʻaki mo e kaveinga ʻo e toe vakaiʻi.

(3) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai ha taha te ne fakafeʻatungiaʻi ʻa e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ʻa ia ʻoku ne ngaueʻaki ʻa e ngaahi mafai ʻi he kupu ni pe ʻoatu ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ha fakamatala loi mo takihala.

(4) Kuo pau ke fakahoko ʻi he tohi ha fekau ki he ngaahi tohi, lekooti pe tohi ngaue pe ngaahi tatau ʻo e ngaahi kongokonga mei ai ʻi he kupu siʻi (2), pea kuo pau

‘e kau ki ai ha fakamatala ʻo e natula ʻo e ngaahi meʻa ʻa ia kuo tuʻutuʻuni ke ʻoatu.

(5) Kapau ʻe fakahoko ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ha fekau ki ha meʻa ʻi he kupu siʻi (2), kuo pau ke ʻoatu ia ʻe he taha ʻoku ne tauhi ʻa e meʻa ko ia ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai pea, ʻi hono kole ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai, kuo pau ke ʻoatu ha faʻahinga tokoni ʻoku fiemaʻu, kau ki ai ha tauhiʻanga fakamatala, sisitemi pe meʻangaue ki hono ngaueʻaki pe toʻo fakamatala ke faʻu ha lekooti ʻi ha fotunga ʻoku lava ke lau, kapau ko e fekau ke ʻoange ha tohi ngaue.

(6) Ka ʻi ai ha taha ʻoku ne ʻoatu ha ngaahi lekooti pe ha tohi ngaue kehe ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai, ʻo kehe mei he meʻa naʻe fiemaʻu ki hono tokangaekina lolotonga ʻo e moʻui ʻo ha taha, ʻe ngofua ke toʻo ia ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai, ʻaki hono ʻoatu ha tohi tali totongi kuo tohi, toʻo ia pea ʻe ngofua ke toe vakaiʻi pe hiki tatau ha taha ʻo e ngaahi meʻa kapau ʻoku ʻikai lava ʻa e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ke toe vakaiʻi pea hiki tatau ia ʻi he ngaahi feituʻu kuo hū ki ai ʻa e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai.

(7) Kuo pau ke fakahoko ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai hano toe vakaiʻi pe hiki tatau ʻo e ngaahi tohi ngaue fakataha mo hano fanongonongo Kupu 78 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 74 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to fakapotopoto, pea kuo pau hili hano toe vakaiʻi pe hiki tatau ke fakafoki ʻa e ngaahi tohi ngaue ki he taha naʻa ne ʻoatu ia.

(8) Neongo ʻa e kupu siʻi (2), kuo pau ʻe ʻikai sivi ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe

‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ha lekooti ʻo e, tuʻutuʻuni ha fakamatala fakamoʻoni ʻo e, pe fakaʻekeʻeke ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo ʻikai ha fakangofua ʻa e taha ʻa ia ʻoku felaveʻi mo ia tukukehe ʻi he ngaahi tukunga kuo ʻuhiga ki ai ʻi he kupu siʻi (9).

(9) ʻE ngofua ke sivi ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ha lekooti ʻo e, tuʻutuʻuni ha fakamatala fakamoʻoni ʻo e, pe fakaʻekeʻeke ki he fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo ʻikai ha fakangofua ʻo e taha ʻa ia ʻoku felaveʻi mo ia kapau –

(a) kuo tomuʻa tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ʻokufakapotopotokefakahokoia,ʻofakatatau ki hangaahimakatuʻunga pe fakangatangata kuo fakamahino pau ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai, ʻa ia kuo pau ke kau ki ai ha fakangatangata ʻi he taimi, ke lava ʻo fakahoko ʻa e toe vakaiʻi pea ko e lelei fakalukufua ʻa hono fakahoko ‘a e toe vakaiʻi, ʻoku fakamoʻoniʻi ‘a hono tukuange ʻo e maʻu mai ʻo e fakangofua ʻa e taha ko ia ʻi he ngaahi tukunga; pea

(b) kuo ʻoatu ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ha fakamatala ki he taha ʻoku ne tauhi pe puleʻi ʻa e lekooti ke sivi, pe ko e fakamatala fakamoʻoni pe fakamatala ke fakaʻekeʻeke ki he, fakaha ʻa e tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ʻi he palakalafi (a) fakataha mo e ngaahi ʻuhinga kuo hiki nounou mo ha ngaahi fakangatangata mo e ngaahi makatuʻunga kuo fakamahino ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai.

(10) ʻE ngofua ke fakangofua ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ha taha ke ʻi ai lolotonga hono fakahoko ʻe ha taha kehe ʻa e ngaahi fakamatala ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai pe ke ne lava ʻo hū ki ai tukukehe kapau ʻe hoko ia ke ʻilo ai ʻa e –

(a) kakano ʻo ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, ʻa ia ʻoku pehee ʻe ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻoku ʻi ai ʻene totonu ke ʻikai tali ha kole ke hū ki ai, ʻa ia kuo fakahoko ʻi he kupu 58 ‘o e Lao ni; pe

(b) fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku ʻikai ‘i ai ha totonu ki ai ʻa ha taha ke kole ʻa e hū ki ai ʻi kupu 58 ‘o e Lao ni.

(11) Kapau kuo vaheʻi ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue hono ngaahi mafai ʻi he kupu ni ki ha ʻofisa pe taha ngaue ʻo e Potungaue, kuo pau ke ʻoatu ʻe he ʻofisa pe taha ngaue ʻoku ne ngaueʻaki ʻa e ngaahi mafai, ʻi hano kole, ʻa e tohi fakamoʻoni ʻo e vaheʻi ʻa ia kuo fakamoʻoni hingoa ki ai ʻa e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue.

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 79 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 75

#### 79 Ngaahi mafai ʻo e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai

(1) ‘I he hili hono fakahoko ha toe vakaiʻi ʻi he kupu 77, ʻe ngofua ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai –

(a) kapau ʻoku felaveʻi ʻa e toe vakaiʻi mo ha launga ki ha kole ʻa ha taha ʻi he kupu 58(1) ‘o e Lao ni ke hū ki ha lekooti ʻo ha fakamatala taautaha ki hemoʻui,kenetuʻutuʻuni ki hetauhifakamatalaki hemoʻuifekauʻaki mo e launga naʻe fakahoko ke foaki ki he taha ko ia ʻa e hū ki he lekooti kuo kole;

(b) kapau ʻoku felaveʻi ʻa e toe vakaiʻi mo ha launga ki ha kole ʻa ha taha ʻi he kupu 60(1) ‘e Lao ni ki hono fakatonutonu ʻo ha lekooti ʻo ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, ke ne tuʻutuʻuni ki he tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo e taha naʻe fakahoko ki ai ha launga ke ne fakahoko ʻa e fakatonutonu kuo kole;

(c) ke tuʻutuʻuni ha taha ʻa ia kuo toe vakaiʻi ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe

‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ʻene ngaahi ngaue ke ne fakahoko ha ngaue kuo hilifaki ʻe he Lao ni pe ko e Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni;

(d) ke tuʻutuʻuni ha taha ʻa ia kuo toe vakaiʻi ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe

‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ʻene ngaahi ngaue ke taʻofi ʻene hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui kapau ʻoku pehe ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ʻoku hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻa e fakamatala ‘e he taha ko ia, ko e ha pe meʻa ʻoku hoko, pe ʻoku ʻamanaki ke ne fakahoko ia ʻo maumauʻi ai ʻa e Lao ni, ko e Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni pe ko ha aleapau kuo kau ki ai ʻi he Lao ni;

(e) ke ne tuʻutuʻuni ki ha taha ʻa ia kuo toe vakaiʻi ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ʻene ngaahi ngaue ke fakafoki, ʻave, pe fakaʻauha ʻa e ngaahi lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻa ia ʻoku pehe ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai naʻe hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻe he taha ko ia ʻo maumauʻi ai ʻa e Lao ni, ko e Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, pe ko ha aleapau kuo kau ki ai ʻi he Lao ni, ʻo kapau pe ko e fakafoki, ‘ave pe fakaʻauha ‘o e ngaahi lekooti ʻoku ʻamanaki fakapotopoto ʻe ʻikai uesia kovi ‘a hono fakahoko ʻo e tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui ki ha taha;

(f) ke ne faʻu ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻo tuʻutuʻuni ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻa ia kuo toe vakaiʻi ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ʻene ngaahi ngaue ke liliu, taʻofi pe kamata ha ngaue fakaefakamatala ʻa ia kuo fakamahino pau ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai, kapau ʻoku pehe ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ʻoku maumauʻi ʻe he ngaue fakaefakamatala ʻa e Lao ni pe ko e Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni;

(g) ke ne tu‘utu‘uni ha tauhi fakamatala ki he mo‘ui ‘a ia kuo toe vakai‘i ‘e he ‘Ofisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ‘ene ngaue ke fakahoko ha founga ngaue fakaefakamatala kuo fakamahino pau ‘e he ‘Ofisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai, kapau ‘oku pehe ‘e he ‘Ofisa Pule Ngaue Kupu 80 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 76 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ‘oku fiema‘u fakapotopoto ‘a e founga ngaue fakaefakamatala koe‘uhi ke fakakakato ai ‘a e faipau ki he Lao ni mo e Ngaahi Tu‘utu‘uni;

(h) ke ne tuʻutuʻuni ha taha ʻa ia ko ha fakafofonga ʻo ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui, ʻa ia kuo toe vakaiʻi ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ʻene ngaahi ngaue pea ko ha tuʻutuʻuni kuo faʻu ʻi ha palakalafi (a) ki he (g) ʻoku ne tuʻutuʻuni ke fakahoko ha ngaue fakalao pe fakaʻehiʻehi mei hono fakahoko ha ngaue fakalao, kapau ʻoku pehe ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ʻoku fiemaʻu ke faʻu ʻa e tuʻutuʻuni ki he fakafofonga ke fakapapauʻi ʻe faipau ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he tuʻutʻuni kuo faʻu ki he tauhi fakamatala;

(i) fakatataukihekupu80‘oeLaoni,tuʻutuʻunihatahaʻaiakuotoevakaiʻi ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ‘ofisa kuo fakamafai ʻe ne ngaahi ngaue ke ne totongi ha tautea fakangaue ʻi he mahuʻinga kuo fakaha ʻi he tuʻutuʻuni kapau ʻoku pehe ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue kuo maumauʻi ʻe he taha ko ia ʻa e Lao ni pe ko e Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni; pe

(j) ke ne fakahoko ʻa e ngaahi fokotuʻu ʻo fekauʻaki mo e ngaahi nunuʻa ki he totonu ki he meʻa fakafoʻituitui ʻo ha meʻa ʻa ia ʻoku tuʻunga ai ʻa e toe vakaiʻi.

(2) ʻE ngofua ke kau ‘i ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) ʻa e ngaahi tuʻunga ʻoku pehe ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻoku taau.

(3) Kuo pau ke ʻoatu ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ha tatau ʻo e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni, kau ki ai ʻa e ngaahi ʻuhinga ki hono faʻu ʻo e tuʻutuʻuni, ki he –

(a) taha launga mo e taha ʻoku fekauʻaki mo ia ʻa e launga kuo fakahoko;

(b) kotoa ʻo e kakai kehe ʻa ia ʻoku fakataumuʻa ki ai ʻa e tuʻutuʻuni;

(c) sino pe ngaahi sino ʻoku ʻi ai ʻenau totonu fakalao ke puleʻi pe vakai ki he ngaahi ngaue ʻa ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui; mo

(d) ha taha kehe ʻa ia ʻoku pehe ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻoku taau.

(4) Kapau, hili hono fakahoko ha vakaiʻi ʻi he kupu 77 ‘o e Lao ni, ʻoku ʻikai faʻu ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he kupu siʻi (1), kuo pau e ʻoatu ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ki he taha launga, kapau ʻoku ʻi ai, mo e taha ʻa ia naʻe vakaiʻi ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻene ngaahi ngaue ha fanongonongo ‘oku ne fakaha ‘a e ngaahi ‘uhinga ‘a e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ki he ʻikai faʻu ha tuʻutuʻuni.

#### 80 Ngaahi tautea fakangaue

(1) ʻE ngofua ke tuku atu ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻo tuʻutuʻuni ke totongi ʻe ha taha ha tautea fakangaue ʻi he kupu 79(1)(i) ‘o e Lao ni koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ke –

(a) poupouʻi ʻa e faipau ki he Lao ni mo e Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni; pe Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 81 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 77

(b) taʻofi ha taha mei hono toʻo, fakahangatonu pe ʻikai fakahangatonu, ha monuʻia fakaʻekonomika ko e ola ʻo hano maumauʻi ʻo e Lao ni pe ko e Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(2) Kuo pau ko e mahuʻinga ʻo e tautea fakangaue ki ha maumau lao ke –

(a) ha ai ʻa e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻoku ʻuhinga ki ai ʻi he kupu siʻi (1); pea

(b) fakamahino ia ‘i hono tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(3) Kuo pau ko ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻoku ne tuʻutuʻuni ha taha ke totongi ha tautea fakangaue ke –

(a) kau ki ai pe ʻoatu fakataha mo ha fakamatala ʻo e maumauʻi; pea

(b) fokotuʻu ʻa e mahuʻinga ʻo e tautea ke totongi mo fakamahino pau ʻa e taimi mo e founga ʻo e totongi.

(4) Ka ʻi ai ha taha kuo tuʻutuʻuni ke ne totongi ha tautea fakangaue kuo pau ke totongi ʻa e tautea paʻanga ki he paʻanga hū mai fakalukufua ‘o e Puleʻanga.

#### 81 Tangi koeʻuhi ko ha tuʻutuʻuni

(1) ‘E ngofua ke tangi ha taha kuo uesia ‘e ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e ‘Ofisa Pule Ngaue kuo fakahoko ‘i he kupu 79(1)(c) ki he (i), ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni ki he Minisita ʻi loto ʻi he ʻaho ʻe 21 hili hono maʻu ʻa e tatau ʻo e tuʻutuʻuni.

(2) Kuo pau ke ‘oatu ‘e he Minisita ha tuʻutuʻuni ki he tangi kuo fakahoko ‘i he kupu siʻi (1) ‘i loto ‘i he ‘aho ‘e 21 hili ‘a hono maʻu ‘a e tatau ‘o e tangi.

(3) ʻE ngofua ki ha taha ‘oku ‘ikai fiemalie ki ha tuʻutuʻuni kuo fakahoko ‘e he Minisita ‘i he kupu siʻi (2) ke ne tangi ‘a e tuʻutuʻuni ki he Fakamaauʻanga Lahi.

#### 82 Tuʻutuʻuni makehe ʻa e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue

(1) ‘I he hili hono fakahoko ha toe vakaiʻi ʻi he kupu 77 ‘o e Lao ni mo hono faʻu ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he kupu 79(1) ‘o e Lao ni, ʻe ngofua ke fakafoki pe liliu ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻa e tuʻutuʻuni pe faʻu ha tuʻutuʻuni makehe ʻi he kupu siʻi ko ia kapau ʻoku ʻiloʻi ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ha ngaahi moʻoniʻi meʻa foʻou fekauʻaki mo e kaveinga ‘o e toe vakaiʻi pe kapau ʻoku ʻi ai ha liliu lahi ʻi he ngaahi tukunga felaveʻi mo e kaveinga ke toe vakaiʻi.

(2) ʻI hono faʻu ha toe tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he kupu siʻi (1), kuo pau ke ʻoatu ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue hano tatau ki ha taha ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu 84(3)(a) ki he

(d) ‘o e Lao ni pea kuo pau ke kau ki ai ha tatau ʻo ha fanongonongo ʻoku ha ʻi ai –

(a) ʻa e ngaahi ʻuhinga ʻa e ‘Ofisa Pule Ngaue ki hono faʻu ʻo e tuʻutuʻuni; pea Kupu 83 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 78 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(b) kapau naʻe faʻu ʻa e tuʻutuʻuni ʻi ha taha ʻo e kupu 79(1)(c) ki he (i) ‘o e Lao ni, ko ha fakamtala ʻo e kakai naʻe uesia ʻe he tuʻutuʻuni ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e totonu ke tangi ʻa ia kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi (3).

(3) ʻE ngofua ke tangi ha taha kuo uesia ʻe ha tuʻutuʻuni kuo fakafoki, liliu pe faʻu ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻi ha taha ʻo e ngaahi kupu 79(1)(c) ki he (i) ‘o e Lao nikihetuʻutuʻunikiheFakamaauʻangaPolisiʻofekauʻakimohafehuʻifakalao ʻo fakatatau mo e ngaahi lao ʻo e Fakamaauʻanga ʻaki hano faile ʻo ha fanongonongo tangi ʻi loto ʻi he ʻaho ʻe 30 hili hono maʻu ʻo e tatau ʻo e tuʻutuʻuni pea ke ngaueʻaki ʻa e kupu 81 ‘o e Lao ni ‘oku ngaueʻaki ki he tangi.

VAHE 2 –ʻOFISA PULE NGAUE

#### 83 Ngaahi mafai fakalukufua

ʻI hono tanaki atu ki he ngaahi mafai ʻo e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ‘a ia ‘oku hā ʻi he kupu

#### 84 ‘o e Lao ni, ʻe ngofua ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ke –

(a) kau pe tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e fekumi ki he ngaahi meʻa ʻoku ne uesia hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e Lao ni;

(b) fakahoko ʻa e ngaahi polokalama fakaako maʻae kakai pea ʻoatu ha fakamatala fekauʻaki mo e Lao ni mo e fatongia mo e ngaahi ngaue ʻa e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue;

(c) maʻu ha ngaahi fakamatala mei he kakai fekauʻaki mo e ngaue ʻo e Lao ni;

(d) ʻi he kole ʻa ha taha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui, ‘oatu ha ngaahi fakamatala fekauʻaki mo e ngaahi founga ngaue fakaefakamatala totonu pe fokotuʻu ‘a e tauhi fakamatala; pe

(e) ʻihengaahitukungataau,fakamafaiʻihonotanakiʻoengaahifakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ‘oku fekauʻaki mo ha taha ʻi ha founga kehe ange mei he taha ko ia; 84 Vahe‘i ʻo e fatongia ʻE ngofua ke vahe‘i ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻi he tohi, ha ngaahi mafai, ngaue pe fatongia ʻo e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻi he Lao ni, ʻo kau ki ai ʻa e mafai ke faituʻutuʻuni ki ha ʻofisa pe ha taha ngaue ʻa e Potungaue.

#### 85 Ngaahi fakangatangata ki he fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui

(1) ʻE ngofua ke hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe tauhi ʻe he ‘Ofisa Pule Ngaue mo ha taha ʻoku ngaue ʻi he mafai ‘o e ‘Ofisa Pule Ngaue ‘a e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ‘i hono fakahoko ‘o ha ngaahi fatongia ‘i he Konga ni ‘o kapau pe ‘e

‘ikai ha fakamatala kehe te ne fakakakato ‘a e taumuʻa ‘a hono hū, tanaki, Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 85 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 79 ngaueʻaki pe tauhi ‘o e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui pea ʻikai ʻi ha toe tukunga kehe.

(2) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai ke hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe tauhi ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ‘i he lolotonga ‘a hono fakahoko ‘o e ngaahi fatongia ‘i he Konga ni, ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo lahi ange ʻi hono fiemaʻu fakapotopoto ke lava ʻa e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ke fakahoko ‘a e ngaahi fatongia ‘o e ‘Ofisa Pule Ngaue ʻo felaveʻi mo hono fakahoko ʻo e Lao ni pe ko ha fakatonutonu fakalao ʻoku fakahoko ʻi ai.

(3) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai fakaha ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ha fakamatala ʻoku ne ʻilo ki ai lolotonga hono ngaueʻaki hono ngaahi fatongia ʻi he Lao ni tuku kehe kapau –

(a) ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e fakaha koʻeuhi ko e taumuʻa ‘a hono ngaueʻaki ʻo e ngaahi fatongia ko ia;

(b) ‘oku felaveʻi ‘a e fakamatala ki ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui;

(c) ʻoku fakahoko ʻa e fakaha ki ha sino ʻoku ʻi ai ʻene totonu fakalao ke puleʻi pe vakaiʻi ʻa e ngaahi ngaue ʻa e tauhi fakamatala pea ʻoku pehe ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻoku totonu ʻa e fakaha;

(d) naʻe maʻu ‘e he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻa e fakamatala ʻi he kupu 78(9) ‘o e Lao ni pea ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e fakaha ʻi ha fakaʻilo ki ha hia ʻi he kupu 63 ʻo Lao ki he Ngaahi Hia [Vahe 4.04] ʻo fekauʻaki mo e fakamoʻoni fuakava; pe

(e) ‘oku fakahoko ‘a e fakaha ki he ‘Ateni Seniale, ʻoku fekauʻaki ʻa e fakamatala mo hono fakahoko ʻo ha hia ʻo maumau ai ha Lao pea ʻoku pehe ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e fakamoʻoni ʻo e hia pehe.

(4) Neongo ha meʻa ʻi he kupu siʻi (3), kuo pau ʻe ʻikai fakaha ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue –

(a) ha fakamatala ki he tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻoku ne ʻilo ki ai lolotonga hono ngaueʻaki hono ngaahi fatongia ʻi he Lao ni; pe

(b) ʻa e fakahaaʻi ʻo ha taha, kehe mei ha taha launga ʻi he kupu siʻi 76(1) ‘o e Lao ni, ʻa ia kuo ne ʻoatu ha fakamatala ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pea kuo ne kole ʻa e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ke tauhi ʻa e fakapulipuli ʻo e fakahaaʻi ʻo e taha ko ia.

(5) Kuo pau ke fakahoko ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻi ha toe vakaiʻi ʻi he kupu 77 ‘o e Lao ni mo ha fakamaauʻanga, fakatonutonuʻanga pe ko ha taha kehe, kau ki ai ʻa e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue, ʻi ha fakatonutonu fakalao ʻoku lave ki ai ʻi he kupu

#### 89 ‘o e Lao ni pe ko e kupu ni, ʻa e tokanga fakapotopoto kotoa pe, kau ki ai, ʻi

hano fiemaʻu, tali ʻo e ngaahi fakamatala ʻo ʻikai ha fanongonongo mo fakahoko ha ngaahi hopo ʻoku ʻikai fakaʻatā ki ha taha, ke fakaʻehiʻehi hono fakaha ʻo ha fakamatala ʻa ia ʻoku ʻi ai ʻa e totonu ʻa e tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ke ʻikai tali ha kole ke hū ki ai ʻa ia naʻe fakahoko ʻi he kupu 58 ‘o e Lao ni.

Kupu 86 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 80 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(6) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ke ne fakamoʻoni ʻi ha fakamaauʻanga pe ʻi ha fakatonutonu fakalao ʻoku natula faka-fakamaauʻanga ʻo fekauʻaki mo ha meʻa ʻoku ne ʻilo ʻi hono ngaueʻaki hono ngaahi fatongia ʻi he Lao ni ʻa ia ʻoku tapui ke ne fakaha ʻi he kupu siʻi (3) pe (4). 86 Fakaʻatā ʻE ʻikai ha ngaue pe ha fakatonutonu fakalao kehe koeʻuhi ko ha ngaahi maumau ʻe ngofua ke fakahoko ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ki ha –

(a) meʻa kuo fakahoko, lipooti pe lea ʻaki ʻi he loto lelei pea ʻi hono ngaueʻaki pe taumuʻa ngaueʻaki ha taha ‘enau ngaahi mafai pe ngaahi ngaue ʻi he Lao ni; pe

(b) ha taʻetokanga kuo tukuakiʻi pe maumau ʻi hono ngaueʻaki ʻo e loto lelei ʻi ha taha honau ngaahi mafai pe ngaue ʻi he Lao ni.

## KONGA 9 – FAKALUKUFUA

87 ʻIkai fakafepaki Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai lava ha taha ke ne fakamalolooʻi, tuku ki tuʻa fakataimi, fakahifo, tauteaʻi, fakakinaʻi pe fakafaingata‘aʻia kehe ange ha taha koeʻuhi –

(a) ko e taha ko ia, ʻi he ‘ene fakahoko ‘i he loto lelei pea tu‘unga ‘i he tui fakapotopoto, kuo ne fakaha ki he ‘Ofisa Pule Ngaue kuo maumauʻi ʻe ha taha kehe pe ʻoku ʻamanaki ke ne maumauʻi ha kupu ʻo e Lao ni pe ko hono Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni;

(b) ko e taha ko ia, ʻi he ‘ene fakahoko ‘i he loto lelei pea tu’unga ‘i he tui fakapotopoto,kuonefakahoko pekuonefakahahataumuʻa kefakahoko ha meʻa ʻa ia ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ke fakahoko ke taʻofi hano maumauʻi ʻe ha taha ha kupu ʻo e Lao ni pe ko hono Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni;

(c) ko e taha ko ia, ʻi he ‘ene fakahoko ‘i he loto lelei pea tu’unga ‘i he tui fakapotopoto, kuo ʻikai ke ne fie fakahoko pe kuo ne fakaha ʻa e taumuʻa ʻe ʻikai ke ne fie fakahoko ha meʻa ʻoku maumauʻi ai ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻo e Lao ni pe ko hono Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni; pe

(d) ʻoku tui ha taha ko e taha ko ia te ne fakahoko ha meʻa pe kuo fakamatalaʻi ʻi he palakalafi (a), (b) pe (c). 88 Fakaʻatā

(1) ʻE ʻikai ha ngaue pe fakatonutonu fakalao kehe koeʻuhi ko ha ngaahi maumau ʻe ngofua ke fakahoko ki ha tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui pe ha taha kehe koeʻuhi ko ha – Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 89 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 81

(a) meʻa kuo fakahoko, lipooti pe lea ʻaki, fakatouʻosi ʻi he loto lelei mo fakapotopoto ʻi he ngaahi tukunga, ʻi hono ngaueʻaki pe taumuʻa ngaueʻaki ki ai ha taha ‘enau ngaahi mafai pe ngaahi ngaue ʻi he Lao ni; pe

(b) ha taʻetokanga kuo tukuakiʻi pe maumau naʻe ʻuhinga lelei ʻi he ngaahi tukunga, ʻi hono ngaueʻaki ʻa e loto lelei ʻi ha taha ‘enau ngaahi mafai pe ngaue ʻi he Lao ni.

(2) ʻOku ʻikai fakaʻatā ʻe he kupu siʻi (1) ʻa e Kalauni mei ha moʻua ʻo fekauʻaki mo ha maumau kuo fakahoko ʻe ha taha ʻoku lave ki ai ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) ʻa ia ʻe fakatatau ki ai.

(3) Ka ʻi ai ha taha, ʻoku ne fakafofongaʻi pe fetongi ha taha, ʻoku ne fakangofua pe ʻikai fakangofua ha hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo e taha ko ia, fakahoko ha kole, ʻoatu ha fakahinohino pe fakahoko ha ngaue ʻe ʻikai ke ne ala moʻua ki ha ngaahi maumau ki hono fakahoko ia kapau ʻoku ngaue fakapotopoto pe ʻa e taha ko ia ʻi he ngaahi tukunga, ʻi he loto lelei pea fakatatau ki he Lao ni mo hono Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

(4) Tukukehe kapau ‘oku ʻikai ʻuhinga lelei ke fakahoko ia ʻi he ngaahi tukunga, ʻoku ʻi ai ‘a e totonu ʻa ha taha ke ne fakafalala ki he tonu ʻo ha fakamatala naʻe fakahoko ʻe ha taha kehe, ‘o fekau’aki mo ha tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ‘o e, pe hū ki he, fakamatala ʻi he Lao ni, ki he mafai ko e taha kehe ko ia –

(a) ko ha taha kuo fakamafaiʻi ke kole ‘a e hū ki ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu 58;

(b) ko ha taha ʻoku ʻi ai ʻene totonu ʻi he kupu 26 pe 27(1) ke loto ki he hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo fekauʻaki mo ha taha kehe;

(c) fakakakato ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻo e kupu 27(2)(b) mo e (c); pe

(d) maʻu ʻa e ngaahi tui ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu siʻi 27(5). 89 Ngaahi Hia

(1) ʻOku halaia ha taha ki hono fakahoko ʻo ha hia kapau ko e taha ko ia ʻoku ne –

(a) loto ke hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi hono maumauʻi ʻo e Lao ni;

(b) fakahoko ha kole ‘i he Lao ni, ‘i ha fakamatala loi, ke hū ki ha pe fakatonutonu ha lekooti ‘o e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui;

(c) loto ke maumauʻi ʻa e kupu 13;

(d) ʻi heʻene fekauʻaki mo e hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ‘o e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui pe hū ki ha lekooti ʻo ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, fakahoko ha fakamatala, ʻi heʻene ʻiloʻi ʻoku ʻikai moʻoni, ki he mafai ‘o e taha ko ia – Kupu 89 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 82 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(i) ko ha taha ʻoku ʻi ai ʻene totonu ke loto ki hono tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ‘o e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha kehe;

(ii) fakakakato ʻa e ngaahi fiemaʻu ʻa e kupu 27(2)(b) mo e (c);

(iii) maʻu ʻa e ngaahi tui ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he kupu 27(5); pe

(iv) ko ha taha ʻoku ʻi ai ʻenetotonu ke hū ki ha lekootiʻo efakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻi he kupu 57;

(e) fakaʻauha ha lekooti ʻo ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku tauhi pe puleʻi ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻaki ha taumuʻa ke kalo mei ha kole ke hū ki he lekooti kuo maʻu ʻe he tauhi fakamatala ʻi he kupu 58(1);

(f) loto ke fakaʻauha ha lekooti ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻo maumau ai ʻa e kupu 15;

(g) maumauʻi ʻa e kupu 36(2) pe (3) pe kupu 51(7) (a), (e) pe (f);

(h) loto ke fakafaingataʻiaʻi ʻaeʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ko hataha ʻoku ne ʻilo ʻoku leʻoleʻo ʻi he mafai ʻo e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻi hono fakahoko hono ngaahi fatongia ʻi he Lao ni;

(i) loto ke faʻu ha fakamatala loi ke takihalaʻi pe feinga ke takihalaʻi ʻa e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ko ha taha ʻoku ne ʻilo ʻoku leʻoleʻo ʻi he mafai ʻo e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻi hono fakahoko hono ngaahi fatongia ‘a e ‘Ofisa Pule Ngaue ʻi he Lao ni;

(j) loto ke ʻikai faipau ki ha tuʻutuʻuni kuo faʻu ʻe he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue pe ko ha taha ʻoku ne ʻilo ʻoku leʻoleʻo ʻi he mafai ʻo e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻi he Lao ni; pe

(k) maumauʻi ʻa e kupu 86.

(2) Ka ʻi ai ha taha ʻoku halaia ki ha hia ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) ʻoku ne ala moʻua, ʻi hano fakahalaiaʻi ia –

(a) kapau ko e taha ko ia ko ha taha fakaenatula, ki ha moʻua paʻanga ʻoku ʻikai laka hake ʻi he $200,000 pe ki ha vahaʻa taimi ngaue popula ʻo ʻikai lahi hake ʻi he taʻu ʻe 7, pe fakatouʻosi; pe

(b) kapau ko e taha ko ia ʻoku ʻikai ko ha taha fakaenatula, ki ha moʻua paʻanga ʻoku ʻikai laka hake ʻi he $1,000,000.

(3) Kapau ʻe fakahoko ʻe ha kautaha ha hia ʻi he Lao ni, ko e ʻofisa, memipa, taha ngaue kotoa pe pe ko ha fakafofonga kehe ʻo e kautaha naʻa ne fakamafaiʻi ʻa e hia, pe naʻa ne maʻu ʻa e mafai ke taʻofi ʻa hono fakahoko ʻo e hia ka naʻa ne fakaʻehiʻehi mei ai ʻi he ʻiloʻilo pau, ko ha faʻahi ia pea ʻoku halaia ʻi he hia pea ʻoku ne ala moʻua, ʻi hano fakahalaiaʻi, ki he tautea ʻo e hia ko ia, ʻo tatau ai pe pe kuo fakaʻilo pe fakahalaiaʻi ʻa e kautaha.

(4) ʻE ʻikai ha taha ʻe ala fakaʻilo ki ha hia ʻo maumau ai ʻa e Lao ni pe ko ha Lao kehe koeʻuhi ko e faipau ki ha tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue ʻi he Lao ni.

Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 90 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 83

(5) Kuo pau ʻe ʻikai kamata ha talatalaaki hia ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) ʻo ʻikai loto ki ai ʻa e ʻAteni Seniale pe ko e fakafofonga ‘o e ‘Ateni Seniale.

(6) ʻI ha talatalaaki hia ki ha hia ʻi he kupu siʻi (1) ʻi heʻene felaveʻi mo ha fakatotolo ki ha hia ʻi he malumalu ʻo e Lao ni, ʻe ngofua ki he Fakamaauʻanga, ʻi ha taimi pe, ke ne tokanga ʻo fakaʻehiʻehi ai mei he hono fakaha ʻe he Fakamaauʻanga pe ko ha taha ha fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui fekauʻaki mo ha taha, kau ki ai, kapau ʻe fiemaʻu –

(a) toʻo ʻa e fakamatala fakahaaʻi ʻo ha taha pe ʻa ia ko hono fakamatala taautahakihemoʻuiʻokuʻuhingakiaiʻihangaahitohingauepe naunau;

(b) maʻu ha ngaahi fakamatala ʻo ʻikai ha fanongonongo;

(c) fakahoko ʻa e ngaahi hopo pe konga ʻo e ngaahi hopo ʻo fakapulipuli; pe

(d) silaʻi ʻa e kotoa pe konga ʻo e ngaahi faile fakamaauʻanga.

#### 90 Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni

(1) ʻE ngofua ke faʻu ʻe he Minisita ʻi he loto ki ai ʻa e Kapineti, ha Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni ke –

(a) tuʻutuʻuni pe fakamahino pau ha meʻa ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he Lao ni ko e meʻa kuo tuʻutuʻuni ‘e he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, fakamahino pau, fakamatalaʻi, kuo tuʻutuʻuni, pe fakamafaiʻi ʻi he Lao ni;

(b) fakaʻatā ʻa e niʻihi pe ngaahi fakakalakalasi ʻa e kakai mei he niʻihi ʻoku fakamatalaʻi ʻi he palakalafi (d) ʻa e ʻuhingaʻi lea ‘o e “taha ngaue tokangaekina ʻo e moʻui” ʻi he kupu 3(1);

(c) fakamahino pau ʻa e niʻihi pe ngaahi fakakalakalasi ʻo e kakai kuo pau pe kuo pau ʻe ʻikai fakakau ʻi he ʻuhingaʻi lea “kau tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui” ʻi he kupu siʻi 3(1);

(d) fakamahino pau ʻa e faʻahinga fakamatala ʻe niʻihi kuo pau pe kuo pau ʻe ʻikai fakakau ʻi he ʻuhingaʻi lea “fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui” ʻi he kupu siʻi 3(1);

(e) fakaʻuhingaʻi, koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e Lao ni mo hono Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni, ha lea pe kupuʻi lea ʻoku ngaueʻaki ʻi he Lao ni ʻa ia kuo teʻeki ai ke fakaʻuhingaʻi mahino ʻi he Lao ni;

(f) fakamahino‘a efaʻahinga ‘o ha niʻihi pe niʻihi ‘okutuʻutuʻuni‘i hekupu 3(1)(h) ‘o e ‘uhingaʻi lea “tauhi fakamatala ki he mo‘ui”;

(g) fakamahino pau ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni fekauʻaki mo e ngaahi founga ngaue fakaefakamatala koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e kupu 9(1);

(h) fakamahino pau ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni, pe ko ha founga ngaue ki hono fokotuʻu ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e kupu 9(3);

(i) fakamahino pau ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni koeʻuhi ko e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e kupu 19(1)(c);

Kupu 90 Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Peesi 84 Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 to

(j) fakamahino pau ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻa ia kuo pau ke fakakau ʻi ha aleapau naʻe fakahoko ʻi he Lao ni pe ko hono Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni;

(k) fakamahino pau ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni, fakangatangata pe fakataputapui ʻo fekauʻaki mo e hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki pe fakaha ʻo ha faʻahinga kalasi ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻe ha taha ʻo tanaki atu ki he ngaahi fakangatangata pe fakataputapui kuo fokotuʻu ʻi he Lao ni;

(l) fakamahino pau ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻa ia kuo pau ke fakakakato ʻe ha fakahinohinonaʻefakahamahinoʻaiaʻokulavekiaiʻihekupu 41(1)(k), pe 54(1)(d) (i);

(m) fakamahino pau ‘a e ngaahi tukunga ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he kupu 43(1)(b);

(n) fakangofua ʻa e ngaahi fanongonongo, fakamatala pe ha toe ngaahi meʻa kehe, ʻa ia ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ʻi he Lao ni ke ʻoatu ʻi he tohi, ʻi he founga fakaʻilekitulonika pe ko ha founga kehe, ʻo fakatatau ki he ngaahi tuʻunga pe fakangatangata ʻa ia ʻoku fakamahino pau ʻi he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni kuo faʻu ʻi he Lao ni;

(o) tuʻutuʻunikeʻoatuʻehekautauhifakamatalakihemoʻuiʻaefakamatala ki he ʻOfisa Pule Ngaue mo fakamahino pau ʻa e faʻahinga fakamatala ke ʻoatu pea mo e taimi mo e founga ʻe ʻoatu ai;

(p) fekauʻaki mo e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻa ia ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni ke faipau ki ai ʻa e kau tauhi fakamatala ki he moʻui ʻi he taimi ʻoku fili mo ngaueʻaki ai ʻa e ngaahi founga fakaʻilekitulonika ke hū, tanaki, ngaueʻaki, liliu, fakaha, tauhi pe fakaʻauha ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui, kau ki ai ʻa e founga ki hono fokotuʻutuʻu, vakaiʻi mo hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ko ia;

(q) tuʻutuʻuni ʻo e ngaahi tautea fakangaue; pe

(r) ha meʻa kehe pe ʻoku fiemaʻu ke fakahoko lelei ʻa e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e Lao ni.

(2) ʻE ngofua ke faʻu ʻe he Minisita ʻi he loto ki ai ʻa e Kapineti ʻa e Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni ki hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaahi taumuʻa ʻo e Konga 6 –

(a) tuʻutuʻuniʻi kuo pau pe kuo pau e ʻikai kau ha faʻahinga komiti ʻe niʻihi ʻi hono fakaʻuhingaʻi ʻo e “komiti tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina” ʻi he kupu 67;

(b) tuʻutuʻuniʻi ʻa e tuʻunga ʻo e fakamatala ʻa ia ko e fakamatala ki he tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina;

(c) tuʻutuʻuniʻi ʻa e fakamatala ʻa ia ʻoku ʻikai ko e fakamatala ki he tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina; pe

(d) puleʻi ʻa e ngaahi founga ngaue ke muimui ki ai ʻe ha komiti tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina ʻa ia ʻoku felaveʻi mo e fakamatala ki he tuʻunga ʻo e ngaue tokangaekina.

(3) ʻE ngofua ke faʻu ʻe he Minisita ʻi he loto ki ai ʻa e Kapineti ha Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni ki hono fakahoko ʻo e ngaahi taumuʻa mo e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni ʻo e Lao ki hono Malu'i 'o e Fakamatala Taautaha ki he Mo'ui 2025 Kupu 90 to Lao Fika 31 ‘o e 2025 Peesi 85 Konga 7, pea ʻikai fakangatangata ʻa e fakalukufua ʻo ia kimuʻa, ʻe ngofua ke faʻu ʻa e Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni ke –

(a) tuʻutuʻuni‘i ʻa e ngaahi mafai, ngaahi ngafa mo e ngaahi fatongia ʻo e Potungaue;

(b) tuʻutuʻuni‘i ʻa e ngaahi tuʻutuʻuni fakalahi ʻa ia kuo pau ke faipau ki ai ʻa e Potungaue ʻi hono faʻu mo tauhi ʻo e NHIS;

(c) tuʻutuʻuni ʻa e ngaaahi kalasi ʻo e kau tauhi ʻo e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui pe ha kau tauhi kuo fakamahino pau ʻo e fakamatala taautaha kihemoʻuikeʻoatuʻaefakamatalataautahakihemoʻuikihe Potungaue ʻi he Konga 7 mo fakamahino pau ʻa e fakamatala taautaha ki he moʻui ʻoku tuʻutuʻuni kinautolu ke ʻoatu;

(d) fekauʻaki mo hono fakahoko ʻe he Potungaue ʻa e ngaahi ngaue ʻoku felaveʻi mo e NHIS ki ha taha; pe

(e) tuʻutuʻuniki ha meʻa peʻi he Konga 7ʻe ngofua pe kuopau ketuʻutuʻuni pe kuo tuʻutuʻuni ʻe he Ngaahi Tuʻutuʻuni.

Naʻe tali ‘e he Fale Alea ‘i he ‘aho 6 ni ‘o ‘Aokosi 2025.
